mēlē kāṭṭiya eṇkaḷil patiṉmūṉṟu paṇkaḷ tēvārat til kāṇum paṇkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.  tēvārattil kāṇappaṭāta āṟu paṇkaḷ tiruvāymoḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  tiruvāy moḻiyil kāṇappaṭāta eṭṭup paṇkaḷ tēvārattil uḷḷaṉa.  paṇṇukku nērāka uḷḷa irākap peyarkaḷ pala, muraṇit tōṉ ṟuvatai aṟintu avaikaḷ piṟkālattavarkaḷāl varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar vipulānanta aṭikaḷ tam yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. atuvē uṇmai.
paṇkaḷiṉ perukkam
muṟkālattil patiṉōrāyirattut toḷḷāyirattu toṇṇūṟṟoru (11991) āti icaikaḷ iruntaṉa.  narappaṭai yāl uraikkappaṭṭa patiṉōrāyirattut toḷḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya āti icaikaḷ eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāra uraiyil kūṟiyiruppataik koṇṭu paṇṭaiya tamiḻarkaḷ oru icaiyaip palavāṟu pakuttuṇarntu patiṉāyirak kaṇakkāṉa paṇkaḷaik kaṇṭatu avarkaḷatu nuṇmāṇ nuḻai pulattiṉ ciṟappaik kāṭṭukiṟatu.  itu paṇṭaiya tamiḻar icai yiṉ parappaiyum ciṟappaiyum oruvāṟu nām aṟi vataṟku ētuvāka irukkiṟatu.  11991 icaikaḷ ātikālattil tamiḻarkaḷiṭamiruntu piṟkālattil maṟaikkappaṭṭup peyar māṟip pōyuḷḷaṉa.  appāl tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ kālattiṟku muṉ irunta 103 paṇkaḷum iruntu viṭam teriyātu pōyiṉa.  iṉṟu tēvārattil uḷḷa paṇkaḷil aḻintavai pōka eñci niṟpaṉa 23 eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.  anta irupattu mūṉṟu paṇkaḷum iṉṟu vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu colla vaḻiyillai.  ceṉṉai tamiḻicaic caṅkam pallāṇṭu āyntu paṇkaḷaip paṟṟi oru muṭivukāṇa muyaṉṟu varukiṉṟatu.  ikkāla ōtuvār palarukkum aṟiviyal pāṅkāṉa payiṟci illāmaiyāl inta āyvu muṭivillātu pōyk koṇṭē irukkiṉṟatu.  12000 paṇkaḷaik kaṇṭa tamiḻakam iṉṟu 12 paṇkaḷaik kūṭap pātukākkat tavaṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟu collum nilaiyil irukkiṟatu.  āṉālum iṉṟaiya teṉṉintiya irākaṅkaḷ yāvum paḻaiya tamiḻp paṇkaḷaic cērntavai eṉpa til aiyamillai.  eṉṟālum intiyā cutantiram aṭaintapiṉ 1947ām āṇṭiṟkuppiṉ namatu nāṭṭil ciṟappākat tamiḻakattil kalaikaḷukku maṟumalarcci aḷikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa pērārvam eḻuntu varukiṟatu.  itu tamiḻakattil maṭṭumaṉṟi, kēraḷa nāṭṭilum tirāviṭarkaḷukku 12000 paṇkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟa eṇṇam paravi irukkiṟatu.  paḻaiya paṇkaḷai icaikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvam tuḷirttu varukiṟatu.
āntirāvilum kaṉṉaṭattilum kūṭa ippoḻutu inta eṇṇam tuḷirkka ārampittuḷḷatu.  tamiḻp paṇkaḷukku, ēṉ? tirāviṭap paṇkaḷukku nalla oḷitarum varuṅkālam uṇṭeṉṟu mēṉāṭṭu icai aṟiñarkaḷum kūṭa colla muṟpaṭṭu viṭṭaṉar.1
tēvārap paṇkaḷ ki. pi. 600 mutal 800 varai appar, campantar, cuntarar mutaliyōrkaḷāl pōṟṟip pēṇap peṟṟu vantaṉa.  tamiḻp paṇkaḷ vaiṇava āḻvārkaḷāl kaṇṇiṉ karumaṇiyeṉṉak karutappeṟṟu vaḷamuṟṟiruntaṉa.  nāta muṉiyāl tirāviṭa vēta cākaram eṉṟu ēṟṟam peṟṟatu.  tiruvāṅkūril uḷḷa cēra maṉṉarkaḷiṉ marapu vaḻiyil vanta makkaḷāl 14 mutal 
16-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai ātarittu vaḷarkkap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻakattil tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṭci kulaiyavē tamiḻp paṇ kaḷum ātarippāriṉṟi, pēṇuvāriṉṟi maṟaintaṉa.  tēvārap paṇkaḷ kūṭa pāṭuvāriṉṟi arukiṉa.  tirukkōyilkaḷil vaṭa moḻiyāḷarkaḷiṉ celvākkup peruki tēvāram otukkap peṟṟatu.  tirumuṟai kaṇṭa cōḻa maṉṉaṉ tiru nāraiyūr nampi yāṇṭār nampiyiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu tēvārat tiruppatikaṅ kaḷait tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭittu muṟaippaṭuttiṉāṉ.  avaṉ tirunīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar marapilutitta icait tuṟaiyil nalla payiṟci peṟṟa pāṭiṉi mūlam tēvārap patikaṅkaḷukkup paḻaiya paṇ muṟai kaḷai aṭiyoṟṟi icai vakuttuc ceytāṉ.  appaṇ ki. pi. 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai nilai peṟṟiruntatu.  ki. pi. 1210-1241 varaiyuḷḷa kālattil cāraṅkatēvar tamiḻakam pukuntu tēvārap paṇkaḷai aṟintu tamatu vaṭamoḻi caṅkītarattiṉākaram eṉṉum nūlil tēvārap paṇkaḷ cilavaṟṟaip pōṟṟi vaittuḷḷār.  cila tēvārap patikaṅkaḷai muṉṉōr vakutta vaḻiyil paṇ muṟaippaṭi pāṭi makiḻa caṅkītaratṉākaram tuṇaipurikiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.  tēvārap paṇkaḷiṉ icai marapu ki.pi. 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai tamiḻakattil maṅkātiruntu vantatu.  tañcai makārāṭṭirar āṭciyil, piṟanāṭṭu icaik kalappiṉālum piṟa moḻip pāṭal kaḷait tamiḻ icaik kalaiñarkaḷ pāṭum vaḻakkam mikutippaṭṭata ṉālum tamiḻp paṇ uruvaṅkaḷ citaintum maṟaintum pōy viṭṭaṉa.  paṇkaḷaip pāṭuvōrkaḷum arukiṉar.  ōtuvārkaḷ maṭaṅkaḷi lum kōyilkaḷilum iṉṉiṉṉa paṇ amaittup patikaṅkaḷai iṉṉa iṉṉa irākaṅkaḷil pāṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu oru varaiyaṟai ceyyat tavaṟiviṭṭaṉar.  ippoḻutu anta varaiyaṟaiyum kaiviṭappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
paṇkaḷaip pakaṟpaṇ, irāppaṇ, potuppaṇ eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakaiyākap pirittu avaikaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa kālaṅkaḷil pāṭutaṟkuriyaṉa veṉṟum, iṉṉiṉṉa paṇ amaitta patikaṅkaḷai ikkālattil vaḻaṅkum iṉṉiṉṉa irākattil pāṭavēṇṭum eṉṟu oru varaṉmuṟai ēṟpaṭuttiyatu paḻamaikkup poruntiya tillai eṉpar cilar.  immuṟaikaḷai maṟuttuc cila paṇkaḷai vēṟu paṇkaḷil pāṭum marapum ōtuvārkaḷiṭam ippoḻutu eḻuntuḷḷatu.  cila paṇkaḷait tukka rākam, makiḻcci rākam, pōr rākam, vacantakāla rākam, mālai rākam, yāma rākam, viṭiyaṟkālai rākam, ucci rākam, eṉṟu pirittuḷḷārkaḷ.  avaikaḷiṉ viḷakkam aṭiyil vaṟumāṟu: (ivai yāvum piṟkāla rākaṅkaḷukkuc ceyyap peṟṟavai).
1.	tukka rākam: (1) ākiri (2) kaṇṭāravam (3) nīlāmpari 
(4) piyākaṭai (5) puṉṉākavarāḷi (6) varāḷi.
2.	makiḻcci rākam: (1) kāmpōti (2) taṉṉiyāci (3) cāvēri.
3.	pōr rākam: (1) nāṭṭai.
4.	vacantakāla rākam: (1) kāmpōti (2) acāvēri (3) taṉṉiyāci.
5.	mālai rākam: (1) kalyāṇi (2) kāpi (3) kaṉṉaṭam 
(4) kāmpōti.
6.	yāma rākam: (1) ākiri.
7.	viṭiyaṟkāla rākam: (1) intōḷam (2) irāmakali 
(3) tēcāccari (4) nāṭṭai (5) pūpāḷam.
8.	ucci rākam: (1) cāraṅkam (2) tēcākkiri.
ivaikaḷil ākiri, intōḷam, irāmakali, cāraṅkam, pūpāḷam tavira ēṉaiya rākaṅkaḷ ekkālattiṟkum ēṟṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
ivaikaḷellām iṭaik kālattiluḷḷavarkaḷ kaṇṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ.  tamiḻaka icai aṟiñarkaḷ pakaṟpaṇ irāppaṇ pōṉṟa paṇkaḷaip pirippatē paḻaiya marapaṉṟu eṉa ataik kaṇṭittu eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  icai paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ piṟ kālattil palavāṟāka eḻuntuviṭṭaṉa.  paḻaiya aracarkaḷatu araṇ maṉaikaḷilum nilakkiḻārkaḷatu (camīṉtārkaḷatu) ātaravilum vāḻnta icaip pulavarkaḷ kuṟittu vaitta kuṟippē itaṟkuk kāraṇamākum.
iṇaiyaṟṟa icai
ulakilē tamiḻ makkaḷ oru taṉiyiṉam.  avarkaḷ moḻiyum atē pōṉṟu ciṟappiyalpu vāynta taṉi moḻi.  itu piṟa moḻi yiṉ tuṇaiyiṉṟi taṉittiyaṅka valla uyar taṉic cemmoḻi.  inta uṇmaiyai oppi eḻutiṉār, tamiḻukku oppilakkaṇam kaṇṭa āṅkilap pēraṟiñar, kiṟittavap periya camayakuru, tiru. kālṭuvel avarkaḷ.  tamiḻ moḻi piṟa moḻikaḷukkillāta taṉicciṟappuṭaṉ iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻaik koṇṭa mutumoḻi.  ulakil uḷḷa toṉmaiyāṉa moḻikaḷ pala aḻintupaṭa mikap paḻamaiyāṉa tamiḻ aḻiyātu iḷamaiyōṭiruppatē atu uyiruḷḷa moḻi - teyva moḻi - kaṉṉittamiḻ moḻi eṉpataṟku ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.
niṟka, tamiḻ icai, civaperumāṉum, tirumālum, murukaṉum uvanta icai;  muṉivarkaḷum, aṟavōrkaḷum, aṟivarkaḷum pōṟṟi vaḷartta icai;  makkaḷ kaṇṇeṉap pōṟṟi vaḷartta icai.  iv vicaiyai vaṭanāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōy cila muṟaikaḷaic cērttu intucuttāṉi icai eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ icaittu varukiṉṟaṉar.  mārkkam eṉṟa tēvakāṉam tamiḻakattilum, intucuttāṉi eṉṟa tēvakāṉam vaṭaintiyāvilum vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu.  intucuttāṉi icaiyaiviṭa tamiḻt tēciya icai paḻamaiyum ciṟappum, aḻakum viññāṉappāṅkāṉa amaippum vāyntatu eṉṟu mēṉāṭ ṭaṟiñarkaḷ palarum karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.1   tamiḻaka nipuṇarkaḷ itai ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
tāy irākam
āti kālattil cempālai eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭa ātip paṇ iṉṟu arikāmpōti eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  intap paṇṇē tāyp paṇ allatu tāy irākam eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.  it tāyp paṇṇai ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇa icaikaḷil vevvēṟu pētaṅkaḷ ceytu pala paṇkaḷai namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ uruvākkiṉar.  cempālaip paṇṇai ēḻu curaṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟaiyum kirakam māṟṟik koṇṭu pōka āṟu tāyp paṇkaḷ tōṉṟum.  intap paṇkaḷai arikāmpōtiyiliruntu naṭapairavi, cuttatōṭi, caṅkarāparaṇam, karakarappiriyā, anumattōṭi mēcakalyāṇi eṉa āṟu tāy kirakaṅkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu icai vallunarkaḷ kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar.  ituvē paḻaiya muṟai eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
inta āṟu tāy irākaṅkaḷiliruntu amcattoṉi aṭāṉā, ārapi, kāṉaṭā, kētāram, piyāku, pilakari, cutta cāvēri, karuṭat toṉi, tēvakāntāri, tarpār, piyākaṭai, nīlampari mutaliya 300kku mēṟpaṭṭa caṉṉiya rākaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa eṉpar.  nīlāmpari muṟ kālattil mēkarākat kuṟiñci eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.  paṇṭaiya cātāri piṟkālattil kāmavarttaṉi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.  tamiḻakattiṉ taṉip perum teyvamāṉa civaṉ vaṭa intiya nāṭṭaic cērnta ēmanātaṉ eṉṟum icaik kalaiñaṉ muṉ viṟakāḷāy pāṇapattiraṉ māṇavaṉākap pōntu icai mīṭṭi avaṉai ōṭacceytatait tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam kūṟum.  nīlāmpari iṉṟu tālāṭṭup pāṭṭākat tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu.  taṟkālattil tāy irākaṅkaḷ 72 eṉṟum caṉṉiya rākam 4624um 200um 72um irukkiṟa tākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi tañcai caracuvatimakāl nūl nilaiyattil 1000 tāy irākaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum ilaṭcaṇa cūttiramum teluṅkil uḷḷatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  teṉṉintiyā vil eḻutappaṭṭa icaikkalai vaṭamoḻiyilum teluṅkilum iruppiṉum avaiyāvum tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻakkil uḷḷavaiyē eṉpatu uṇmaiyākum.
irākaṅkaḷiṉ pirivu
paṇ eṉpataip piṟkālattār irākam eṉṟaṉar.  inta irākaṅkaḷ cumār 1000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē iṇai icaip paṇ, kiḷai icaippaṇ, naṭpu icaippaṇ, pakai icaippaṇ eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  atai aṭiyārkku nallār pōṉṟa uraiyāciriyarkaḷum oppuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
cilappatikāram,
iṇaikiḷai pakainaṭ peṉṟin nāṉki
ṉicaipuṇar kuṟinilai yeyta nōkki	(cilap. vēṉiṟ 2-m 2)
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
paṇṇiṉ cuvaikaḷ pala.  avaṟṟait tēvārat tiruppatikap pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭum uṇaralām.  piṟkāla irākaṅkaḷil iṉṉiṉṉa irākam iṉṉiṉṉa cuvaikku uriyatu eṉṟu oṉpatu cuvaikkum oṉpatu paṇkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avai aṭiyil varumāṟu: paṇ eṉpatu paṇṇappaṭuvatu.  irākam eṉpatu virumpappaṭuvatu.  itai muṉṉarum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟōm.  
1.	uvakaiccuvaikku	piṟkālattil 	pūpāḷam
2.	nakaiccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	vacantā
3.	karuṇaiccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	palamañcari
4.	vīraccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	nāṭṭai
5.	accaccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	māḷavi
6.	avalaccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	srī rākam
7.	vekuḷiccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	pairavi
8.	viyappuccuvaikku	piṟkālattil	paṅkāḷam
naṭunilaimaic cuvaikku ellāp paṇkaḷum ukantavai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  cilar ovvoru cuvaikkum pala paṇkaḷ uṇ ṭeṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ.  ivaiyum piṟkāla vaḻakkē.
āṇ peṇ irākam
irākaṅkaḷil āṇ irākaṅkaḷ eṉṟum peṇ irākaṅkaḷ eṉṟum pirittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  avai aṭiyil varumāṟu:  ivaiyāvum mikap piṟkālattil eḻunta vaḻakku eṉappaṭum.

	cāraṅkam
vaṭaintiyāvil irākaṅkaḷai irākam (āṇ) irākiṉi (peṇ) eṉṟu pirittu avaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ.  ivvāṟu pirippataṟku vitikaḷum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  iḵtaṉṟi ovvoru irākaṅkaḷukkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  ākavē intiya icai muṟaikaḷiltāṉ ivvakai vēṟupāṭukaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa eṉpatai maṟantuviṭak kūṭātu.

7. yāḻiṉ tōṟṟamum ēṟṟamum
tamiḻar kaṇṭa cīriya narampuk karuviyākiya yāḻ tamiḻakattil toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil tōṉṟiyatu.  ivviṉiya icaik karuviyait tamiḻarkaḷ teyva nalyāḻ eṉṟu ēṟṟup pōṟṟi vantaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ ellām mika viruppuṭaṉ yāḻai mīṭṭi vantaṉa eṉṟu camaya nūṟkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
villil nāṇēṟṟi ampai eyyuṅkāl atil eḻum iṉṉōcaiyaik kaṇṭu vilyāḻai mutalil uruvākkiṉar.  vil yāḻ, kuṟiñci nilattil kuṟa makkaḷiṭai mutalil kāṇap peṟṟatāl itu kuṟiñci yāḻāka, mutal yāḻāka, tāy yāḻāka malarntatu.  itu vaḷarcci aṭaintu nāṉilaṅkaḷilum-ēṉ-aintu nilaṅkaḷilum paraviyatu.  appāl makkaḷiṉ aṟiviṉṟiṟattil ovvoru nilattilum ovvoru vitamāṉa yāḻākap putturuvam peṟṟup puṉita yāḻāka, amuta yāḻāka, teyva yāḻāka maṟumalarcci peṟṟatu.  eṉavē yāḻkaḷukkellām tāyāka vilyāḻ eṇṇappeṟṟatu.  itaṉ oppaṟṟa vaḷarcciyil pēriyāḻum, piṟayāḻum eḻuntaṉa.
inta yāḻ vāyiṉāl pāṭikkoṇṭē kaiyiṉāl mīṭṭuvataṟkēṟṟa karuviyāyt tikaḻntatu.  paṇ, paṟṟu (curuti), pāṇi eṉṟa mūṉṟaiyum oruṅkē icaikkavalla ēṟṟam peṟṟa icaik karuviyāy iru viḷaṅkiyatu.  ikkaruvi, paṉṉiru kēḷvippālai yum ellāt tāṉanilaikaḷaiyum (tāyikaḷaiyum) oruṅkē amaiyappeṟṟa oru vakai icaikkaruviyākum.  ovvoru kēḷvikkum uriya pālaikaḷiṉ akanilaiyaiyum puṟanilaiyaiyum oliyāka icaippataṟku oppaṟṟa icaik karuviyāyt tikaḻntu irukkakkūṭum.1  ikkaruviyē 21 narampukaḷai uṭaiyatāy mutal mutal tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟatākum.
vaṭṭapālaiyiṟ piṟakkum nālvakai yāḻkaḷaiyum avai ovvoṉṟuḷḷum evvēḻu pālai piṟakkum eṉpataip paṟṟiyum avaikaḷiṉ alaku muṟaikaḷaip paṟṟiyum itaṉ muṉ kaṇṭōm.  avaikaḷē nāṟperum paṇ eṉpatu teḷivākat terikiṟatu.  ipperum paṇkaḷ ovvoṉṟiliruntum nālu nālu cātikaḷ piṟakkum eṉṟu colvatiliruntu,
nāṟperum paṇṇum cāti nāṉkum
pāṟpaṭu tiṟaṉum paṇṇeṉap paṭumē.
eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ karuttut teḷivākiṟatu.  avai ovvoṉ ṟiṟkum akanilai, puṟanilai, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṟa nālu peyarkaḷ koṭuttu avai ovvoṉṟum kuralāka icaikkum icai iṉṉateṉṟum ataṉpiṉ varum.
mēlum, iṉṟu intiyāvil kāṇak kiṭakkum 500-kku mēṟpaṭṭa icaik karuvikaḷil yāḻil maṭṭumē uyarnta iyakku (tāyi) kaḷ varai, curuti cuttamāka icaikka iyalum.  eṉavē namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ yāḻ narampiṉiṉṟu oṉṟu, mūṉṟu, aintu - iṟutiyāy ēḻu iṉṉōcaiyaik kaṇṭaṉar.  ataip pālum tēṉum karumpum kaṉiyum eṉṟu cuvaittaṉar.  intat teyva vācciyatti ṉiṉṟu eḻum iṉṉoli, vilaṅkukaḷaiyum paṟavaikaḷaiyum ūrvaṉavaṟṟaiyum ēṉaiya uyiriṉaṅkaḷaiyum kavaravallatāyiṟṟu.  ettakaiya koṭiya arakkarkaḷiṉ kōpattaiyum taṇikkat takkatāy perun teyvaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉattaiyum taṇivikkavallatāy; avaikaḷai makiḻvikkavallatāyiṟṟu.  irāvaṇaṉ taṉ yāḻai mīṭṭiyē iṟaivaṉiṉ kōpattait taṇivittu, avaratu aruḷai aṭaintu vēṇṭiya varaṅkaḷaip peṟṟuḷḷāṉ eṉpatu namatu paḻaṅkatai.  pukaḻ mika vāynta piṟkālap pulavaṉ pukaḻēntiyum,
neṟṟit taṉikkaṇ neruppaik kuḷirvikkum
koṟṟat taṉiyāḻ.
eṉṟu naḷaveṇpāvil veku aḻakāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  icaikaḷ iṉṉiṉṉaveṉṟum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cātip paṇkaḷ akanilai, puṟanilai, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉa nālām.  ivai nālum marutam, kuṟiñci, neytal, pālai eṉṉum nālvita yāḻ vakaiyil patiṉāṟu vakaiyākat tuḷirkkum.  avai akanilai marutam, puṟanilai marutam, arukiyal marutam, perukiyal marutam, akanilaik kuṟiñci, puṟanilaik kuṟiñci eṉṟāṟpōla antanta yāḻiṉ peyarkaḷai aṭuttuvarum.  aka nilai - ovvoru yāḻiṉ toṭakka icaiyil varum paṇṇamaiti akanilaiyām.  puṟanilai nālvakai yāḻiṉ toṭakka icaikku nālāvatāṉa naṭpu narampil toṭaṅkum paṇṇām (ca. mavaip pōl) eṉṟu karuṇāmirtacākarattil mu. āpirakām paṇṭitar (mutaṟ puttakam - 1917) kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  kalittokaiyil,
kāḻvarai nillāk karuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal
yāḻ varait taṅki yāṅku
eṉṟu kūṟum ceyyuḷ mūlam yāṉaikaḷum yāḻiṉ icaiyaik kēṭṭu matam aṭaṅkip pacup pōṉṟu amaiti peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟu aṟiyalām.  atōṭu yāḻai mīṭṭuvarkaḷ poṟumaiyum amaitiyum uṭaiyōrāy naṉmoḻi pēcupavarkaḷāy meṉ moḻi mēvuvarkaḷāy viḷaṅkuvārkaḷ.  itaṉaip pattup pāṭṭil, tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyil,
cevinērpu vaitta ceyvuṟu tivviṉ
nalliyāḻ naviṉṟa nayaṉuṭai neñciṉ
meṉmoḻi mēlavar iṉṉaram puḷara
eṉṟu viḷakkik kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  civaperumāṉ icai vaṭiviṉaṉ.  makkaḷukku icaip payaṉai aḷippavaṉ.  icaikku urukupavaṉ.  civa perumāṉ taṉ cevikaḷil kuṇṭalamāka acuvatarar, kampaḷar ākiya iru yāḻ vallōrkaḷai aṇintu koṇṭu eppoḻu tum avarkaḷiṉ yāḻ icaiyaic cevimaṭuttuk koṇṭu iṭai yaṟātu iṉpan tuyttuvarukiṟār eṉṟu purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  civaṉaṭiyārākiya pāṇapattiraṉukkāka viṟaku viṟpavarāy civaperumāṉ vēṭam pūṇṭu yāḻ mīṭṭuvatil vallōrāy ēma nātaṉōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭṭu yāḻ mīṭṭi avaṉai veṉṟu vaṭa nāṭṭiṟku viraṭṭiyaṭittataiyum aṟiyum poḻutu yāḻ iṇai yaṟṟa icaikkaruvi eṉpataiyum iṟaivaṉ yāḻiṉ perumaiyaik kāttataiyum naṉkaṟikiṟōm.  cilappatikāra uraiyilē,
teyvañ cāṉṟa tīñcuvai nalyāḻ					(cilampu 8. vēṉiṟ. vari. 23. urai)
eṉavum,
yāḻ mātaṅki eṉum teyvam iruttaṟkiṭa meṉṟu
nūlkaḷ kūṟumātaliṉ	(cilap. 7: kāṉal. vari. urai)
eṉṟu kūṟum cilappatikāram uraiyāciriyar viḷakkattāṉum yāḻ teyvam uṟaiyum tīñcuvai tarum oru aṟputamāṉa ariya icaik karuvi eṉpataiyum nūṟkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭa vitippaṭi marattālum, tōlālum, narampālum amaikkappaṭṭa nalyāḻ mātaṅki eṉṉum icait teyvam icaintuṟaiyum perum pēṟṟaip peṟukiṟatu eṉpataiyum aṟikiṉṟōm.  tam kaiyil yāḻum vaittār eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar civaperumāṉaic cuṭṭik kūṟukiṟār.
karṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum icaip perum nūlil aṟiñar tiru. mu. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ tūla carīrattiṉ amaippaiyum pirāṇaṇiṉ nilaiyaiyum ataṉ iyakkattaiyum naṉṟāy aṟinta teyva paktarkaḷ evvitattilum attūla uṭampaiyē ottirukkum yāḻ eṉṉum ciṟanta vāttiyattaic ceytu taṅkaḷ teyva tōttiraṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiya kāṉattiṟku utaviyāka upayōkittārkaḷ.  vāttiyattiṉ amaippum, ataṉ iṉṉicaikaḷum muṟṟilum carīrattiṉ cila irakaciyaṅkaḷai veḷip paṭaiyākak koṇṭaṉa veṉṟum, ellā vāttiyaṅkaḷilum iṉimai mikkateṉṟum kāṉattil kuṟil, neṭil, oṟṟu, kuṟukkam, aḷapeṭai mutaliyavaṟṟait teḷivāyc collak kūṭiyatu yāḻ oṉṟutāṉ eṉṟum, yāḻiṉaik koṇṭu iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum, yāḻicai illāta pirārttaṉai payaṉaṟṟatu.1  eṉṟum viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār.  uṟṟu nōkki uṇartaṟkuriyaṉa avaratu karuttukkaḷ.
mēlum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum piṟa tamiḻp pērilakkiyaṅ kaḷilum yāḻiṉ perumai naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  tamiḻp peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa cilappatikārattilē,
kuḻalum yāḻum amiḻtuṅ kuḻaittaniṉ
maḻalaik kiḻavikku varuntiṉa vākiyum
eṉṟum,
alaiyiṭaip piṟavā vamiḻtē yeṉkō?
yāḻiṭaip piṟavā icaiyē yeṉkō?

āṭaṟ kamainta vācāṉ ṟaṉṉoṭum
yāḻum kuḻaluñ cīru miṭaṟun

eṉṟum kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  peruṅkataiyil,
āynta nalyāḻt tīñcuvai yuṇarnta

pāṇaṉ cūṭāṉ pāṭiṉi yaṇiyāḷ
eṉṟum,
vāṅkumarup piyāḻoṭu palliyaṅ kaṟaṅka
eṉṟum,
vāṅkiru maruppiṟ ṟīntoṭaic cīṟiyāḻ
pāṇaṉ kaiyatu tōlē
eṉṟum viḷakkik kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.  maṇimēkalaiyil
kūttum pāṭṭum tūkkun tuṇivum
paṇyāḻk karaṇamum pāṭaip pāṭalum
taṇṇumaik karuviyum tāḻtīṅ kuḻalum
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  porunarāṟṟuppaṭaiyil
paṇṇamai cīṟiyāḻ
eṉṟum, malaipaṭukaṭāttil
nalliyāḻp paṇṇup peyarttaṉṉa
eṉṟum, ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil
iṉkuraṟ cīṟiyā ḻiṭavayiṟ ṟaḻīi
eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tirukkuṟaḷil yāḻiṉ cuvaiyai
kuḻal iṉitu yāḻ iṉitu eṉpa
eṉṟum viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  olkāp perum pukaḻ paṭaitta tolkāppiyam tōṉṟuvataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉarē yāḻ arumpiviṭṭatu.  tolkāppiyattilē aiyamaṟa yāḻ eṉṉum icaikkaruvi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  paṇṇatti muṟaikaḷ eṉpaṉa yāḻiṉ vaḻi yeḻātu vēṟu etaṉ vaḻi eḻum?  tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattil
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟai
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokaii
avvakai piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa
eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  yāḻum, yāḻil eḻum icaiyum tol kāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpatē aṟiñar karuttu.  mēlum tolkāppiyar kālattil yāḻai mīṭṭum pāṇar kaḷum, āṭum kūttarkaḷum, pāṭum porunarkaḷum, viṟaliyarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.  tolkāppiyattil,
tāvil nallicai karutiya kiṭantōrkku
cūtar ēntiya tuyileṭai nilaiyum
kūttarum pāṇarum porunarum viṟaliyum					(tol. poruḷ. 1034)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatiṉiṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālattil nāṭakam, nāṭṭiyam, kūttu, icai ākiyavaikaḷum icaik karuviyākiya muracu, yāḻ, kuḻal pōṉṟavaikaḷai iyakkupavarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉṟu aiyamaṟat terikiṉṟataṉṟō!
tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil pāṇaṉ, paṟaiyaṉ, tuṭiyaṉ mutaliya iṉattavarkaḷ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉar.  pāṇar eṉpataṟkup paṇ pāṭuvōr eṉpatu poruḷākum.  pāṇaril icaippāṇar, yāḻppāṇar, maṇṭaippāṇar eṉṟa pala piriviṉar iruntaṉarām.  ivaruḷ - yāḻppāṇaril ciṟu pāṇar, perum pāṇar eṉṟu iru piriviṉar uṇṭu.  cīṟiyāḻai yum, pēriyāḻaiyum uṭaimaiyāl ippeyar eḻuntatu.  poruna rāṟṟuppaṭai; pāṭiṉiyiṉ kaiyiluḷḷatu pālai yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.  pāṇarkaḷ yāḻ vācippatil vallavarkaḷ eṉṟum itaṉāl ivarkaḷukku yāḻppāṇar eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu eṉṟum kūṟuvatiliruntu tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē yāḻum atai mīṭṭum pāṇarkaḷum pāṭiṉikaḷum tōṉṟi viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu naṉkaṟiyalām.  tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyum ataṉālē viḷaṅkum.
tamiḻarkaḷ tolkāppiyam tōṉṟuvataṟku 4 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē yāḻai uruvākki viṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattil vēṭṭuva vāḻkkai naṭattiyapoḻutē vil yāḻai uruvākki atai icaipaṭa mīṭṭi vantamaiyāl iṉṟaikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arappā nākarikattil vil yāḻ pōṉṟa yāḻ kāṇappaṭuvatai aṟiñarkaḷ aṉaivarum ārāyntu itu tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻ eṉṟu pōṟṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  arappā nākarikattiṟ kaṇṭa yāḻkaḷaip pōṉṟa icaikkaruvikaḷ pala cumēriyā nākarikattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṅku akaḻntu ārāynta pēraṟiñarkaḷ ci. iliyōṉārṭ ulli, ci. cē. kēṭ, vi. kōrṭaṉ cailṭ jiyārj kōṉṭaṉā pōṉṟavarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
caṅka nūṟkaḷil yāḻiṉ uruvattaip paṟṟi paṭam varaintāṟ pōṉṟu ariya ōviyam pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭai, yāḻ, paciya kaṇkaḷ vāynta karuṅkuraṅku pāmpiṉ talaiyaip piṭittapoḻutu pāmpu orukāl iṟukavum orukāl nekiḻavum ataṉ kaiyaic cuṟṟuvatu pōṉṟu yāḻiṉ tivaviṉai narampu ceṟiyac cuṟṟiyuḷḷatu.  yāḻiṉ ariya viḷimpukaḷ porunta āṇikaḷ iṇaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  atu oḻuṅkāṉa toḻil vakai yamainta pattarai (akaḷattiṉai) uṭaiyatu.  tuvarūṭṭiṉa ciṟanta tōl pōrvai kumiḻam kompu pōṉṟa niṟattil uḷḷatu.  tēṉvaṇṭu miḻaṟṟuvatu pōṉṟa iṉṉoli eḻuppum narampu kaḷaiyuṭaiyatu1 eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟi perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai pacumaiyāṉa ilai kaḷai utirtta tāḷiṉaiyuṭaiya pātiri malariṉ uḷḷiṭattai yotta tōl pōrvaiyai uṭaiyatu.  ippōrvai naṉṟāka iṟukkik kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum cuṉai vaṟṟi uḷ iruṇṭāṟ pōṉṟa vaṟuvāyiṉai uṭaiyatu.  mutaṟ piṟai tōṉṟi ēnti irun tāṟ pōṉṟu ēntiya kavaik kaṭaiyaiyum, nekiḻavum iṟukavum ēṟṟāṟpōṉṟa tivavu, nīṇṭa mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōlkaḷaiyuṭaiya maṅkaiyiṉ muṉkaiyai aṇi ceytu niṟkum aḻakiya toṭiyaip pōṉṟu tikaḻum yāḻil ilaṅkum aḻakiya maruppu, nīlamaṇi oḻuki ṉāṟ pōṉṟa karumai niṟamuṭaiyatu.2 eṉṟellām kūṟukiṟatu.
tamiḻ makkaḷāl peritum pōṟṟap peṟum caiva vaiṇavat tōttirap pāṭalkaḷ palavaṟṟilum yāḻ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  teyvīka icaik karuviyākiya yāḻiṉ icai civaperumāṉ, tirumāl mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷil cintaikkiṉiyatāy cevikku uṇavāy iruntatu.  māṇikkavācakar tiruvācakattiluḷḷa tiruppaḷḷi eḻucciyil,
iṉṉicai vīṇaiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  tiruñāṉacampantar tēvārattil,
kūṭarava montai kuḻali yāḻ muḻaviṉoṭu micai ceyya
eṉṟum,
paṟaiyuñ ciṟukuḻalum yāḻum pūtam payiṟṟavē
maṟaiyum palapāṭi mayāṉat tuṟai maintaṉār
eṉṟum,
iṉ kuralicai keḻumi yāḻ murala
eṉṟum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
civaperumāṉ, eṉ appaṉ eṉṟu ēttip pukaḻum peṟṟi vāynta camaya kuravarkaḷiṉ mutiyavarāyt tikaḻnta appar aṭikaḷ,
aṟaikalanta mutalmontai vīṇai yāḻum
eṉṟum
paṇṇōṭu yāḻvīṇai payiṉṟāy pōṟṟi
eṉṟum,
ēḻicai yāḻvīṇai muralak kaṇṭēṉ
eṉṟum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  caivacamaya kuravaril oruvarākiya cuntarar
maṇṇellām muḻava matirtara māṭamāḷikai kōpurattiṉ 										mēl
paṇṇiyāḻ muralum paḻaṉat tiruppaṉai yūr
eṉṟum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
vaiṇava camaya nūlākiya ātiyulāvil yāḻum pala icaik karuvikaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  kuṟippāka,
kuṭamuḻavaṅ kokkarai vīṇai kuḻal yāḻ
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.  vaiṇava aṭiyār kaḷil oruvarāṉa tiruppāṇāḻvār icaiyilum, yāḻ mīṭṭuva tilum ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉār eṉṟum icai tirumālukku ukantatu eṉṟum naṉkaṟikiṟōm.  yāḻiṉ icai vētattiyal eṉpatu taṉiyaṉ vākku.
yāḻiṉ tōṟṟam
yāḻ iṉṟaikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  atu tōṉṟiya tol ūḻik kālattilē atu vaḷarcci peṟṟatāy ellā ēṟṟamum peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu colvataṟkillai eṉpar.  atu ātiyil toṭakka nilaiyil oru narampaip peṟṟup paṭip paṭiyāy nāḷaṭaivil iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu narampukaḷaip peṟṟu oru niṟaivāṉa icaik karuviyāy miḷirntatu.  piṉṉartāṉ yāḻukku ēḻu narampukaḷ ēṟṟatu eṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭiṉar.  ātiyil arumpiyavil yāḻ oru narampuṭaṉ tōṉṟi nāḷaṭaivil mūṉṟāy piṉṉar aintu narampukaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟa ātiyāḻākap pariṇa mittatu.  ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ, tāram mutal narampu piṉṉar peṇ kuralaiyotta kural, appāl uḻai, iḷi, tuttam eṉṉum mūṉṟum kūṭṭi aintu narampukaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭaṉar eṉpar.  aintu narampilē arumpum paṇ taḷam cila iruntaṉa.  kural mutalāka eṭuttu viḷari kaikkiḷaiyai nīkkip pāṭa, matumātavi eṉṉum irākam ma, pa eṉṟum tāram mutalāka eṭuttu viḷari kaikkiḷaiyai viḷakkippāṭa irāmakiruti irākam, ma,pa eṉṟum iḷi mutal eṭuttu viḷari kaikkiḷai nīkkippāṭa tanyāci irākam eṉṟum nārata caṅkīta makarantam naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉavum kūṟuvar.
piṉṉāḷil oru curam, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu curaṅkaḷāki iṟutiyil ēḻākap pariṇamittatu eṉṟu ellā icai vallunarkaḷum ēṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟārkaḷ.  vaṭamoḻi icai marapilum muṉṉar aintu curaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya icai iruntuvantatu eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.  iṉṟum cīṉa nāṭṭil aintu curaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya icai vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.  ēḻu icaiyai kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉṟu aḻaippatu potuvaḻakku.  vaṭa nūṟpulavarkaḷ caṭcam, iṭapam (ricapam), kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, taivatam, nicātam eṉpar.  (1) uḻai eṉṉum icai narampiṉ ōcaiyai yāṉaiyiṉ oli eṉṟum (2) kaikkiḷai narampiṉ ōcaiyai kutiraiyiṉ oli eṉṟum (3) tuttam eṉṉum narampiṉ oliyai kiḷi kuyil ākiyavaikaḷiṉ ōcai eṉṟum (4) kural eṉṉum narampiṉ ōcaiyai vaṇṭu, kokku ākiya vaikaḷiṉ oli eṉṟum, (5) tāram eṉṉum narampiṉ ōcaiyai āṭṭiṉ oli eṉṟum (6) viḷari eṉṉum narampiṉ ōcaiyai pacuviṉ oli eṉṟum (7) iḷi eṉṉum narampiṉ ōcaiyai mayiliṉ oli eṉṟum icai aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiṉar.  itu oliyaik kuṟikkum aṭai yāḷap peyarkaḷākak karutappaṭalām eṉṟu cilar moḻikiṉṟaṉar.
yāḻiṉ vaṭivam
yāḻiṉ vaṭivam, atai amaikkum muṟai, curuti kūṭṭum muṟai, icaikkum vaḻi mutaliyavai paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl yāḻiṉ vaṭivattai ituvarai nammāl kāṇa muṭiyavillai.  namatu nāṭṭup putai poruḷ ārāyccikkārarkaḷ evarum yāḻai akaḻntu kaṇṭatākak kūṟavillai.  muṟkāla yāḻkaḷil oṉṟaiyāvatu namatu kōyil kaḷilō, piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilō pātukāttu vaikkappaṭṭiruppatāka nām kēḷvippaṭṭatum illai;  kaṇṭatumillai.  yāḻkaḷiṉ uruvattaik kāṭṭum ōviyamō, ciṟpamō nēṟṟu varai evarum kaṇṭatākak kūṟamuṭiyavillai.  yāḻ nūlaik kaṇṭa pērāciriyar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ tamiḻakattil yāḻ ōviya vaṭivattaiyō, ciṟpa vaṭivattaiyō kāṇavillai.  avar kaṇṭa amarāvati ciṟpaṅkaḷil tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa cīṟiyāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, vilyāḻ mutaliyavai etuvum illai.  yāḻ eṉṉum teyvīka ikkaruvi paṉṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu maṟaintu pōyviṭṭatu.  tamiḻarkaḷ ataṉaip piṟkāla makkaḷukkākap pātukāttu vaikka villai.  ataṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷai ōviyattilum ciṟpattilum tīṭṭi vaikkavillai eṉṟu ematu tantaiyār uḷam nontu varuntiya tuṇṭu.  yāḻ maṟaintatōṭaṉṟi ataṉ uruvamum tamiḻakatti ṉiṉṟu maṟaintoḻintu pōṉatu, cemmaiyum, taṇmaiyum, tiruvum, piṟa ciṟappum iḻantu viṭṭatu pōlākum.  ippēriḻappai icai aṟiñarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻ makkaḷ aṉaivarum kaṉavilum naṉavilum niṉaintu niṉaintu neñcam puṇṇāṉārkaḷ eṉṟārkaḷ.  itu 40 āṇṭukaḷākiyum eṉ niṉaiviṉiṉṟu akala villai.  yāḻiṉ ārāycciyai mutaṉmutalāka ārampitta uyartiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ amarāvati, cāñci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa cila ciṟpaṅkaḷil yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ cila iruppataik kaṇṭu kaḻipēruvakaiyuṟṟār.  āṉāl anta yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai aṭikaḷō piṟarō kūṟamuṭiyavillai.  aṉaittulakak kalaimētai uyartiru ānantak kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷ eḻutiya āṅkilak kaṭṭurai oṉṟil paḻamaiyāṉa yāḻ eṉṉum icaikkaruviyai amarāvatiyil uḷḷa kallōviyattil kaṇṭu kaḷittup paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa iccīriya icaikkaruvi tamiḻnāṭṭilē vaḻaṅkivantatu eṉpataiyum mikat toṉmaiyāṉa icai marapu oṉṟu tamiḻakattil nilavi maṟaintatu eṉpataiyum ulakil uḷḷa icai nipuṇarkaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.  ikkaṭṭurai aṟiñar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ uḷḷattaiyum kavarntatu.
ilaṇṭaṉ tolporuḷ kāṭciccālaiyilum ceṉṉai tolporuḷ kāṭciccālaiyilum amarāvati ciṟpaṅkaḷ cila uḷḷaṉa.  acciṟpaṅkaḷil cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  anta ariya yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭa aṟiñarkaḷ evarum avaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaik kuṟippiṭavillai.  āṉāl tiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ ilaṇṭaṉ tolporuḷ kāṭciccālai yil uḷḷa amarāvati ciṟpattil oru yāḻai cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  mēlum anta yāḻaip paṟṟit tamatu yāḻ nūlil oru viḷakkamum tantuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarp paḻam poruṭkāṭciccālaiyilē yuḷḷa amarāvatik kallōviyattilē kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu cakōṭa yāḻ.  itilē tivavu eṉṉum uṟuppu ilatu, narampukaḷai valittal, melittal ceyyum māṭakam eṉṉum iṟukkāṇi pattarilē amaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  pattar yāḻ vācikkum peṇṇiṉ uṭaliṉāl maṟaivuṇṭiruttaliṉ nām pattariṉaiyum māṭakattiṉaiyum kāṇamuṭiyavillai; kōṭum narampumē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.  kōṭṭiṉ uruvattai yāḻ vācikkum peṇṇiṉ uruvattōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkumiṭattup peṟappaṭu kiṟa kōṭṭiṉ nīḷattiṉaiyuṅ koṇṭu ikkaruviyiṉ vaṭivam aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟai oruvāṟu kaṇittaṟiyalām.  iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kallōviyattilē cakōṭayāḻ uruvattiṉai nām kāṇappeṟṟatu icai maṭantai namakku aḷitta periyatōr aruṭ koṭaiyē.  ceṉṉai mānakarp paḻam poruḷkāṭciccālaiyi luḷḷa yāḻēntiya ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ uruvattai VIM« paṭattil kāṇka.  pēriyāḻiṉaip poṟuttavaraiyil ataṉ uruvattiṉai namatu nāṭṭilē kāṇap peṟṟilamēṉum kaṭalkōḷiṉ piṉ, tamiḻar kaṭal kaṭantuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya piṟa nāṭukaḷilē ik karuviyiṉ uruvattiṉaik kāṇak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.1  aṭikaḷār kuṟippiṭṭa cakōṭayāḻ eṉṉum amarāvati ciṟpam avaratu yāḻ nūlil 141ām pakkattil iṇaikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  aṭikaḷār kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻ uruvam oṉṟu atē yāḻ nūlil 53ām pakkattil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  ivviru uruvaṅkaḷum oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭavillai.  aṭikaḷār kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻ vaḷainta kompaiyuṭaiyatāy paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷait taḻuviyatāy iruk kiṟatu.  amarāvatic ciṟpattil pāṭiṉiyākiya aḻakiya āraṇaṅku kaiyēnti niṟkum yāḻ nimirntu nīṇṭa kompukaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy irukkiṟatu.  mēlum avar tīṭṭiya amarāvatic ciṟpattil uḷḷa ēṉaiya mūṉṟu yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷai nōkkiṉālum ivar kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻukku oppuvamai uṭaiyaṉavāka illai.  avvāṟu iruntum ēṉ amarāvati yāḻaic cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭ ṭār eṉṟu eṉṉāl cintikka muṭiyavillai.  eṉavē namatu vācakarkaḷiṉ muṭipiṟku itai viṭṭuviṭa eṇṇi amarāvatiyil uḷḷa kōli eṉṉum iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa ciṟpa uruvaṅ kaḷai niḻal uruvap paṭam piṭittu innūlil veḷiyiṭṭirup patōṭu aṭikaḷār kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻ uruvattaiyum veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷōm.  (paṭam VII pārkka)
yāḻ uruvam, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ tavappēṟākac camīpattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  tirucci māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa putuk kōṭṭaikku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa cuvaril aḻakiya yāḻ uruvam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaik kēṭṭut tamiḻulakam kaḻipēruvakai aṭaiyum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm.  iṅkuḷḷa ciṟpattil muṉivarkaḷiṉ naṭuvil nāratar yāḻuṭaṉum tumpuruvar vīṇaiyuṭa ṉum kāṇappaṭukiṟār eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  peru māṉiṉ talaippakkattil cuvaril kāṇappaṭum ciṟpa uruvil muṉivar kūṭṭattil nāratar mūṉṟāvatākavum tumpuruvar nāṉkāva tākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟār.
tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar kaiyiliruntatu cakōṭayāḻ.  inta cakōṭayāḻtāṉ tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷaiyārāl pārāṭṭap peṟṟatu.  inta icaik karuviyiṉ uruvam namakku veku nāḷākat teriyātu.  ippoḻutu tiru erukkattampuliyūril uḷḷa tirukkōyilil tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇariṉ tiruuruvattil yāḻ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  atu cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
āṉāl tiruñāṉa campantariṉ varalāṟṟil tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇarum avartam tuṇaiviyār mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyārum kaḻumalattīcaṉ kavuṇiyap piḷḷaiyārkku mūvāṇṭil aruḷ ñāṉam kuḻaittūṭṭiya aṟputa nikaḻcciyaik kēṭṭu campantaraik kaṇṭu pōṟṟa cīrkāḻikkup pōntaṉar.  atai aṟinta campantar avarkaḷai varavēṟṟu upacarittār.  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar campantarait toḻutu tantiriyāḻiṉai vīkki icai yārāyntu iṟaivaṟkukanta pāṇiyiṉai ēḻicai valla mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyā ruṭaṉ pāṭi yāvarum viyakka yāḻiliṭṭu vācittup pōṟṟiṉār eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.1
itaṉāl tantiriyāḻum cakōṭayāḻum oṉṟā?  vēṟā? eṉpatu nammāl tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyavillai.  patiṉāṉku narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu cakōṭa yāḻ.  ataṉ paḻaiya peyar cey muṟaik kēḷvi eṉpatākum.  itu cakōṣam eṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷa rāl aḻaikkappeṟṟuc cakōṭayāḻ eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkiyatu.  yāḻkkaruviyiṉ vaḷainta kōṭu nimirkkap peṟṟu cemmaiyāka māṟṟap peṟṟu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻāka maṟumalarcci peṟṟu ēḻu narampukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟaṉa.
kumpakōṇattiṟku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa tārācuram eṉṉum kōyililum tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar uruvac ciṟpattil yāḻ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
yāḻum - vīṇaiyum
yāḻ tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa toṉmaiyāṉa icaikkaruvi - yāḻ tamiḻakattai viṭṭu iṉṟu maṟaintuviṭṭatu.  muṟkālattil tamiḻakattil eṇṇaṟṟa yāḻ vakaikaḷ iruntaṉa.  iṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka oru yāḻ uruvaṅ kūṭak kiṭaikkavillai.  yāḻaip paṟṟi aṟiya pala mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ tuṭituṭikkiṉṟārkaḷ.  tamiḻ nāṭṭil attakaiya tuṭituṭippō akkaṟaiyō iṉṉum eḻa villai.  irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ yāḻaip paṟṟi ārāya muṟpaṭṭār.  mikunta ciramappaṭṭu yāḻ nūlai eḻutit tamiḻ ulakiṟku aḷittār.  annūlil cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷaiyum namakku  varaintu tantār.  marattāl cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷaiyum ceytu tantār.  avar kālattiṟkup piṉ anta āyvu toṭarntu naṭaipeṟavillai.  avar namakkuc ceytu tanta yāḻiṉ uruvaṅkaḷaik kūṭap pātukāttu vaikkat tavaṟi viṭṭōm.
tamiḻaṉukku ātiyil aṟamuraitta aṟak kaṭavuḷ iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉum muttamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ - mukkaṇṇaṉ tamatu tirukkarattāl yāḻai mīṭṭiṉār.  itaṉ iṉiya oliyaic cuvaittu iṉpuṟṟār.  tamiḻ makkaḷukku yāḻai mīṭṭuvataiyum avar kaṟpittuk koṭuttār eṉṟu namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ kūṟiṉar.  patiṉaintu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmai yāṉa aṟaneṟikaḷukkup puttuyirum putuvāḻvum ūṭṭiya camaya aṟivarkaḷ nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟaivaṉai yāḻmoḻi ammaiyōṭallavā kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatai nām ciṟitum cintikkavillai.  pala ūrkaḷil uḷḷa tēviyārkku, yāḻiṉumeṉ moḻiyammai, yāḻiṉum meṉmoḻi nāyaki, yāḻaip patitta moḻiyammai eṉṉum peyarkaḷ cūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  atōṭu mukkaṇṇuṭaiya īcaṉār karaṅkaḷil yāḻēnti iṉṉicai mīṭṭi ulakai iṉpuṟac ceytār.  atai appar aṭikaḷ
paṇṇoṭu yāḻvīṇai payiṉṟāy pōṟṟi
eṉṟum,
ēḻicai yāḻvīṇai muralak kaṇṭēṉ
eṉṟum,
aṟai kalanta kuḻal montai vīṇaiyāḻum
eṉṟum pōṟṟiya puṉitamāṉa teyvat tiruyāḻait tamiḻarkaḷ iḻantuviṭṭaṉar; maṟantuviṭṭaṉar.  iṉṟum avarkaḷ antat tūya teyvīka icaikkaruviyākiya yāḻiṉ uruvattaik kāṇa uḷḷam tuṭittu eḻavillai.  patiṉmūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar, maṟaintutoḻintupōṉa yāḻai āyntu paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭiya tōṭamaiyātu atai ilakkaṇa vitippaṭi marattāṟ ceytu tamiḻaṉṉaiyiṉ poṟpāta kamalaṅkaḷil aṟiñar vipulā ṉantar kāṇikkaiyākap paṭaittār.  tamiḻaṉ anta ariya icaik karuviyaikkūṭa iḻantuviṭṭāṉ.  tamiḻakattiṟku itu oru perum iḻukkākum.  namatu nāṭṭup paṟṟukku, namatu teyva paktikku, namatu paṇpiṟku, namatu icaip perumaikku, namatu pukaḻukku itu pēriḻappākum.
inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil, tamiḻaṉiṉ teyvīkac cottāṉa, amutamāṉa yāḻkkaruviyai mīṇṭum tamiḻarkaḷ peṟacceyta iru perum īḻat tamiḻarkaḷāṉa uyartiru ānantak kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷukkum vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷukkum eṉṟum nām naṉṟip pārāṭṭit taṉatu vaṇakkattaic celuttak kaṭamaip paṭṭuḷḷōm.
patiṉaintu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē yāḻ tamiḻakattil celvākkup peṟṟirukkum poḻutē tamiḻarkaḷ yāḻiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyil putiya icaik karuviyaik kaṇṭaṉar.  atu yāḻ pōl pērum perumaiyum peṟṟatu.  tirukkōyilkaḷil yāḻaip pōṉṟu mīṭṭap peṟṟu makkaḷiṉ naṉmatippaip peṟṟu vantatu.  ataṉaiyē vīṇai eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar.
paṇṭait tamiḻakattil pāṇar kaiyilum pāṭiṉi kaiyilum iruntu pēriya paṇkaḷai olitta pāriya pēriyāḻ cīṟiya cīṟiyāḻ, uḷḷam kavarum vilyāḻ pōṉṟa icaik karuvikaḷ pala iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ kālattil icaip pulavar kaikaḷilum nāṭaka makaḷir kai kaḷilum iyaintu, cittirap paṭattil cērntu, ceḻuṅkōṭṭil malar puṉaintu, maṇamakaḷir eḻilnilai eṉṉak kaviṉ peṟak kāṭci nalkiṉa. āḷuṭaiya piḷḷaiyār avaṉiyil avataritta aruṭ kālattilē āṇṭavaṉ aḷitta aṟputap poṟpalakaiyiṉ mītēṟi, aṟavōr akaṅkuḷira aruṭkavi pāṭuvataṟkut tuṇai purintaṉa.  aruḷ vaṭivāy avaṉiyeṅkum pōṟṟa eḻunta araṉār karattil iṭam peṟṟatu.  yāḻmurinātar ēntiya teyvat tīntamiḻ yāḻeṉa ciṟappuṟṟatu.  koṅku vēḷirum, tiruttakka tēvarum ēṭeḻutiya kālattil eḻil mikka iḷavaracikaḷ paiṅkaraṅkaḷilē iṭam peṟṟu, avarkaḷiṉ aṉpiṟkuriya aḻakiya kātalarai avarukku aḷikkum arum perum kātaṟ karuviyāyt tikaḻntu, varalāṟṟup perumaiyum ilakkiyac ciṟappum peṟṟut tikaḻntu viḷaṅkiyatu.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa mutuperum tamiḻ nilattilēyuḷḷa mullaiyil āyarkaḷ kaṇṭa vilyāḻ eṉṉum iṉmoḻi payanta iṉiya kuḻavi tōṉṟi, maḻalai miḻaṟṟik kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarkaḷaiyum kuṟa makaḷirkaḷaiyum akamakiḻac ceytatu. appāl atu vaḷarntu eḻilum iḷamaiyum peṟṟuttāṉ pōṟṟiya mullai nilattil naṉmoḻi naviṉṟu mullai nila āyarkaḷaiyum āycciyarkaḷaiyum iṉpuṟac ceytatu.  piṉ, atu cīṟiyāḻ eṉṉum pētaip paruvattai eyti cīriḷamaic celvac ciṟumiyāy pāṇaṉ, pāṭiṉiyōṭu kūṭi nāṭeṅkum ceṉṟu talai makaṉum talaimakaḷum maṭṭumiṉṟi ēḻaikaḷum, pāḷaikaḷum, pāṭṭāḷikaḷum kūṭṭāḷikaḷum iṉpuṟa iṉmoḻi pēci vantatu.  piṉṉar pēriyāḻ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ petumpaip paruvam eyti māṭamāḷikai kūṭakōpuram ellām pukuntu kuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷum muṭiyuṭaiya vēntarkaḷum vaḷḷalkaḷum pulavarkaḷum kēṭṭu viyakkumpaṭi nayavurai pakarntatu, appāl maṅkaip paruvamuṟṟu āṭai aṇikaḷ pūṇṭu aḻaku oḷira nāṭaka araṅkukaḷil tōṟṟit taṉ āṟṟal aṉaittaiyum kāṭṭip perumaiyuṟṟatu.  piṟaku maṭantaip paruvam eyti kalaiñarkaḷum kaviñarkaḷum, vallōr kaḷum, nallōrkaḷum pōṟṟip pukaḻum cīrum ciṟappum eytiyatu.  tirunīlakaṇṭa teyvap perumpāṇarōṭu avaratu akamuṭai yāḷ mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyārōṭu teyvat tirukkōyilkaḷil valam vantu teyva icaiyai nalki aṭiyār uḷḷattai urukki āḷuṭaiya piḷḷaiyāriṉ akaṅkuḷirac ceytatu.  avaratu pārāṭṭaip peṟṟatu.  atu arivaip paruvam uṟṟatum araciḷaṅ kumarikaḷukku uyirp pāṅkiyāy avarkaḷukku ēṟṟa talaivarkaḷai avarkaḷiṭam cērkkum mantira caktiyāy miḷirntatu.  uyar neṟiyāḷar tam uyiriṉum iṉiya talaivaṉāy viḷaṅkum civaperumāṉ yāḻmuri nātar eṉṉum peyaraip peṟac ceytatum avaratu āṟṟalāy aruḷ caktiyāy viḷaṅkum tēviyai avar, yāḻaip paḻitta moḻiyammai eṉṟu tittikkat tēṉ oḻuka aḻaikkum perum pēṟṟaip peṟṟatu,  teyvat tiruvaruḷai yuṟṟatu, civaṉār tiruvā yāl aṭikkaṭi aḻaikkappeṟṟa aruṭ pēṟṟai aṭaintatu, ivvāṟu pērum perumaiyum cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟa yāḻ eṉṉum naṉmoḻi vāynta iṉmoḻi payakkum tīñcuvai celviyām yāḻ iṉṟu irunta iṭam teriyātu maṟaintu pōṉāḷ eṉṟu uḷḷam urukik kaṇṇīr malki namakku ellām aṟivuc cuṭar kāṭṭi avaḷ irunta tiru uruvam kāṭṭi mīṇṭum avaḷ tamiḻakat til koluvīṟṟirukkac ceyyumpaṭi tūṇṭiṉār, nam aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār.
mātaṅki uṟaiyum maṇiyoli yeḻuppum teyvat tiru vuṭaiya yāḻ mīṇṭum tamiḻ makkaḷ illaṅkaḷ aṉaittilum olikkac ceyya vēṇṭumeṉa vipulāṉantar kaṉavu kaṇṭār; mīṇṭum paḻantamiḻar kaṇṭa yāḻ eṉṉum teyva naṅkai tamiḻakat til aracuk kaṭṭilai aṭaiyac ceyya iṭaiviṭātu muyaṉṟār.  tamiḻaṉākap piṟanta ovvoruvar illattilum avarkaḷ tāyt tirunāṭṭil iruntālum cari allatu cēy nāṭākiya īḻa nāṭṭil iruntālum cari allatu ēla nāṭṭil vāḻntālum cari allatu malai nāṭṭil vaṇikam purintālum cari, ciṅkapurattil ceṅ kōlōcciṉālum cari, intōṉēciyāvil irantālum cari, parmā nāṭṭil pāṭupaṭṭālum cari, allatu pālittīvil pārli payiriṭṭu uḻaṉṟālum cari, allatu campā, pōrṉiyā, keṉiyā, morēciya nāṭukaḷil kūliyākavō, aracāṅka aluvalar kaḷākavō amaiccarākavō, akatiyākavō alaintālum cari avarkaḷatu cevikaḷil ovvoru nāḷum yāḻ oli kēṭkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻaintār vipulāṉantar.  avaratu kaṉavai avaratu ācaiyait tamiḻaṉṉaiyiṉ putalvarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟik koḷvōr aṉaivarum naṉavākka naṭaimuṟaiyil ceytu kāṭṭa muyala vēṇṭum.  atuvē nam kaṭamai eṉpataiyum uṇaral vēṇṭum.
olic ciṟappu
makkaḷukkuc cevikkiṉpattaiyum uḷḷattiṟku uvakaiyai yum ūṭṭattakkatāy amaiyappeṟṟa, ēṟṟattāḻvaṟṟa icai, oli aḷavōṭu kūṭa eḻumpōtu icaiyoli eṉap peṟṟatu.  appāl icaikkaruvikaḷil icaiyōrttup pāṭum paṇṇiṟkuriya pāṇi yiṉ naṭaiyum tāṉavuṟutiyum cantak kuḻippilakkaṇam vaḻuvātu miḻaṟṟuvatāl eḻum oliyaiyum icaiyoli eṉṟu ilakkaṇa varampu niṟuvappeṟṟatu.  ivvicaiyoli mūlam mutal makkaḷiṉ miṭaṟṟilē (kaṇṭattilē) tāṉa nilaiyil arumpiyatu.  appāl avarkaḷiṉ aṟiviṉṟiṟattāl iyaṟkaiyiṉ vaḻittāy cūḻal, yāḻ, mattaḷam, tuṭi, nātacuram pōṉṟa pala icaik karuvikaḷaip paṟṟi āyntu ataṟku ilakkaṇamum kaṇṭu vitippaṭi uruvākki avaikaḷiṉiṉṟu iṉṉolikaḷai eḻuppi iṉpuṟṟu vantaṉar.
pā, paṇ, pāṇi eṉṉum mūṉṟu ciṟanta uṟuppukkaḷai yuṭaiya icaippāṭṭiṉai vaḻuviṉṟi icaikkum ēṟṟamuṭaiya karuvikaḷ icaikkaruvikaḷ eṉṟaḻaikkappeṟṟaṉa.  ivaikaḷai iruvakaiyākap pirittup payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.  avai muṟaiyē miṭaṟṟuk karuvi kuyiluvak karuvi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
1.  miṭaṟṟuk karuvi
miṭaṟṟuk karuvi icai olikaḷaiyum pākkaḷaiyum oruṅkē nikaḻttum pāṉmai vāyntatu.  makkaḷatu kaṇṭam - atāvatu miṭaṟēyākum.  itaṉaik kural eṉṟum cārīram eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  miṭaṟu eṉpatu ākupeyarāṉ, miṭaṟṟup pāṭalukkāyiṟṟu eṉpar.
2.  kuyiluvak karuvi
icaip pākkaḷiṉ icaiyaiyum pāṇiyaiyum nikaḻtta vallatum maṉitaṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatumāṉa karuvikaḷ aṉaittum kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ ākum.  kuyilutal eṉṟāl olittal eṉṟu poruḷ tarum.
kuyiluvak karuvi, tōṟkaruvi, tuḷaikkaruvi, narampuk karuvi, kañcak karuvi eṉa nāṉku vakaippaṭum.  namatu nikaṇṭukaḷilum akarātikaḷilum inta nāṉku vakaik karuvikaḷaiyum icaik karuvikaḷāka kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
yāḻiṉ ēṟṟam
pāṭṭiṟku urukum paramacivaṉ, pāṇapattiraṉ poruṭṭu, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut talaimaip patiyākiya maturaiyilē tōṉṟi ṉār.  viṇṇiṉiṉṟu maṇṇiṟku iṟaṅkiṉār. avaratu teyvat tiru uruvai māṟṟi makkaḷ uruvan tāṅki, atilum viṟaku viṟpavaṉ pōla vēṭantāṅkiṉār.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu icaivāṇaṉ muṉ, vaṭa nāṭṭu icaivāṇaṉ niṟkavum añci iravōṭiravāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭait tuṟantu - itai ēṟiṭṭuppārkkavum añci vaṭa nāṭṭil pōyp patuṅkik koḷḷac ceytār.
tamiḻicaiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭu yāḻicaiyum kēṭṭa mayilum kuyilum, māṉum āṉum pariyum kariyum, puliyum eliyum, taṉvaya miḻantu oṉṟukūṭi niṉṟaṉa eṉṟu namatu purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tirunīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇarukkup poṟpalakai īntu yāḻiṉ perumaiyai viṇṇiṟku uyarttiṉār civaperumāṉ.  uḷḷam makiḻntu tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷaiyārukkup poṟṟāḷa mīntu tamiḻ icaiyiṉ ēṟṟattai ulakil nilaināṭṭiṉa teyvam civaṉēyām.
aruṭperuntakaiyām tirukkurukaip pirāṉ kaṉṉalum kaṉiracamum pōṉṟa kārmukil vaṇṇaṉai,
muṉ nalyāḻ payiṟnūl narampiṉ mutircuvaiyē
eṉṟu pukaḻntu, avaṉ yāḻiṉ narampukaḷāy mutircuvaiyāy tittikkum tīñcuvaiyāy iṉimaiyāy niṉṟāṉ eṉṟa uṇmaiyai eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tiṉaitiṉṉavanta yāṉai yāḻiṉ iṉṉōcaiyaik kēṭṭu taṉṉai maṟantu aṭutta aṭiyum vaikkātu uḷḷa muruki niṉṟatu.  itaṉaip pālaikkaliyil,
yāḻvarai nillāk karuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal
yāḻvarait taṅki yāṅkut tāḻpuniṉ
tolkaviṉ tolaita lañci yeṉ
colvarait tāṅkiṉar kātalōrē
eṉṟu talaivikkup poruḷ tēṭa eṇṇip piriya virukkum talaimakaṉiṉ nilaippaṟṟi tōḻi orutti kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.
icaik karuvikaḷ pala oruṅkē iyaintu olippatu paṇṭait tamiḻakattil āmantirikai eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭatu.  itaṉai āṅkilēyar ārcceṭṭirā (Orchestra) eṉṟu aḻaippar.  cīvaka cintāmaṇi eṉṉum cīriya kāppiyattiṟku urai kaṇṭa āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, kuḻal vaḻi yāḻeḻīit taṇṇumai piṉṉar muḻaviyampa lāmantirikai eṉṟu mēṟkōḷum kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
kuḻalvaḻi niṉṟa tiyāḻē yāḻvaḻit
taṇṇumai niṉṟatu takavē taṇṇumaip
piṉvaḻi niṉṟatu muḻavē muḻavoṭu
kūṭiniṉ ṟicaitta tāman tirikai
eṉṟa cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟuk kātaip pakutiyālum muṟkālat tamiḻakattil icaik karuvikaḷ pala oruṅku cērntu olitta kāṭciyai aṟikiṉṟōm.
malaimakaḷ makaṉām mayilēṟum māmakaṉiṉ malaiyil (tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil) orupuṟam iyaṟkai eḻilum maṟṟorupuṟam ceyaṟkaic cīrum mayaṅkuvatu pōl iyaintu, kaviñar uḷḷattaik kaḷippikkiṉṟatu.  paṇṇārnta pāṇariṉ paintamiḻp paḻam perum yāḻiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum paḻañcuvai pōṉṟa tīñcuvaiyiṉ iṉṉoli oru puṟam eḻum.  avviṉiya ēḻicai kēṭṭu eḻum vaṇṭukaḷiṉ rīṅkāram maṟṟoru puṟam olikkum.  kuḻaliṉ icai yōṭu tumpiyiṉ icaiyum muḻaviṉ oliyum aruvi nīriṉ oliyum viṟaliyar (kūttiyar) nuṭakkattoṭu pūṅkoṭikaḷiṉ nuṭakkamum pāṭuvatil valla pāṭiṉikaḷ pālaip paṇṇaip pāṭiṉārkaḷ.  atil kiḻamai, niṟai, kuṟaikaḷiṉ tōṟṟattōṭu āṭum mayiliṉ arinta kural oliyum māṟuṟṟaṉa pōṉṟu etirniṉṟaṉa.  itaṉaip paripāṭalil nallaḻuciyār eṉṉum pulavar perumakaṉ,
orutiṟam pāṇar yāḻiṉ tīṅkuraleḻa
orutiṟam yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ imiricaiyeḻa
orutiṟam kaṇṇār kuḻaliṉ karaipueḻa
orutiṟam paṇṇār tumpi paranticaiyūka
orutiṟam maṇṇār muḻavi ṉicaiyeḻa
orutiṟam aṇṇal neṭuvarai yaruvi nīrttumpa
orutiṟam āṭaṉal viṟaliyar olkupu nuṭaṅka
orutiṟam vāṭai yuḷarvayiṟ pūṅkoṭi nuṭaṅka
orutiṟam pāṭiṉi muralum pālaiyaṅ kuraliṉ
nīṭukiḷar kiḻamai niṟaikuṟai tōṉṟa
orutiṟam āṭucīr maññai yarikural tōṉṟa
māṟumā ṟuṟṟaṉapōṉ māṟetir kōṭaṉ
māṟaṭṭāṉ kuṉṟa muṭaittu
eṉṟu aḻakuṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  pāṭiṉiyiṉ miṭaṟṟup pāṭalaiyum pāṇaṉ icaikkum yāḻ oliyaiyum, kuḻalōṉ vācikkum vaṅkiyap pāṭalaiyum viṟaliyar āṭum āṭṭattaiyum puṉattilum poḻililum kuṉṟattilum maṉṟattilum kaṭaṟkarai yilum kāṉyāṟṟu maṇalilum tēvakōṭṭattilum aṟavōr paḷḷiyilum kēṭṭum kaṇṭum uḷam makiḻntu uḷḷam pūrittu iṉpak kaṭalāṭi vantaṉar paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ.  yāḻ oli avarkaḷ uyirai vaḷarttu vantatu.  ataṉ viḷakkamē ikkālattiya tamiḻar kaḷiṉ icaiyuṇarvām.
yāḻiṉ piṟappiṭam
civalōkam eṉap pōṟṟappeṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭi luḷḷa potiya malaiyil iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻum piṟanta teṉpar, tamiḻ aṟiñar.  aṅkuttāṉ paḻam perum tamiḻ ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟappeṟum olkāpperum pukaḻ tol kāppiyam tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  ippotiya malaiyil allatu atait toṭarntu teṟkē nīṇṭirunta malaiyucciyil tāṉ tamiḻaṉiṉ taṉip perum icaikkaruviyāṉa yāḻum kuḻalum piṟantaṉa.  tēṉiṉum iṉiya teyvīka icaikkaruviyāṉa yāḻaik kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.  akkaruviyē kuṟiñci yāḻ.  appāl malaiyaṭivārattil vāḻnta tolkuṭi makkaḷāṉa āyarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ kaṇṭa vaṅkiyattōṭu yāḻaiyum vaḷarttaṉar.  atuvē mullai yāḻ.  koṟṟavai kuṟiñci nila makkaḷukku vil yāḻai aḷittāḷ.  tīṅkuḻalait tirumāl mullai nila makkaḷukku uvantaḷittār.
viṇṇait toṭṭu niṟkum māmalaiyiṉ aṭivārattilē kāṭum ceṭiyum kāṉyāṟum kāṭci tarum mullai nilattilē paccaik kampaḷam virittāṟ pōṉṟu eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻum pacumpuṟ ṟarailē āṭum māṭum mēyntu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  kaṉṟukaḷ tuḷḷik kutittu viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa.  kārkālam ākaiyāl eṅkum kaviṉ peṟakkāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.  maraṅkaḷilum, ceṭikaḷi lum, koṭikaḷilum eḻil tarum eṇṇilā vaṇṇamalarkaḷ kottuk kottākap pūttuk kuluṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa; kaṇṭōr kaṇ kaḷaiyum karuttaiyum kavarvaṉavāy kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṅkum kamakama veṉṉum naṟumaṇa nāṟṟam vīcikkoṇṭē irukkiṉṟatu.
māṭṭu mantaiyiṉ mattiyil āyaṉ aṇipeṟa niṟkiṉṟāṉ.  avaṉ irupatu vayatum nirampappeṟāta kaṭṭiḷam vāynta kāḷai.  kaṭṭu vāynta kaviṉuṟu uṭalaiyum tiraṇṭa tōḷkaḷaiyum virinta mārpaiyum uṭaiya oru āṇaḻakaṉ.  kārmēkam pōṉṟa kaṟutta niṟattaiyuṭaiyavaṉ.  ciṟanta nōkkiṉaṉ.  araiyil kaṉatta āṭaiyai iṟukap piṇaittuḷḷāṉ.  avaṉatu paranta tiraṇṭa tōḷil mullai malarum ellaiyaṟṟa maṇantarum ēṉaiya eḻil tarum pūkkaḷum kōkkap peṟṟa mālaiyoṉṟu aṇi ceykiṟatu.  araiyiṟ piṇittuḷḷa kaccaiyil oru vēyṅkuḻal cerukap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  oru kaiyilē kōlum maṟṟoru kaiyilē vilyāḻum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
katiravaṉ taṉ poṟkatirkaḷai vīcikkoṇṭu takatakaveṉak kiḻakkilē tōṉṟi mika viraivāka uccikku nērāka vantuḷḷāṉ.  eḻil mika vāynta iḷam naṅkai orutti kuṭuvaiyilēyuḷḷa kūḻai eṭuttut talai mītu vaittukkoṇṭu oyyāramāka naṭantu varukiṉṟāḷ.  ataip pārttatum āyaṉ mukattil iḷam nakai polikiṉṟatu.  kārikaiyaik kaṇṭatum kāḷaikkuk kaṭṭuk kaṭaṅkāk kaḷippu.  avaḷ koṇṭuvarum kūḻaik kaṇṭatum kāḷaik kaḷippeṉṉum kaṭalil mūḻkiṉāṉ.  eṉṟālum avaṉ avaḷaik kaṇṭatum taṉ āruyirt tuṇaiviyeṉa ōṭippōy, kaṭṭi aṇaittu mārpuṟat taḻuvi muttamiṭavillai.  avaṉ avaḷ arukē vantatum mutaṉmutalāka veḷḷāṭu iru kuṭṭikaḷ īṉṟataiyum, kārām pacu oru kaṉṟu pōṭṭataiyum kūṟiviṭṭuk kūḻai vāṅkik koṇṭu avaḷatu karaṅkaḷaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu ālamarattaṭiyil amarntu akamakiḻntu akamuṭai yāḷōṭu aḷavaḷāvip pēcikkoṇṭē vayiṟu nirampak kūḻaik kuṭittāṉ.  appāl makiḻcci poṅka maraniḻalil maṇal mītu amarntu iṭuppil ceruki irukkum vēyṅkuḻalai eṭuttu vāyil vaikkiṉṟāṉ.  arukil amarntirukkum avaṉatu akamuṭaiyāḷ tirumālākiya mullai nilat teyvattaik kaṇṇārak kāṇpatu pōlavē kaṇavaṉaik kaṇṭu kaḷikkiṉṟāḷ.  kaṇavaṉ tīṅkuḻalil pālaip paṇṇaip pāṅkuṭaṉ icaikkiṉṟāṉ.  iḷam pāvai nallāḷ iṉiya icaiyil mūḻki iṉpakkaṭalil amiḻntu kiṭakkiṉṟāḷ.  ciṟitu nēram ceṉṟatum aḻaku mikka anta āycci, akattai nōkki avacaramāka naṭantu ceṉṟāḷ.
avaṉ ceṉṟapiṉ āyaṉ taṉ vilyāḻai eṭuttu mīṭṭukiṉ ṟāṉ.  inta vilyāḻ āyaṉē amaittatu.  tuḷaiyuṭaiya kumiḻaṅ kompai villāka vaḷaittu nāriṉālē kaṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ.  orē aḷavuḷḷa ēḻu vilkaḷ oṉṟāka iṇaittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  nāṇkaḷ ciṟitum peritumāy uyarttiyum tāḻttiyum kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  āyaṉ narampukaḷait toṭṭu yāḻai mīṭṭi icai yoppumai ceykiṉṟāṉ.  cōlaiyelām iṉṉoli, vāṉa melām iṉṉoli, āṭu, māṭu, eliyum, puliyum, mayilum, kuyilum, pāmpum, palliyum anta iṉṉoli kēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟu meymaṟantu niṉṟaṉa.  āyaṉ anta yāḻil peṇ iṉpattaiviṭa periya pēriṉpattait tuykkiṉṟāṉ.  iṉpa mikutiyāl avaṉum ciṟitu nēram meymaṟantu niṉṟāṉ.  appāl mālai nēram ākiviṭṭatai aṟintu yāḻai vaittuviṭṭuk kuḻalai eṭuttuc cātārip paṇṇai icaikkiṉṟāṉ.  piṉ āṭukaḷaiyum māṭukaḷaiyum kaṉṟukaḷaiyum tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu vīṭu nōkki varukiṉṟāṉ.  appāl avai kaḷait toḻuvattil kaṭṭiyum paṭṭiyil maṭakkiyum viṭṭuviṭṭu illattiṟkuḷ pukukiṉṟāṉ.  avaṉatu akamuṭaiyāḷ paṭaikkum amutai aruntiviṭṭu veku oyyāramākat taṉ illak kiḻatti yōṭu arukarukē amarntu koṇṭu icai kūṭṭi vaitta vil yāḻai eṭuttuk kuṟiñcip paṇṇai icaikkiṉṟāṉ.  maṉaivi kēṭṭu makiḻkiṉṟāḷ.  anta iravu iṉpa iravāka, tēṉ iravākat tikaḻntatu.  naḷḷiravil kuṟiñci, kuṟiñciyāyp puṇarntatu.
inta vilyāḻtāṉ tamiḻaṉ - ēṉ? mullai nila āyaṉ mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭa icaikkaruvi.  iṉṟaikku aiyāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukkumuṉ mutalāvatāka oru narampuṭaiya vilyāḻ eḻuntatu.  itu caṅkak kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu.  itu paḻaṅ kaṟkālam.  irum pāṇiyō, cempāṇiyō, uḷiyō, cuttiyō illāta kālam.  eṉavē kaiyāl kampukaḷai vaḷaittu maranārkaḷāl villaik kaṭṭi maralnāriṉait tirittu narampākak kaṭṭi yāḻai uruvākki ṉar.  appāl nāḷaṭaivil atu vaḷarcci aṭaintu mūṉṟu narampu kaḷum, aintu narampukaḷum piṉ ēḻu narampukaḷumāka eḻuntaṉa.  itaṟku ilakkaṇamum vakuttaṉar.  yāḻukku ettaṉai vilkaḷ?  ettaṉai narampukaḷ?  eṉṉa kompāl yāḻ ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum, yāḻiṉ nīḷam, akalam, kuṟukkāka vaittuk kaṭṭavēṇṭiya kompukaḷiṉ nīḷam akalam ataṉ eṇṇam muṟukkāṇikaḷiṉ eṇṇam, oṟṟuṟuppukaḷ amaikkum muṟai ivaikaḷukkellām ilakkaṇam vakuttaṉar.  avai mikavum virintaṉa.
piṉṉar vilyāḻiṉ amaippu attuṇai ciṟantatāka illai eṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar.  mēlum yāḻ oliyai arukil iruppavarkaḷ tāṉ kēṭka muṭiyum, nīṇṭa tūram ivvoli kēṭka eṉṉa ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpataiyellām cintikka muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  eṉavē pala vakaiyāṉa yāḻaic ceyyavum, ēḻu, oṉpatu, paṉṉiraṇṭu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉāṟu, irupatu, nūṟu, āyiram eṉṟu narampukaḷaik kaṭṭi pala vakaiyāṉa vaṭivil pala yāḻ vakaikaḷai uruvākkiṉar.
inta yāḻai amaippataṟku maṭṭum appaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ ilakkaṇam kāṇavillai.  atai icai kūṭṭum muṟaikkum ilakkaṇam kaṇṭaṉar.  muṟukkāṇikaḷai muṭukkavum, nekiḻttavum vaḻi kaṇṭaṉar.  muṟukkāṇikaḷaip piṉṉē taḷḷa, muṟukkēṟṟi narampiṉai valittal, melittal ceytu piṉ atait taḷḷi iṟukkik koḷḷavum terintukoṇṭaṉar.  appāl narampukaḷukku uḻai mutalāka kaikkiḷai īṟāka ēḻu peyarkaḷai vaittup pala nāṭkaḷ āyntu iṟutiyāka icai kūṭṭa muṭipu kaṇṭaṉar.  tārattiṉ aintāmicai uḻai eṉṟu koṇṭaṉar.
pallāṇṭākat tamiḻ makkaḷ yāḻaip paṟṟi āyntu palvēṟu vitamāṉa yāḻkaḷaik kaṇṭaṉar.  avaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ namakku atikamākak kiṭaikkavillai.  namakkut terintavaikaḷaip paṟṟi viḷakkik kūṟuvōm.  avai kālattil muṟpaṭṭum piṟpaṭṭum irukkalām.
yāḻ vakaikaḷ
1.	irāvaṇācura yāḻ
2.	kaccapi yāḻ
3.	kaḷāvati yāḻ
4.	kiṉṉari yāḻ
5.	kīcaka yāḻ
6.	cakōṭa yāḻ (14 allatu 16 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu)
7.	cīṟi yāḻ (9 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu)
8.	ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ (7 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu)
9.	tīkkucci yāḻ
10.	tumpuṟu yāḻ
11.	nārata yāḻ
12.	pirakati yāḻ
13.	pēri yāḻ (21 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu)
14.	makati yāḻ (peruṅkalam) (21 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu)
15.	makara yāḻ (19 allatu 17 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu)
16.	maruttuva yāḻ
17.	muḷari yāḻ (muṇṭaka nalyāḻ)
18.	vallaki yāḻ
19.	varāḷi yāḻ
20.	viṟkōṭṭi yāḻ
21.	vil yāḻ
22.	peruṅkalam (paravai yāḻ) 1008 narampukaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟatu.
23.	āti yāḻ
24.	tantiri yāḻ
icai ivvāṟu iṉṉum pala uḷa eṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

8.  yāḻ vakaikaḷum vaṭivaṅkaḷum
āti kālattil kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ vil yāḻaik kaṇṭaṉar.  ammutu makkaḷ kaṇṭa vilyāḻ ātiyil oru narampuṭaiyatākavē iruntatu eṉalām.  āṉāl mullai nilattu āyarē kuḻalaiyum vil yāḻaiyum kaṇṭaṉar eṉap pattup pāṭṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
appāl avarkaḷiṭam eḻunta oli āyvāl iraṇṭu mūṉṟu (nāṉku) aintu narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya yāḻkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉalām.  iṉṟum irāvaṇātam eṉṟu oru yāḻ uḷḷatu.  atu oṟṟait tēṅkāy ōṭṭiṉāl vīṇātaṇṭam nuḻaittu mūṉṟu tantikaḷ pūṭṭi muṟukkāṇi iṟukkappaṭṭa yāḻāka iruk kiṟatu.  itu irāvaṇaṉ talaiyaip pūṭṭit, taṉ atattait taṇṭāyamaittut taṉ narampukaḷai nāṇkaḷākap pūṭṭi icaittuc civapirāṉai ēṟṟip pōṟṟip pāṭiyataik kuṟippiṭum ciṉṉamāka amainta yāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.1  iṉṟu itaṉai irāvaṇaṉ kaiyāḻ eṉak kūṟulām.
itaip paṟṟi nāṭṭiya mētai ār. irañcaṉ taṉatu, nāṭṭiyam eṉṉum tiṅkaḷ itaḻil kuṟippiṭuvatāvatu: irāvaṇātam, 7000-m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ villiṉāl icaikkappaṭṭa tantiravāttiyamāy nilaviyatu.  itu tōliṉāl mūṭṭappaṭṭa attimarak kuṭattaiyuṭaiyatu.  kuṭattiṉiṉṟu 22 aṅkulamuḷḷa marattaṇṭu oṉṟu cerukappaṭṭirukkum.  nuṉiyil iraṇṭu tuḷaiyiṭṭu iru āṇikaḷ pukuttappaṭṭirukkum, āṇi kaḷai muṟukkiṉāl tantikaḷ iṟukum; curuti māṟutal peṟum.  itaṟku mukkāl aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa kutiraiyum uṇṭu.  itaṉ tantikaḷ māṉ narampukaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟavai.  itai mīṭṭa oru villum uṇṭu.
vil, eṉpatu mūṅkil pirampai vaḷaittu iru nuṉikaḷilum iḻuttuk kaṭṭi iruppārkaḷ.  atu irāvaṇaṉ kaṇṭupiṭitta vāttiyam.2
itē pōṉṟu orē narampuḷḷa maruttuva yāḻum iruntatu. itaṉai viḷakki innūlil vēṟoriṭattil virivākak kūṟi yuḷḷōm.
kēraḷa nāṭṭaic cērnta kaḷḷikkōṭṭaikkup pakkatti luḷḷa vaḷḷuva nāṭṭil uḷḷa peruntal maṉṉā eṉṟa ūrukkup pakkattiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil māṭukaḷ mēykkum puḷḷuvar eṉṉum iṉattavar tam kaiyiluḷḷa oru narampuḷḷa vil yāḻil icai mīṭṭi varukiṟārkaḷ.
oru narampuḷḷa tuntināmā eṉṉum icaik karuviyai namatu nāṭṭup piccaikkārarkaḷ iṉṟum icaittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ivai ellām namatu yāḻukku mūlak karuviyāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷōṭu vil yāḻ campantamāy māṟu paṭṭa apippirāyaṅ koṇṭa tiru. ā.a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ, oru narampuḷḷa vil yāḻilē kuṟiñcip paṇṇiṟkuriya ēḻu kēḷvikaḷaiyum icaittal kūṭum.  vil yāḻukku vipulāṉantar kūṟum ēḻu kōṭukaḷ avaciyam illai eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
oru narampuḷḷa vil yāḻiṉiṉṟu 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 11, 14, 16, 17, 19, 21, 100, 1000 narampukaḷ uḷḷa yāḻkaḷait tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.  ovvoru yāḻum vevvēṟu uruvam peṟṟatāy amaikkap peṟṟiruntatu.  ivaikaḷaip paṟṟi namakku ōraḷavu ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷtāṉ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉavē yoḻiya cumēriyā, ekiptu nāṭukaḷaip pōṉṟu acal uruvamō ōviyam ciṟpam pōṉṟavai kaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷō atikam kiṭaikkavillai.  tamiḻakattil ippoḻututāṉ, tirumeyyattilum kumpakōṇattilum (tārā curattilum) cila uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  amarāvatiyilum, (āntirāvilum) vaṭa intiyāvilum cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa.  avaikaḷaip paṟṟi, iṅkuc ciṟitum ārāyvōm.  tiru meyyam, kuṭantai kōyilkaḷil yāḻ, ciṟpaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
vilyāḻ
icai oli vakaikaḷai (ēṭṭai), narampiṉ maṟai (vētam) eṉavum, paṇkaḷai, yāḻiṉ pakuti eṉavum eṭuttōtiṉār, pērā ciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār.  itai uṉṉippāka uṟṟu uṇarntāl paḻan tamiḻarkaḷ narampuk karuviyākiya yāḻiṉai vaittē paṇ kaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷaiyum vakaippaṭuttit tokaip paṭutti avaikaḷai ulakiṟku aḷittaṉar eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum.
ātikālattil makkaḷ vēṭṭaiyāṭi uyir vāḻntu vantaṉar.  villil kaṇai ēṟṟi tolaiyil uḷḷa paṟavaikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, kāy, kaṉikaḷaik kuṟippārttu eyya, kaṇaikaḷ viraintu pāyum vaṇṇam villil nāṇai viṟaippākak kaṭṭuvar.  nāṇiṉ iṟukkattai aṟintu atai viralāl teḷittup pārppar.  nāṇi liruntu kaṇīr eṉṟu oli eḻuntāl nāṇ viṟaippāka iruppatāka eṇṇuvar.  nāṇiliruntu eḻum iṉiya ōcaiyai aṟinta vēṭarkaḷ pallāṇṭāka āyntu vil yāḻai amaittaṉar.  vil yāḻai, nal yāḻākka nīṇṭa kālam muyaṉṟu, vil yāḻiṉ nīḷattaik kūṭṭiyum kuṟaittum, iṟutiyil tam muyaṟciyil veṟṟi kaṇṭaṉar.  appāl iraṇṭu vil yāḻaiyum, mūṉṟu vil yāḻaiyum, nāṉku vil yāḻaiyum iṟutiyāka aintu vil yāḻkaḷaiyum iṇaittuk kaṭṭiṉar.  ovvoru vil yāḻiṉ narampaiyum taḷarttiyum muṟukki yum palvēṟu melivum valivum uḷḷa olikaḷaik kaṇṭaṉar.  iṟutiyāka ēḻu vil yāḻkaḷai iṇaittu ēḻu narampukaḷai yuṭaiya oru niṟaivāṉa vil yāḻai amaittaṉar1.  itaṉ payaṉāka ēḻu curaṅkaḷum piṟantaṉa.  ovvoru narampukaḷukkum muṟaiyē tāram, kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari yeṉa ēḻu curaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ koṭukkap peṟṟaṉa.  appāl nāṉilaṅ kaḷum taṉakkut taṉakkeṉa vevvēṟu vakaiyāṉa yāḻkaḷaik kaṇṭaṉa eṉṟālum, oru nilam piṟa nilattu yāḻai viruppuṭaṉum mīṭṭivantatu.  āṉirai mēykkum āyaṉ makaṉ kumiḻa marak kompu kaḷai villāka vaḷaittu maral nāriṉai narampākak kaṭṭi vil yāḻil kuṟiñcip paṇṇai icaittāṉ.  intak kāṭci perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil peritum viyantu pēcappaṭukiṟatu.  mutaṟ caṅkak kālattiṟku muṉṉar tōṉṟiya vil yāḻai, kaṭaiccaṅkap pulavarāṉa kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṭuttukkāṭṭiyatu yāḻiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum vaṉmaiyaiyum naṉmaiyaiyum naṉku eṭuttu viḷakkuvataṟkēyākum.
pēriyāḻ piṉṉu makarañ cakōṭamuṭaṉ
cīrpoliyuñ ceṅkōṭu ceppiṉār - tārpolintu
maṉṉun tirumārpa vaṇkūṭaṟ kōmāṉē
piṉṉum uḷavē piṟa
eṉṟa cilappatikāra urai mēṟkōṭ ceyyuḷāl pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṉum nālvakai yāḻkaḷ kaṭaiccaṅkak kālattil iruntatākap pulappaṭukiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi tamiḻakattil palvēṟu vakai yāḻkaḷum iruntaṉa.  iṉṟaikku eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭum.  kallāṭam eṉṟa nūlil nārata yāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, maruttuva yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, makati yāḻ, kaccapi yāḻ eṉṟu cila yāḻ vakaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tirunelvēli tiru. kaṅkai muttuppiḷḷai avarkaḷ eḻutiya naṭanāti vāttiya rañcaṉam eṉṉum nūlil tīkkuccikkai yāḻ, varāḷi yāḻ, vallaki yāḻ, irāvaṇēcura yāḻ pōṉṟa cila yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟip piṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  caṅkīta rattiṉākaram eṉṉum nūlil, pirakati, kaḷāvati eṉṟa iru yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vil yāḻ, pāṭṭāṇmai peṟṟa pāṇarkaḷ kaik koṇṭa yāḻalla.  villāṇmai peṟṟa vēṭarkaḷākiya kuṟiñci nilamākkaḷ koṇṭa yāḻākum.  mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē oru narampu pūṭṭiya vil yāḻiliruntu 1008 narampukaḷ pūṭṭiya peruṅkalam eṉṉum pēriyāḻ varai iruntatu eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.  oru kālattil 21 narampukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭa pēriyāḻē peruvaḻakkil iruntu vantatu.  1008 narampukaḷ koṇṭa peruṅkalam (pēriyāḻ) icaippataṟku mikavum iṭar tantatu.  eṉavē ēḻu icaikaḷaiyum melivu camaṉ, valivu eṉṉum mūṉṟu tāṉaṅkaḷilum icaippataṟ kēṟṟavaḻiyil 21 narampukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟa pēriyāḻāka maṟumalarcci aṭaintatu.  makara yāḻ 19 narampukaḷaip peṟṟatu.  cakōṭa yāḻ 14 narampukaḷaiyum, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ 7 narampukaḷaiyum peṟṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi iraṇṭu mūṉṟu - ēṉ?  oru narampaiyuṭaiya ciṟiya yāḻ kaḷum akkālattil iruntu vantaṉa.
yāḻ mika vaḷaivāka iruntatāl aḻakaṟṟum icaippataṟku vacatiyaṟṟum iruntatu; eṉavē cakōṭa yāḻil uḷḷa vaḷaivāṉa kōṭu eṉṉum uṟuppai nimirtti nērākac ceytu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ceyyappeṟṟatu.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ civanta marattiṉāl ceyyappeṟṟatu.  ikkālattil uḷḷa vīṇaiyaip pōlavē itaṟkum ēḻu narampukaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  itu mikac ciṟiyatāka iruntatāl cīṟi yāḻ eṉṟum kūṟappeṟṟatu..
vaḷarcci
yāḻil uḷḷa kōṭu nimirttappeṟṟatait tavira ataṉ uṟuppukaḷ etuvum māṟṟap peṟavillai.  ataṉ narampukaḷum peyarum māṟṟap peṟavillai.  kōṭu tiruttappeṟṟa yāḻ kōṭṭuvāttiyam (makā vīṇai) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭatu.  atu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa aḻaikkappeṟṟatu. kōṭu tiruttap peṟṟu cemmaiyuṟṟatāl cakōṭa yāḻiṉṟu tampurāvum, kōṭṭu vāttiyamum iṟutiyākac ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum, tantiri yāḻum malarntirukkalām ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṉum taṉip peyaraip peṟṟatu.
iṉṟaiyat tamiḻarkaḷ yāḻaik kaṇṭatē illai.  ataṉ iṉṉoliyaic cevimaṭukkum pēṟṟiṉaip peṟṟatē illai.  yāḻ maṟaintu viṭṭālum namatu kōyilkaḷ cilavaṟṟil cilai vaṭivam peṟṟu iṉṟum ataṉ uruvam aḻiyātu ilaṅkukiṟatu eṉa ippoḻutu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  yāḻiṉ uruvaṅ kaḷaip paṟṟi namatu akarātikaḷil kūṭak kāṇamuṭiyavilai.
pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭuttokai ākiya caṅka nūṟ ceyyuṭ kaḷil tamiḻ muṉṉōrkaḷ icaitta paḻaiya yāḻkkaruviyiṉ uruvaccāyalum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷākiya pattar pōrvait tōl, kōṭu, āṇi, tivavu, narampiṉ toṭarcci, unti eṉpaṉavaṟṟil amaippum, viṉai, mey, payaṉ, uru eṉṉum nālvakai uvamaṅ kaḷiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu naṉku viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  paṇṭai nāḷil vaḻaṅkiya narampukkaruviyākiya yāḻ eṉpatu maruppu eṉṉum kōṭākiya uṟuppu vaḷaiyappeṟṟatāyt tōṟṟattuṭaṉ ceviyāṟ kēṭṭu makiḻaṟku iṉiya icai nalaṅkaḷait tōṟṟuvikkum ciṟappuṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkiyatu.
yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ
yāḻiṉai iṉṟu kāṇamuṭiyāviṭṭālum ciṟpa vaṭivil ataṉ uruviṉait tamiḻakattil tirumeyyam (tirumayam), tiruerukkat tampuliyūr, tārācuram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil iṉṟu kāṇamuṭikiṟatu.  tiruerukkattampiliyūril tiru nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar cilaiyil oru yāḻai avar tāṅki niṟpataik kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi āntirāvil uḷḷa amarāvatiyil uḷḷa kōli eṉṉum iṭattil ki. pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷil vaḷainta paḻaiya cīṟiyāḻ pōṉṟa yāḻaiyum kōṭu, nimirnta putiya ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ pōṉṟa aḻakiya yāḻaiyum kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.  mēlum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷākiya tirāviṭar kaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamāṉa mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum cumēriyā nāṭṭiṉ (mecapottāmiyāviṉ) talainakarākiya ūr eṉṉum nakaril akaḻntu kaṇṭa putai kuḻikaḷil 5000āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vaḻakkilirunta poṟṟakaṭṭāl potiyappeṟṟa nalyāḻ uruvaṅ kaḷaip putaiporuḷ ārāycci nipuṇarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭut tuḷḷārkaḷ.1  atu tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻoṭum, mokañcatā rōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷil poṟikkappeṟṟa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷōṭum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyatāyk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu.1
muṟkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻiṉ kōṭu vaḷaintu iruntatu; aḻakaṟṟiruntatu.  itu nām kūṟum kūṟṟaṉṟu; namatu pakaivarkaḷ kūṟum coṟkaḷumaṉṟu; uṇmaiyē atuvākum.  ataṉāl teyvap pulavar tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷil,
kaṇaikoṭi tiyāḻkōṭu cevvitāṅ kaṉṉa
viṉaipaṭu pālāṟ koḷal
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.  itaṟkup poruḷ kaṇṭa parimē laḻakar, ampu, vaṭivāṟ cevvitāyiṉum ceyalāṟ koṭitu.  yāḻ kōṭṭāl vaḷaintatāyiṉum ceyalāṟ cevvitu.  avvakaiyē tavam ceyvōraiyum koṭiyar, cevviyar eṉpatu vaṭivāṟ koḷḷātu avar ceyalpaṭṭa kūṟṟāṉē aṟintu koḷka eṉṟu urai kūṟiyiruppatu yāḻ vaḷaintu aḻakaṟṟiruntatu eṉpatai meyppippatākum.
vil yāḻ, kuṟiñci nilattilē vāḻnta villarkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻ eṉat teriya varukiṟatu.  vil yāḻ mullai nilattilē tōṉṟiya yāḻ eṉṟu perumpāṇāṟṟappaṭai kūṟukiṟatu.  enta nilattaiyum viṭa kuṟiñci nilattilētāṉ vil yāḻ tōṉṟum cūḻnilai atikam. vēṭṭaiyāṭi vayiṟu vaḷarkkum vēṭar iṉam kuṟiñci nilattiltāṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarkaḷukkuttāṉ vēṭṭuvat toḻil uriyatu.  vēṭarkaḷtāṉ mutaṉmutalil villaik kaṇṭaṉar.  villait tiṟampaṭap payaṉpaṭuttavum muṟ paṭṭaṉar.  eṉavē avarkaḷ villar eṉum peyaraiyum peṟṟaṉar.
mēlum, cēra nāṭṭilētāṉ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷiṟ palar kuṭiyēṟiṉar.  cēranāṭṭu makkaḷ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa cantatikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruttamāṉatu.  cērarkaḷ villarkaḷ eṉa aḻaikkappaṭuvar.  cērar āṭci, villaik koṭiyākavum muttiraiyākavum ēṟṟukkoṇṭatu.  cērarkaḷ iṉṟum, malaiyāḷikaḷ, villarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē malaiyāḷikaḷ kaṇṭa icaikkaruviyākiya vil yāḻ, malaiyāḻ eṉṟu tiripuṟṟu iṟutiyil yāḻ eṉṉum peyaraip peṟṟirukka lām eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
kēraḷa nāṭṭil uḷḷa cāṇār eṉṟa oru vakuppār ēkatantiri vīṇaiyaip pōṉṟa oru vakai vil yāḻai iṉṟum payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ.  atu kēraḷa nāṭṭup pāmara makkaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷōṭu (nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷōṭu) cērttu eṇṇappaṭu kiṟatu.  pīkār, cōṭṭā nākappūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum itu pōṉṟa vilyāḻ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  tāylāntu, ci-cāṉ-lōvō, lāvō, piyā, pōrṉiyō, pūcōy eṉṟa nāṭukaḷil vil yāḻ pōṉṟa icaikkaruvikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu intiyāvil pala malaippakutikaḷil vāḻum tirāviṭa iṉattaic cērntavarkaḷākak karutappaṭum vēṭṭuvarkaḷ vil yāḻaip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ.  inta vil yāḻ cermaṉ nāṭṭilum cupeyiṉ (Spain) nāṭṭilum mūr eṉṉum paḻuppu niṟa makkaḷiṭamum nalla vaḷarcci peṟṟa icaik karuviyāyt tikaḻ kiṉṟatu.  itaṉaip paḻamaiyāṉa piṭil eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  inta icaik karuvi cermaṉ nāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil kūṭa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.1
vil yāḻum ataṉ aṭippaṭaiyil uruvākkap peṟṟa pala yāḻkaḷum tamiḻakattilum vaṭa intiyāvilum piṟa nāṭukaḷilum paraviyuḷḷatu.  iṉṟu anta vil yāḻkaḷ toṭakkak kālattuṭaṉ ulakameṅkumuḷḷa nākarikam peṟāta makkaḷiṭamum palvēṟu peyarkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa kāṭurai eṉṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭum orē nāṇaiyuṭaiya kiṉṉari eṉṉum yāḻ namatu paḻaiya vil yāḻuṭaṉ oppu nōkkaṟ kuriyatu, eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ kūrmā nāṭṭiyattil mūṅkilai uṭaittuc ceyyap peṟṟa oru vita icaik karuvi iṉṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  itu vil yāḻiṉ iṉattaic cērntatē yākum.
kēraḷa nāṭṭaic cērnta vaḷḷuva nāṭṭu vaṭṭattil uḷḷa peruntal maṉṉā eṉṟa ūrukku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil āṭu māṭukaḷai mēyttuk koṇṭum piccai eṭuttuk koṇṭum uyir vāḻum nāṭōṭikaḷāṉa puḷḷuvar eṉṉum iṉattavarkaḷ kaiyil vil yāḻai vaittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.  inta vil yāḻil oru nuṉiyil maral kayiṟṟiṉ oru nuṉiyaik kaṭṭi maṟu nuṉiyai1 vil yāḻiṉ maṟu kōṭiyil uḷḷa taṭuppil kaṭṭātu ataik kōttu villiṉ oru pakkamāka iṭakkaiyāl iḻuttup piṭittuk koṇṭu valakkaiyil oru cillai (piḷāccai)k koṇṭu nāṇait teṟikkiṟārkaḷ.  uṭaṉē atiṉiṉṟum ariya nātam eḻukiṟatu.  avarkaḷiṉ iṭatu kaiyāl anta nāṇ iḻupaṭuvataṟku ēṟpa oli melintum valintum eḻukiṟatu.  inta vil yāḻai mīṭṭi avarkaḷ miṭaṟṟāl pāṭuvārkaḷ.  avarkaḷatu miṭaṟṟup pāṭṭil perum pakuti tamiḻc coṟkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  inta vil yāḻiṉ nāṇai iṟukak kaṭṭi villākavum payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ.2
vil yāḻiṉ toṉmai
vil yāḻ mikamikat toṉmai vāynta tamiḻar kaṇṭa icaik karuvi. itutāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ mīṭṭiya āti icaikkaruvi.  inta vil yāḻiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilē mēṉāṭṭiṉar kaṇṭa ārp (Harp) eṉṉum icaikkaruviyum amaintirukkiṉṟatu eṉa icaip pulavarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
inta vil yāḻ tamiḻar kaṇṭa yāḻā?  itu caṅka nūṟkaḷil atikam kāṇappaṭavillaiyē eṉṟu cila tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ kūṭa viṉavukiṟārkaḷ.  caṅkam maruviya pattup pāṭṭukaḷil oṉṟāṉa, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil,
centīt toṭṭa karuntuḷaik kuḻaliṉ
iṉṟīm pālai muṉaiyiṟ kumiḻiṉ
puḻaṟkōṭṭut toṭutta maraṟpuri narampiṉ
vilyāḻ icaikkum viraleṟi kuṟiñcip
palkāṟ paṟavai kiḷaicet tōrkkum
pullār viyaṉpulam pōki
eṉṟu kaṭaic caṅkap pulavarākiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār, toṇṭamāṉ iḷantirayaṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭi inta vil yāḻaik kuṟippiṭṭiruppatu kūrntu nōkkutaṟkuriyatu.  itil pēriyāḻiṟ pulamai vāynta pāṇar, iḷantirayaṉaip pāṭip paricu peṟa virumpiyatākavum maṟṟoru pāṇaṉ kāñciyil āṇṭa piṟitoru iḷantirayaṉaiyum avaṉ vaḷḷaṟṟaṉmaiyaiyum kuṟiñci mutaliya aintiṇaip pakuppaiyum avaṟṟil vāḻvōr vāḻkkai muṟaiyaiyum virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.  itil pēriyāḻaiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷaiyum kāñci vaḻiyil kāṇap pōkum vil yāḻaiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷaiyum viritturaittuḷḷār.  itil, vil yāḻiṟku oru kōṭum oru nāṇum uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  ataṟkup pattal, kavaikkaṭai, vaṟuvāy, puri narampukkōl, tivavu oṟṟuṟuppu mutaliyaṉa illātatāl kuṟippiṭavillai eṉṟum terikiṟatu.  eṉavē ātiyil vil yāḻiṟku oru kōṭum, oru nāṇum iruntatu eṉṟa namatu karuttai ippāṭṭu araṇ ceyvatāka miḷirkiṟatu.  inta oru kōṭum oru narampum uḷḷa vilyāḻilē paḻaṅkāla āyaṉ kuṟiñcip paṇṇai mīṭṭi irukka vēṇṭum.  orē narampil ēḻu kēḷvikaḷaiyum vācittirukkakkūṭum eṉṟum iṉṟaiya aṟiñarkaḷ cilar karutukiṟārkaḷ.  
vilyāḻ, yāḻkaḷiṉ tāy yāḻ
pāṇar, kaivaḻi eṉṟa nūlil, tiru. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟiñar vipulāṉantar, yāḻ nūlil, vil yāḻ eṉṟumē ēḻu narampukaḷaip peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñar vipulāṉantar karuti ṉāl atu tavaṟākum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.  atēpōl, vil yāḻ eṉṟumē orē narampaittāṉ peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñar varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ karutiṉāl atuvum tavaṟē eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu.  toṭakkak kālattil vil yāḻ oru kōṭum oru narampum peṟṟu appāl paṭippaṭiyāy nāḷaṭaivil oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, ēḻu kōṭukaḷaiyum, ēḻu narampukaḷaiyum peṟṟu vilyāḻ virutti aṭaintatu eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu.  atōṭu vilyāḻ tāṉ, mutaṉ mutalil kuṟiñciyil tōṉṟiya āti yāḻ eṉpatu eṉatu muṭivāṉa karuttu.  ataṉai aṟiñar vipulā ṉantar tamatu yāḻ nūlil ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷār.1  āṉāl aṟiñar varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ pāṇar kaivaḻi eṉṉum tam nūlil yāḻ eṉṉum palavitak karuvikaḷ tōṉṟiyapiṉ vil yāḻ arum piyatu eṉṟu kūṟum kūṟṟai eṉṉāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiya villai.2  tiruvarakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṟṟukkuc ciṟitum ātāram kiṭaiyātu eṉpatu eṉatu muṭipu
villiṉiṉṟē mutaṉmutalāka vilyāḻ arumpi, appāl atu oru narampiṉiṉṟu aintu narampaip peṟṟup piṉ ēḻu narampaip peṟṟu vaḷarcciyuṟṟup pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ mutaliyavaikaḷum āti yāḻ eṉṉum āyiram narampuṭaiya peruṅkalamum eḻuntaṉa eṉpatait teḷivāka tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarkaḷil oruvarākiya tiru. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉ paṉṉiru tirumuṟai varalāṟu eṉṉum tam periya nūlil naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
muṟkālattil vēṭṭait toḻilai mēṟkoṇṭa makkaḷ ampukaḷai eyyuṅkāl avai neṭuntūram viraintu celluva taṟku ēṟṟavaṇṇam villiṉkaṇ nāṇai iṟukak kaṭṭiṉar.  vil nāṇiṉ iṟukkattait terintu koḷvataṟku nāṇiṉai viralāl teṟittu atiliruntu eḻum ōcaiyai aḷavu karuviyākak koṇ ṭaṉar.  vil nāṇiṉ ōcaiyaik kēṭṭu ataṉ icai nuṭpattai uṇarnta nam muṉṉōrkaḷ icait tōṟṟattiṟku iṭaṉāyuḷḷa vil nāṇiṉ nīḷattaik kuṟaittuṅ kūṭṭiyum, palvēṟu iṉṉicaic curutikaḷait tōṟṟuvittaṉar.  ivvāṟu vevvēṟu iṉṉicaic curutikaḷait tarum pala viṟkaḷai oṉṟākac cērttu neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉ nam muṉṉōrkaḷāl amaittuk koḷḷap peṟṟa narampuk karuvi, vil yāḻ eṉpatākum.
mikap paḻaṅkālattil tōṉṟiya vil yāḻ ākiya ik karuviyai aṭippaṭaiyākakkoṇṭē icai vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṉum nālvakai yāḻum āyiram narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya peruṅkalam eṉṉum ātiyāḻum piṟa narampuk karuvikaḷum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭaiya perumakkaḷāl āyntuṇarntu amaikkappeṟṟaṉa.
villiṉiṉṟu oru narampuṭaiya vil yāḻum piṉ 5 narampukaḷai yuṭaiya vil yāḻum appāl 7 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya vil yāḻum piṉṉar cīṟi yāḻ, pēri yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, makara yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, āti yāḻ pōṉṟa palavakaiyāṉa yāḻkaḷum piṟantaṉa eṉṉum namatu karuttai vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ ciṟanta māṇava rākiya tiru veḷḷai vāraṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamatu nūlil teḷi vāka viḷakkik kūṟiyiruppatu kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟukiṉṟōm.  atu, uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa pariṇāmavāta cittāntattiṟkum aṟiviṟ kum icai nūlkaḷukkum ēṟpuṭaittāy irukkiṟatu.
piṟa nāṭukaḷil vil yāḻ
mēṉāṭṭup pērāciriyar pāṉum eṉpavar ekiptiyac cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷil vil yāḻ, kiṟittava ūḻi eḻuvataṟku 3000-m āṇṭu mutal 4000ām āṇṭu varaiyuḷḷa kāla ellaiyai uṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.  tamiḻakattil kiṭaikkum cāṉṟukaḷ mūlam yāḻ eṉṉum toṉmaiyāṉa icaik karuviyē ekiptiyac cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷil oḷirkiṟatu eṉpatai nām naṉku meyppikka muṭiyum.  mēlum āciriyar pāṉum ariṭāṭṭilum kuṟippiṭum ki. mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiluḷḷa curuti vāttiya icaiyaic cērnta icaic karuvikaḷum aṭiyiliruntu nuṉivarai nāṇkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭatāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ittakaiya tamiḻaka icaik karuvika ḷāṉa ēka tantiri vīṇai paṇṭaik kāla vil vaṭivamāṉa icaik karuviyiṉiṉṟu piṟkālattil tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  mēlum, ekiptiyarkaḷ paṇṭu eṉṟa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantu ekiptil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu taṅkaḷaik kūṟi varukiṟārkaḷ.  paṇṭu nāṭu eṉpatu pāṇṭiya nāṭu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ.1  itaṉ mūlam ekiptiyarkaḷukkum tamiḻarkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu naṉku terikiṟatu.  itaṉāl tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu vil yāḻ ekiptiṟku pōyirukkalām eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
aitarāpāttil uḷḷa ceñcu makkaḷiṭam kiṉṉari eṉṟa icaik karuvi uḷḷatu.  atu mūṉṟu curak kuṭukkaiyaiyum oli parappum palakaiyaiyum iru tantikaḷaiyum uṭaiyatu.  atu tiṟanta mūṅkil nuṉikaḷai uṭaiyatu.  ivvicaik karuvi iru narampukaḷai yuṭaiya yāḻ iṉattait cērntatu.  itaṉai vīṇai eṉpārumuḷar.  itait tuvi tantirikā eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.  ceñcu iṉattavarkaḷ eppoḻutum inta yāḻaiyum villaiyum ampaiyum tāṅkit tirikiṉṟaṉar.  villāl ciṟu paṟavaikaḷai eytu koṉṟu pucippar.  vil taṟkāppiṟkum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭum.  ivarkaḷatu vil yāḻaip pārttāl itu vil yāḻiṉiṉṟu kiḷaitta kiṉṉari yāḻ eṉṟu naṉku teriyum.  kiṉṉari yāḻ vaḷarcci aṭaintu pattu meṭṭu kaḷum mūṉṟu curak kuṭukkaikaḷum peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu pulappaṭum.1
aṟiñar, eyc pāl pōr eṉpavar tam nūlil kuṟiñci nila vēṭarkaḷiṉ villē ellā vitamāṉa tantik karuvikaḷukkum muṉṉōṭiyāka mūlakkaruvāka irukkiṟatu.2  eṉṟu kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.  itu nam karuttai naṉku araṇ ceyvatāka irukkiṟatu.  vil yāḻ piṟkālattil eḻuntatu eṉṟu karutum tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ tam pāṇar kai vaḻiyil kūṟum kūṟṟiṟku nalla maṟuppākat tikaḻkiṟatu.3
mutaṉmutalil oliyai eḻuppum karuvi oṉṟu tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.  ikkaruvi evarāl ekkālattil evvuru vōṭu?  evvāṟu?  epporuḷāl ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉat taṟkālat tavarākiya  nām kūṟutaṟkiyalātu.  eṉiṉum orumūlap poruḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu tuṇipu.  tunti nāmā eṉṟa karuvi yāḻiṟku mūlakkaruviyāka iruttal kūṭum eṉac cila ārāycci yāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.4  tunti nāmāvaik kuṟippiṭuvatu enta ārāycci yāḷar?  ataṟku eṉṉa ātāram?  vilyāḻ ellā narampuk karuvi kaḷukkum aṭippaṭaiyāka  irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇaṟṟa ātāraṅ kaḷuṭaṉ piṟar kūṟum karuttai ivar ēṉ maṟukkavēṇṭum?  eṉpatu eṉakkup pulappaṭavillai.  tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ vilyāḻ mutalil tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatai eppaṭiyum maṟuttuk kūṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa orē eṇṇattiltāṉ intat tavaṟāṉa muṭivaik kaṇṭuḷḷār eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
vilyāḻ, mutalil oru narampōṭu tōṉṟi, appāl aintu narampiṟku vantu, iṟutiyil ēḻuvil iṇaikkappaṭṭu ēḻu narampaip peṟṟatu eṉṟa karuttaip pala icai aṟiñarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  namatu tamiḻk kalaikkaḷañciyattilum intak karuttu ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
villiṉ kaṇṇē kaṭṭappaṭṭa nāṇiṉ nātattaik kēṭ ṭuṇarnta muṉṉōrkaḷ palvēṟu ōcaiyamaiya nāṇ kaṭṭappaṭṭa viṟkaḷai oṉṟāyc cērttu mutaṉmutalil vācitta narampukkaruvi yāḻākum.1
kaṭaiccaṅkak kālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum tōṉṟiya cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ ceyyuṭkaḷilē vil yāḻum cīṟi yāḻum, pēri yāḻum kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyil pēriyāḻum vilyāḻum kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  cilappatikārattilē cakōṭa yāḻum makara yāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivai, maṇimēkalai, cīvakacintāmaṇi peruṅkatai mutaliya vaikaḷilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa vil yāḻai yotta icaik karuvikaḷ mēṉāṭeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu avaikaḷai ārp (Harp), layar (Lyre), lūṭ (Lute) eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar.  itaip paṟṟic ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakap pērāciriyarāy irunta tiru. vi. ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ maṇimalar eṉṉum kaṭṭurait tokutiyil paṇṭait tamiḻar perumai eṉṟa uraiyil, teṉṉāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻar nākarikam urōmāpurikkum kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum paraviyatu.  iṉṉum takkaṇa pīṭapūmiyaic cērnta vēṭṭuvar eṉṟa kuṟiñci makkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅku taṅkaḷ aracai nilaināṭṭit taṅkaḷ peyaraiyum an nāṭṭiṟkuk koṭuttārkaḷ.  atuvē kirīṭ nāṭu (crete).  itaṉ nākarikattiṟkum tamiḻ nākarīkattiṟkum oppuvamai mikutiyāy uḷḷatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  cilappatikārattil vaṉcol yavaṉar vaḷanāṭāṇṭu eṉṟum patiṟṟuppattu iraṇṭām patikattil, nayaṉil vaṉcol yavaṉar piṇittu eṉavum kūṟap paṭuvataik kāṇumpoḻutu cērarkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭu pōntu, aṅkup pōrāṭi, yavaṉa nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyil veṟṟi peṟṟu āṭci naṭatti irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
villarkaḷ kaṇṭatu vilyāḻ
cērarkaḷ villarkaḷ, malaiyāḷikaḷ eṉṟu paṇṭu toṭṭu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.  kuṟiñci nila makkaḷiṉ nērāṉa vaḻiyil vanta malaiyāḷikaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻ, malai yāḻ vil yāḻ eṉṟu tiripuṟṟu iṟutiyil yāḻ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ nilaviyatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ cāṉṟu kāṭṭi uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
vil yāḻiṉ amaippiṉai yoṭṭi ekiptilum papilōṉi lum icaikkaruvikaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu aṅku naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻ ārāycciyil palavita yāḻkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ekiptu nāṭṭiluḷḷa kallaṟaikaḷilum kaltēyā (cālṭiyā) nāṭṭiluḷḷa ūr eṉṉum iṭattiluḷḷa aracu kuṭumpattār kaḷiṉ putaikuḻiyilum iruntu yāḻkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.  intap paḻamperum yāḻkaḷ ki. pi. 2600āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷaṉa.  ivai iṉṟu īrāk nāṭṭiṉ talainakarākiya paktāt nakarat tolporuḷ kāṭcicālaiyil vaikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.1  mēlum ūr nakaril naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻ ārāycciyil kiṭaitta maṟṟoru yāḻiṉ uruvappaṭamum iṅku veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itil pattar nīṇṭa caturappeṭṭi pōl iruk kiṟatu.  iru kōṭukaḷum 7 narampukaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  yāḻiṉ nuṉiyil oru erutiṉ talai ēṟṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  atu poṟṟakaṭṭāl ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ekiptil mīṭṭappeṟṟa vilyāḻ naṅkā eṉappaṭum.  inta yāḻ iṉṟu piriṭṭiṣ tolporuḷ kāṭcicālaiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ataṉ varaipaṭamum iṉṉum pala ekiptil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa paṭaṅkaḷum nūṟkaḷil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟāl vācakarkaḷ yāḻiṉ vaḷarcciyai yum atu veḷināṭṭilum pērum perumaiyum peṟṟa viparamum naṉku aṟiyalām.  ūr nakaril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṟṟoru yāḻ māṭṭiṉ talaivaikkappaṭṭa muntiya yāḻ uruvattiṉiṉṟu muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu.  itu tamiḻaka yāḻiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil naṉku amaintuḷḷatu.  pattar peṭṭi pōl ceyyappeṟṟu, ataṉ naṭuvē kaṉṟiṉ talai oṉṟu poṉṉum maṇiyum iḻaittu aḻakuṟac ceytuvaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  pattariṉiṉṟu mēlē nīṇṭa kōṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itil patiṉoru narampukaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  itu ūr nakariluḷḷa aracarkaḷiṉ kallaṟaikaḷiṉiṉṟu eṭukkappeṟṟatu.  (ki. mu. 26, 27 nūṟṟāṇṭil uḷḷatu) inta yāḻ uruvamum iṅku iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1
vil yāḻiṉ amaippu
4500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cumēriya nāṭṭilum ekiptu nāṭṭilum kiṭaitta palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa yāḻkaḷaip pōṉṟu mokañcatārōvilum aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻakattil ātiyil arumpiya vil yāḻ 6-cāṇ 54 aṅkula nīḷamum 6 viral atāvatu 4½ aṅkulattiṟkuk kuṟaiyāta cuṟṟaḷa vuḷḷa nērāṉa 7-kumiḻaṅ kompukaḷ koṇṭu amaikkappeṟṟatu.2  atōṭu mēṟkuṟitta cuṟṟaḷavuḷḷa 5-cāṇ 45 aṅkulattil 2, iraṇṭu cāṇ 4 viral 21 aṅkula nīḷattil 1, iraṇṭu cāṇ-18 aṅkula nīḷattil 16, oru cāṇ 4  viral 12 aṅkula nīḷattil 2 kompukaḷumāka 21 kompukaḷ koṇṭu amaikkappeṟṟatu.
cuttamāṉa maraliṉ melliya nārai nūlākki narampu pōl tirittu vēṇṭiya parumaṉil ceytu koḷvar.  kampukaḷaik kaṭṭu vataṟkut taṉiyākac caṇaṟkayiṟṟait tirittu vaittukkoḷvar.  āṟu cāṇ-54 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa kompai vil pōl vaḷaittu iru maruṅkiṉum 45 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa kompiṉai vaittu iṇaittu ēṉaiya ciṟu kompukaḷaik kuṟukkāka vaittuk kaṭṭi ēḻu viṟkaḷai iṇaiyavaittu iruppārkaḷ.  7 narampukaḷ kaṭṭi maṟu talaippil muṟukkāṇikaḷai amaittu oṟṟuṟuppum iṇaip pārkaḷ.  muṟukkāṇiyāl narampiṉai melittal valittal ceyyap paṭum.3
tamiḻakattil mutaṉmutalāka eḻunta vil yāḻ viṟkōṭṭi yāḻāka malarntu, vēṟupala yāḻkaḷ eḻavum tuṇaiyāka iruntatu.4   inta vilyāḻiṉ uruvattai namakku mutaṉ mutalāka yāḻ nūliṉ vāyilāka tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.  avar kaḷukkut tamiḻ ulakam eṉṟum naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamaip paṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu yāḻiṉ peyar kaḷaiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭu kiṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl avaikaḷiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa uruvattai evarā lum kāṭṭamuṭiyavillai.  aṇmaiyil tamiḻakattil tirumayatti lum, (tirumeyyattilum) tiruerukkattampuliyūrilum iruntu iru vitamāṉa yāḻiṉ ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu veḷivantuḷḷa pala yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip paṭittu ataṟkēṟpak kaṟpaṉaiyil uruvākkiya yāḻkaḷēyoḻiya acal yāḻ uruvam eṉṟu colvataṟkillai.  kaṭanta pattu āṇṭu kaḷāka namatu nāṭu muḻuvatum yāṉ cuṟṟiyalaintu pala yāḻiṉ ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷaip paṭam piṭittu vaittuḷḷēṉ.  āṉāl avai kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai eṉṉāl kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyavillai.  icai aṟiñarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu avaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai aṟintu viraivil oru nalla uyariya nūl veḷiyiṭa eṇṇiyuḷḷēṉ.  
2. peruṅkalam (pēriyāḻ)
pēriyāḻ eṉṉum peyarai nammil palar kēṭṭē aṟiyōm.  cila tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil intap peyaraip paṭit tiruppārkaḷ.  āṉāl itaṉ uruvattaip pārttavarkaḷ tamiḻakat til aritāka iruppar.
pēriyāḻ piṉṉum makarañ cakōṭamuṭaṉ
cīrpoliyum ceṅkōṭu ceppiṉār-tārpolintu
maṉṉum tirumārp vaṇkūṭaṟ kōmāṉē
piṉṉum uḷavē piṟa
eṉṟu cilappatikāra urai kūṟukiṟatu.  itaṉ mūlam muṟkālattil pēri yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ mutaliya yāḻkaḷ cilappatikāra āciriyar kālattil iruntatāka aṟikiṉṟōm.
yāḻkaḷil peruṅkalam peruñciṟappaip peṟṟa ariya yāḻākum.  muṟkālattil pēriyāḻ, peruṅkalam eṉṟum paravai yāḻ eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  itaṉ kōṭu 12 cāṇum vaṇar 1 cāṇum pattar 12 cāṇum ataṟkēṟpa āṇikaḷum tivavum untiyum peṟṟu āyiram narampu pūṭṭappeṟṟiruntatu.  itu oru vakaip pēriyāḻ.
āyiram narampiṟ ṟātiyā ḻāku
mēṉai yuṟuppu moppaṉa koḷalē
pattara taḷavuṅ kōṭṭi ṉataḷavu
motta veṉpa virumūṉ ṟiraṭṭi
vaṇarcā ṇoḻitteṉa vaittaṉar pulavar
eṉṟum,
talamuta lūḻiyiṟ ṟāṉavar tarukkaṟap
pulamaka ḷāḷar purinarap pāyiram
valipeṟat toṭutta vākkamai pēriyāḻc
celavumuṟai ellāñ ceykaiyiṟ ṟerintu
maṟṟai yāḻuṅ kaṟṟumuṟai piḻaiyāṉ
eṉṟum cilappatikārattil aṭiyārkkunallār uraip pāyirat tuḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉāl pēriyāḻ, kōṭum pattarum vaṇarūm tivavum (vārkkaṭṭum) kōlkaḷum (purinarampukaḷum) untiyum peṟṟirunta teṉa aṟikiṟōm.  inta yāḻ kuyiluvōrkaḷiṉ, vayiṟṟōṭu cērkkappaṭṭum iṭatu tōḷpakkam aṇaikkap peṟṟum kuyilap peṟṟatu.
toṭaiyamai kēṉvi yiṭavayiṟ ṟaḻīi
iṭattōṭ pakkattē aṇaittu
eṉṟu perupāṇāṟṟuppaṭaip kūṟuvataṉāl pēriyāḻai eppaṭi vaittu mīṭṭiṉar eṉṟu pulappaṭum.  peruṅkalam, mutaṟ caṅkak kālattil nilaviyatu.  appāl atu mutal ūḻiyilē aḻintu pōyiṟṟu eṉṟum terikiṟatu.
1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya pēriyāḻ ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu maṟṟō riṭattiṟkut tūkkic cella muṭiyātiruntatu.  mēlum atai vaittukkoḷḷa oru periya maṇṭapam tēvaiyāy iruntatu; kuyilavum payilavum aritāka iruntatu.  eṉavē iyaṟkaiyiṉ kōpattāl mutal ūḻiyil maṟaintu pōṉa peruṅkalam mīṇṭum maṟumalarcci peṟāmal maṟaintu viṭṭatu.
1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya peruṅkalam eṉṟa paravai yāḻ oru poḻutum iruntirukka muṭiyātu; atu aṟiñarkaḷ kaṟpaṉaiyil kaṇṭa oru ceytiyēyoḻiya uṇmaiyāka irātu eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭu nākarika mōkaṅkoṇṭa iṉṟaiya iḷaiñarkaḷ cilar navilkiṉṟaṉar.  itu tavaṟāṉa karuttēyākum.  cumār āyiram narampukaḷai koṇṭa yāḻ pōṉṟa icaikkaruvi ittāli nāṭṭil iṉṟum icaikkappeṟukiṉṟatu.  curamaṇṭalam eṉṉum inta ittāli vāttiyam iṉṟum iruppatai nampum namatu naṇparkaḷ 1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya peruṅkalam iruntatai nampa maṟuppatu ēṉ?  eṉṟu terivillai.  itaiviṭa vintai, iṉṟu ittāliyil 1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya curamaṇṭala vāttiyam irukkalām; muṟkālattil vaṭaintiyāvil 1000-narampukaḷai yuṭaiya nāratap pēriyāḻum iruntirukkalām; āṉāl tamiḻakattil cumār 5000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ 1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya peruṅkalam iruntatu eṉpatai nampamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟum tamiḻ iḷaiñarkaḷ pōkkiṟku eṉ colvatu?
peruṅkalam eṉum ātiyāḻ mutal ūḻiyilē iruntatu.  arakkarkaḷ narampuk karuviyai icaippatil nikaraṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar.  avarkaḷ tarukkiṉai akaṟṟa nallaṟivuṭaiya aṟiñarkaḷ 1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya āti yāḻai amaittaṉar eṉṟu peruṅkatai kūṟukiṟatu.
inta yāḻ 12 cāṇ nīḷamum kōṭu vaḷaiyoḻittu 12 cāṇ nīḷamum uṭaiyaṉavām.  yāḻukkuriya ēṉaiya uṟuppukaḷ mēṟkūṟiya aḷaviṟkēṟpa amaikkappaṭuvaṉa veṉṟum aṭiyārkkunallār eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
curutikaḷiṉ ellai irunūṟu eṉṟum, avaikaḷai meli viṟku melivu, melivu, camaṉ, valivu, valiviṟku valivu eṉṉum aintu tāṉaṅkaḷilum vaikka narampukaḷiṉ tokai 1000 eṉṟum, ittakaiya nuṇṇiya curutikaḷai vācippataṟkup payaṉpaṭum muṟaiyil tamiḻ makkaḷāl ātiyil amaittukkoḷḷappaṭṭa ariya yāḻ karuviyē āyiram narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya ātiyāḻ eṉṟum peruṅkalam eṉṟum aruṭṭiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār tam cīriya yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itai maṟuttuk kūṟu vēṇṭiyatu avaciyamē illai.  atuvē uṇmai eṉa muṟaiyāka icaip payiṟciyuṭaiyār palarum oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
pēriyāḻ
peruṅkalam eṉṉum ātiyāḻ ōriṭattil vaittu icaip pataṟku ēṟṟatēyoḻiya pāṇarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ cella virumpum iṭaṅkaḷukkuk kaiyil tāṅkic celvataṟku ēṟṟatāka illai.  eṉavē piṟkālattil pāṇarkaḷ eḷitil tūkkic celvataṟkum eḷitil ceyvataṟkum eḷimaiyāka icaippataṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu irupattoru narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya pēri yāḻ uruvākkap peṟṟatu.
