intap putiya-kaikku aṭakkamāṉa pēriyāḻaip paṟṟic caṅka nūṟkaḷil kuṟippiṭap peṟṟu, aṟiñarkaḷāl uṇarappeṟum ceytikaḷ pala uḷa.  avai pēriyāḻ periya icaiyiṉai eḻuppa vallatu.  itu icai ilakkaṇa viti tiṟampātu ceyyappeṟṟa ariya yāḻ.  pāṭiṉiyiṉ, pāl pōṉṟa veḷiṟiya kaikaḷil iruntu pain tamiḻp paṇṇai olittu vantatu.  atu māṉ kuḷampu aḻuttiya iṭam pōṉṟu irupuṟamum tāḻntu naṭuvuyarnta pattarai uṭaiyatu.  iḷaiya cūlaiyuṭaiya cenniṟam cērnta cēyiḻaiyār vayiṟṟi luḷḷa aitākiya muṭiyiṉaip pōṉṟu irupuṟamum cērttuk kaṭṭap peṟṟa tōl pōrvaiyaiyuṭaiyatu.  naṇṭiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa tuṟai kaḷiṉ vāyai maṟaittaṟkuriya āṇikaḷ māṭṭap peṟṟatu.  eṭṭām nāḷ iravil eḻum tiṅkaḷ vaṭivil vaṟuvāy eṉṉum uṟuppu ciṟappuṟa amaintirukkum.  taṇṭil aṭuttaṭuttu vārkaḷ (tilavukaḷ) karumai niṟam vāynta kārikaiyar muṉ kaikaḷil ilaṅkum aḻakiya vaḷaiyalkaḷ pōl nekiḻattakkatāy ilaṅkum, tiṉai arici pōṉṟa viralkaḷāl iyakkum kuṟṟamaṟṟa poṉṉiṟam pōṉṟa aḻakiya narampukaḷāl pūṭṭappeṟṟirukkum.  putiya yāḻ aḻaku ceyyappeṟṟa iḷam maṇamakaḷaiyottirukkum eṉṟel lām kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
pēriyāḻiṉ tōṟṟam
itu narampukaḷ muṟukkik kaṭṭappeṟṟu iṉṉoli eḻuppuvatāy amaintirukkum.  vaṉappu vāynta varakuk katiril varicaiyāka varakukaḷ oḻuṅkuṟa amaittu iruppatupōl yāḻiṉ nīḷattil neruṅkiya varicaiyākac ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷ pōṭṭu cuḷḷāṇikaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum.  pattariṉ kuṟukkē tantattāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa yāppup poruttap peṟṟirukkum.  antap pattar piciṉāl oṭṭappeṟṟa poṉṉiṟamāṉa tōl pōrvaiyāl pōrttappaṭṭirukkum.  yāḻiṉ maruppu vaḷaintu arattiṉāl arāvi, nimirntu oḷirum kaḷām paḻattiṉ karumaiyāṉa kaviṉ perum vaṇṇampōl kāṭci aḷikkum eṉṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
peruṅkalattil muṟukkāṇi (māṭakam) amaikkappaṭa villai.  narampukaḷait tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭākiya tivavu avai kaḷai melittal valittal ceyyap payaṉpaṭum.
māyōḷ muṉkai āytoṭi kaṭukkum
kaṇkūṭu irukkai tiṇpiṇit tivaviṉ
eṉṟu porunar āṟṟuppaṭai kūṟukiṟatu.  itaṉ poruḷ kariya niṟattaiyuṭaiya, tāyt teyvamākiya paḻaiyōḷiṉ muṉkaiyil uḷḷa vaḷai pōṉṟu tivavu viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpatākum.
neṭum paṇait tiraṭōḷ maṭantai muṉkaik
kuṟun toṭi yēykkum melintu vīṅku tivavu
eṉṟu perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai kūṟukiṟatu.  neṭiya mūṅkilaip pōṉṟa tiraṇṭa tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya makaḷir muṉkaivaḷai pōṉṟu nekiḻa vēṇṭiya poḻutu nekiḻntum iṟuka vēṇṭiya poḻutu iṟukum vārkkaṭṭu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
paiṅka ṇūkam pāmpupiṭit taṉṉa
aṅkōṭṭuc ceṟinta aviḻntuvīṅku tivaviṉ
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai aḻakuṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu.  itaṉ poruḷ:  paciya kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya karuṅkuraṅku pāmpiṉ talaiyaip piṭittāl appāmpu kaiyaic cuṟṟikkoṇṭu oru kāl iṟukavum oru kāl nekiḻavum ceyvatu pōṉṟa aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya yāḻiṉ taṇṭiṭattē ceṟiyac cuṟṟiṉ nekiḻa vēṇṭiya iṭattu nekiḻntum iṟukavēṇṭiya iṭattum iṟukiyum narampu tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭu eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
itaṉāl tivavukaḷ puri narampukaḷālāṉa tiraṇṭa tiṇmai yāṉa kōlkaḷaik kaṭṭi caṟṟu uṟaḻcciyōṭu kūṭi kōṭṭuṟuppil kāṇappaṭum vārkkaṭṭukaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  māṭakattiluḷḷa āṇikaḷil kaṭṭappeṟṟa paṇmoḻi narampuka ḷallāta tiṇmaiyāṉa puri narampākiya kōlkaḷaik kaṭṭum vārkkaṭṭukaḷ tivavukaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu.  māṭakattil kaṭṭap peṟum narampukaḷ vēṟu; tivavukaḷil kaṭṭappeṟum narampukaḷ vēṟu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
itōṭu tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, malaipaṭukaṭām āciriyar kaḷum uraiyāciriyarkaḷum kūṟuvatiliruntu pēriyāḻ irupat toru narampukaḷaik koṇṭatu eṉṟum, oṉpatu tivavukaḷ uṭaiya oru kaviyum pēriyāḻ eṉṟa peyarāl viḷaṅkiyataiyum aṟiyalām.
mēlum yāḻ narampukaḷ melliyaṉavāka irukkum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ciṟitum kuṟṟamaṟṟavaṉavākavum aḻakākavum irukkum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  itiṉiṉṟu eḻum oli amiḻtu polintu irukkum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
toṭittiri vaṉṉa toṇṭupaṭu tivaviṉ
kaṭippakai yaṉaittuṅ kēḷvi pōkāk
kural orttut toṭutta cukirpuri narampiṉ
aralai tīra urīi varakiṉ
kuralvārn taṉṉa nuṇṭuḷai irīic
cilampamai pattal pacaiyoṭu cērtti
ilaṅkutuḷai ceṟiya āṇi muṭukkip
putuvatu puṉainta veṇkai yāppamaittu
putuvatu pōrtta poṉpōṟ paccai
vatuvai nāṟum vaṇṭukama ḻaimpāl
maṭantai māṇṭa nuṭaṅkeḻi lākattu
aṭaṅkumayir oḻukiya avvāy kaṭuppa
akaṭu cērpu porunti aḷaviṉiṟ ṟiriyātu
kavaṭupaṭak kalaiiya ceṉṟuvāṅ kunti
nuṇaṅkaram nuvaṟiya nuṇṇīr - māmaik
kaḷaṅkaṉi yaṉṉa kataḻntukiḷar uruviṉ
vaṇarntutēntu maruppiṉ vaḷḷuyirp pēriyāḻ					malaipaṭukaṭām.
itil tivavu, narampu,. paccai, unti, maṟuppu mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ pēriyāḻukku iruntaṉa eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.  mēlum, toṇṭupaṭu tivavueṉpataṉāl tivavukku oṉṟāka narampum oṉpatu uḷa eṉṟu peṟappaṭukiṟatu.
innālvakai yāḻiṟkum narampu koḷḷumiṭattup
pēriyāḻiṟku irupattoṉṟu.............
eṉṟum,
oṉṟu mirupatu moṉpatum pattuṭaṉē
niṉṟa patiṉāṉkum piṉṉēḻum - kuṉṟāta
nālvakai yāḻiṟku naṉṉarampu coṉmuṟaiyē
mēlvakai nūlōr viti
eṉṟa cilappatikāra uraikkup pāṭapētam uṇṭu eṉṟum narampukaḷ kūṭiyum kuṟaintum irukkalām eṉṟum vipulāṉantar kūṟukiṉṟār.
pēri yāḻ eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉaic cīvaka cintāmaṇi āciriyar paravai yāḻ eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.  itaṟku vaṟuvāy, kavaik kaṭai, tivavu, taṇṭu (kōṭu), narampiṉ toṭaiyamai kēḷvikōl, pōrvai, pōrvaiyaip pottaluṟu nuṇtuḷai iṟukkum āṇi, pattal muṟukkāṇi, unti, veṇkāyāppu mutaliya ellā uṟuppukaḷum iruntaṉaveṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.
irupattoru narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya pēri yāḻum pattoṉpatu narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya makara yāḻum, patiṉāṉku narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya cakōṭayāḻum, ēḻu narampukaḷai uṭaiya ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum akkālattil tamiḻakattil celvākkup peṟṟiruntaṉaveṉa naṉkaṟikiṟōm.  cilappatikārattiṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷa ceymuṟaik kēḷvi eṉṟa yāḻkkaruviyaiyē cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟa tākap peyarmāṟṟal kuṟittu vipulāṉantar, ariya karuttiṉai yāḻnūlil kūṟiyuḷḷār.
cakōṭa yāḻ
caṅkak kāla yāḻkaḷ nāṉkiṉuḷ cakōṭa yāḻum oṉṟu.  inta yāḻ mutaṟcaṅkak kālantoṭṭu tiruñāṉa campantappiḷḷaiyār kālam varai nilavi iruntatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa.  cilappatikārattil,
īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟaik kēḷvi	(cilap. araṅ. 70)
eṉṟum,
piḻaiyā marapiṉīrēḻ kōvai	(cilap. vēṉiṟ 31)
eṉṟum,
aṇaivuṟak kiṭanta yāḻiṉ ṟokutiyum
īrēḻ cakōṭamu miṭainilaip pālaiyum					(cilap. 10 nāṭu. kaṭṭurai vari 13-14)
eṉṟum cakōṭayāḻ pōṟṟappeṟukiṟatu.  ippāṭalukku urai kaṇṭa aṭiyārkkunallār, mayaṅkā marapiṉaiyuṭaiya ip patiṉāṟ kōvaiyākiya cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu teḷivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭic cakōṭayāḻ cilappatikārak kālattilē ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu eṉpatai nilaināṭṭukiṟār.  itai, tiruvarakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ pāṇar kaivaḻiyil cilappatikārattil cakōṭa yāḻ kuṟippiṭa villai eṉṟu avaciyamiṉṟic cila varikaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷār.
aruṭṭiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūlil pattarum vaḷainta kōṭum piṟa uṟuppukaḷum 14 narampukaḷum uṭaiya cakōṭa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ pala paṭam pōṭṭuk kāṭṭi cakōṭayāḻiṉ ilakkaṇattaiyum atai amaikkum muṟaiyaiyum icaikkum vaḻikaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyatōṭu itai marattāṟ ceytu itaiyum iṉṉum mūṉṟu yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷaiyum ceytu tirukkoḷḷamputūrt tirukkōyilil āḷuṭaiya piḷḷaiyār tiruuruviṉmuṉ kūṭiya pulavar pēravaiyil 5-6-47il yāḻ nūlai araṅkēṟṟumpoḻutu kāṭcikkum vaittuk kāṭṭiṉār.
intac cakōṭa yāḻaic camaippataṟku avar enta ōviyatti ṉiṉṟu cāṉṟu kaṇṭār eṉṟu eṉakkut teriyavillai.  ituvum avaratu kaṟpaṉai yāḻ eṉṟē avaratu nūliṉiṉṟu nāṉ uṇar kiṟēṉ.  avar kaṇṭa cakōṭa yāḻ uruvattaiyum innūlil paṭa māka varaintu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ.
āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷa amarāvati arukil uḷḷa kōli eṉṉum iṭattil cila ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉa.  avai iṉṟum ilaṇṭaṉ tolporuḷ kāṭcicālaiyilum uḷḷaṉa.1  avai ki. mu.  iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭappeṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ivaikaḷil cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivai tirāviṭa ciṟpak karuvūlamāka   aṟiñar kaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaikaḷil yāḻ vācikkum aṇaṅku kaḷuṭaiya aḻakiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ pala uḷa.  ivaṟṟai aṉaittulakak kalai aṟiñar āṉanta kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷ kaṇṭu āyntār.  cilappatikāra kālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum tamiḻ nāṭṭilē paḻamaiyāṉa yāḻkaḷ vaḻaṅkivantaṉa.  paḻamaiyāṉa icai marapu nilavi vantatu.  atai meyppippatupōl amarāvati kōlic ciṟpam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu mutaṉmutalāka ulakiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār aṟiñar āṉantakumāra cuvāmi.
aruṭṭiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūlai yāttataṟku muṉpē, kalai mētai āṉanta kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷ amarāvati yāḻc ciṟpattaik kaṇṭu āyntu ataip paṟṟi ariya oru kaṭṭuraiyait tīṭṭiyuḷḷār.  vipulānanta aṭikaḷ ataik kaṇṭu viyantu yāḻ nūliṟ pārāṭṭi eḻutiyuḷḷār, inta nūṟṟāṇṭil.  eṉavē yāḻ ārāyccikku mutaṉmutalāka aṭippaṭaiyiṭṭavar tiru. āṉanta kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷēyākum.  niṟka, amarāvati ciṟpattil kāṇappaṭum oru yāḻ cakōṭa yāḻ.  atil tivavu, valittal, melittal, ceyyum māṭakam (muṟukkāṇi) pattarilē amaikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.  yāḻ mīṭṭum peṇ uruvam pattarai maṟaittuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu.  māṭakam kāṇappaṭa villai.  kōṭum narampum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar vipulā nantar kūṟiyuḷḷār.  mēlum avar tam nūlil oru varaipaṭamum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  āṉāl nāṉ atai acal uruvattaiyē paṭam piṭittu innūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷēṉ.
intac ciṟpa uruvil yāḻ icaikkum maṅkaiyiṉ uruvattai yum yāḻ uruvattaiyum oppiṭṭu ārāyntāl yāḻiṉ uruvam 42 aṅkulamum (3½ aṭiyum) kōṭṭiṉ uruvamum elip eṉṉum āyta vaṭṭa vaṭivākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  āyta vaṭṭattiṟku iru maiyaṅkaḷ uḷa (itaṉūṭākac cellum nīṇṭa viṭṭam 42 aṅkulam eṉa mēlē kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.) inta vaṭṭatti liruntu nēr kōṇamākac cellum kuṟukku viṭṭam 28 aṅkula nīḷam uḷḷatu.  inta aṭippaṭaiyil āyta vaṭṭam varaintāl yāḻkkōṭu amaintuviṭum.  ciṟpa uruvil kāṇappaṭāta pattarum āyta vaṭṭattil ellaippaṭutti niṟkac ceyyalām.  kuṟukku viṭṭattiṟku 30 pākai cāyvākiya nērkōṭu pattariṉ naṭuvē pōrvait tōl mītu pollam pottappaṭṭa iṭam eṉak koḷḷamuṭiyum.  narampu kaṭṭiyirukkum tōṟṟattaiyum piṟavaṟṟai yum paṭattiṟ pārkkaveṉṟu yāḻnūlil vipulānanta aṭikaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
āṉāl inta amarāvati ciṟpattil talaiyuṭaintu pōyirukkum peṇ oru kaiyāl yāḻai vayiṟṟōṭu taḻuvi ataṉ aṭippakutiyai iṭukkik koṇṭum maṟṟoru kaiyāl ataṉ nuṉip pakutiyaip piṭittuk koṇṭiruppatumāṉa nīṇṭa kompuḷḷa yāḻai evvāṟu cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṉa kuṟippiṭṭār eṉṟu eṉṉāl yūkikka muṭiyavillai.  ivar tam yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa cakōṭa yāḻ vaḷainta kōṭukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu.  ataṟkum amarāvatic ciṟpattiṟkum immi aḷavum oppuvamai kūṟamuṭiyātu.  ātiyil vaḷaintirunta cakōṭa yāḻiṉ kōṭu nimirkkappeṟṟu amarāvati ciṟpattil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyiruntāl oru ciṟitu oṟṟumai irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
aṭikaḷāriṉ intak karuttu, eṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ uḷḷak karuttai kalakkiviṭukiṟatu eṉṟu iṅkuc collit tīravēṇṭiyava ṉāka irukkiṟēṉ.  ātiyil cakōṭa yāḻ vaḷainta kōṭukaḷaip peṟṟiruntatu.  appāl kōṭu nimirkkappeṟṟatu.  yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭuvatu kōṭu nimirkkappeṟāta cakōṭa yāḻ.  amarāvatic ciṟpattil kāṇappaṭuvatu kōṭu nimirkkappeṟṟa piṟkālac cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṟu vipulānanta aṭikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭiruntāl teḷivāka irukkum.
putiya oḷi
iruḷil eṉṉa ceyvatu eṉṟu tayaṅki niṉṟa oruvarkku tiṭīreṉṟu oru putiya oḷi tōṉṟiyatu pōl, mutalāvatu yāḻ ciṟpa uruvam oṉṟu kiṭaittatu.  atu tañcai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tiru erukkattam puliyūril uḷḷa tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar tiru uruvac ciṟpam.  antac ciṟpa uruvil yāḻ vaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.
inta yāḻ vaṭivam cakōṭa yāḻ vaṭivamēyākum eṉṟu eṉ naṇpar palarum aṟutiyiṭṭu uṟuti kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, avar kālattil mīṭṭi vantatum pōṟṟi vantatum cakōṭa yāḻtāṉ eṉpatil eṉakku aiyamuṇṭu.  tirumuṟai varalāṟṟai naṉku ārāynta aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ ārāyccit tuṟai virivuraiyāḷarum vittuvāṉumāṉa tiru. ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉ avarkaḷ paṉṉiru tirumuṟai varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlil tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar tantiri yāḻai mīṭṭiya tākak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
tirunīlakaṇṭayāḻppāṇar piḷḷaiyārait toḻutu tantiri yāḻiṉai vīkki icaiyārāyntu iṟaivarkkukanta pāṇi yiṉai ēḻicai valla mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyāruṭaṉ pāṭi, yāvarum viyantu pōṟṟa yāḻiliṭṭu vācittup pōṟṟiṉār.1 eṉpatākum.
tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar cīkāḻippatikku vantu ñāṉa campantaraik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅki avar virumpiyavāṟē tamatu kaiyi lirunta tantiri yāḻil iṟaivaṉukkukanta pāṭalaip pāṭiyirukkum poḻutu, avar cilaiyil kāṇappaṭuvatu cakōṭa yāḻ eṉa eppaṭik kūṟuvatu eṉṟa aiyam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
āṉāl itē nūlil vēṟoru pakkattil tiru. veḷḷai vāraṇaṉ avarkaḷ,
tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷaiyār kālattavarākiya tiru. nīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, tam maṉaiviyār mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyāruṭaṉ cīkāḻip patikkuc ceṉṟu āḷuṭaiya piḷḷaiyārai vaṇaṅki avar pāṭiyaruḷiya tiruppatikaṅkaḷait tam yāḻil icaittup piḷḷaiyāruṭaṉ tamiḻ nāṭeṅkuñ ceṉṟu, nāḷum iṉṉicait tiṟattāl teyvattaip pōṟṟiṉār eṉpatu varalāṟu.  avar vācitta yāḻk karuvi cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṉum peyaraiyuṭaiya teṉpatu...... eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
ivar mikat teḷivāyk kuṟippiṭuvatiṉiṉṟu cakōṭa yāḻukku, tantiri yāḻ eṉṟa peyar oṉṟu irukkumō eṉṟu cantēkappaṭukiṉṟēṉ.  ēṉeṉil yāḻ nūlil aruṭṭiru vipulā nanta aṭikaḷ cakōṭa yāḻukku viḷakkam kūṟumpoḻutu tirunīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇa nāyaṉār kaiyilirunta itteyvak karuvi muttamiḻ vittakarākiya tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷaiyārālē pārāṭṭappeṟṟatu.  ittakaiya ciṟappuvāynta karuviyiṉ icai yiṉai mīṭṭu morumuṟai tōṟṟumāṟu ceytal tamiḻakattiṉ maṟumalaccikkuc ciṟantatō rētuvākum1  eṉṟu kūṟiyatu cakōṭa yāḻ oli mīṇṭum tamiḻakattil olikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aṭikaḷ aḷapperum āval koṇṭiruntār eṉpatait terivikkiṟatu.
itaṉāl eṉakku ōraḷavu maṉaniṟaivu ēṟpaṭṭatu.  tiru nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar icaittatu cakōṭa yāḻāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ.  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇaraic cakōṭa yāḻt talaivar eṉac cēkkiḻār aṭikaḷ pōṟṟiyatu oṉṟē tiru. nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, piṟanta tiru erukkattampuliyūril uḷḷa avaratu cilaiyil kāṇappaṭum yāḻ uruvam cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṉum muṭipiṟku eṉṉai varac ceytatu.  ataṉai viḷakki vipulānantarum kūṟiyirukkiṟār.  āṉāl iṅkē oṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum.  vipulānanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūl iyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭa piṟakē, pala tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ, yāḻ, yāḻ eṉṟu, kūvi tamiḻarkaḷ icaikkaruvi yāḻē eṉṟum kūṟi, yāḻ paṟṟiya āyvil iṟaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉar eṉpatē maṟaikkātu kūṟavēṇṭiya ceytiyākum.  avarukku muṉṉāl?  kuṭantaiyaic cārnta tārācurattilum tiru nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar cilai uru, cakōṭa yāḻait tāṅki niṟpatu pōl kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  
ivaikaḷaiyellām ārāyntu kāṇpōrkkup paṇṭaiya tamiḻar mutal ūḻiyil uruvākkiya mutuperum yāḻ uruvil cakōṭayāḻ uruvamē takka cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat teriya varum.
eṉatu karuttaiyum aṟiñar veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār karuttai yum araṇceyyum muṟaiyil taṉittamiḻp perum pulavarum kīḻkalait tērcciyāḷarumāṉa uyartiru. ñā. tēvanēyaṉ, em. ē. avarkaḷ,
narampuk karuvikaḷellām paṇṭaik kālattil yāḻ eṉṟē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.  ippōtuḷḷa vīṇai ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻāka allatu ataṉ tiruttamāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  ceṅkōṭu - nērāṉa taṇṭu.  ceṅkōl eṉpatai itaṉoṭu oppunōkkuka.
icait tamiḻ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa piṟkālattil kampar mutaliyōr yāḻum vīṇaiyum vēṟākak karutiṉar.  yāḻ eṉṉum teṉcoṟkup patilāka vīṇai yeṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟateṉka
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1  pala tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ vīṇai eṉṟa patam tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.  ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻiṉ tanti viṇ eṉṟa oliyai eḻuppiyatāl atu viṇ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟu iṟutiyil vīṇai eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu eṉpar.
vīṇai, yāḻ, calam, kiṉṉaram, kōṭāvati, kōṭṭam, taṇṭu, māṭam, vallaki, vipañciyam, tantiri yāḻ eṉṟellām aḻaikkap paṭum.
cīṟiyāḻ
cilappatikāram, pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa nālvakai yāḻkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.  mikap peritāṉa pēri yāḻai mutalil kūṟi mikac ciṟiyatāṉa ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻai iṟutiyil kūṟiyuḷḷatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  nālvakai yāḻkaḷil ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ kōṭu cenniṟamāka iruntatāl itaṟkuc ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu irukkalām eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
pēri yāḻukku irupattoru narampukaḷum makara yāḻukku pattoṉpatu narampukaḷum cakōṭa yāḻukkup patiṉāṉku narampu kaḷum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukku ēḻu narampukaḷum kūṟuvatiliruntē, ēṉaiya yāḻkaḷaiviṭa ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ciṟuyāḻ eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭum.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ciṟiya yāḻāka iruppatāl ciṟuyāḻ eṉṟum cīṟiyāḻ eṉṟum aḻaittu vantaṉar.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ilakkiyac ciṟappu mikka cilappatikārattiṉ puṟañcēriyiṟutta kātaiyil,
cenniṟam purinta ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil	(cilap. puṟam 106)
eṉṟum, naṭukaṟkātaiyil
vaṇarkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ vāṅkupu taḻīi	(cilap. naṭukaṟ - 31)
eṉṟum, puṟanāṉūṟṟil,
kaivaḻi maruṅkiṟ cevvaḻi paṇṇi	(puṟam-149)
eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  kaiyakattu eppoḻutum irukkat takkatākaiyāl kaivaḻi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu.  pāṇarkaḷ kaiyil eṭuttukkoṇṭu ūrūrāyc celvataṟku ēṟṟatāy eḷi tāy iruntatālum pāṇarkaḷ kaiyil eppoḻutum irunta tālum kaivaḻi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu.  iccīṟiyāḻ puṟanāṉūṟṟil
vāṅkiru maruppiṟ ṟīntoṭaic cīṟiyāḻ	(puṟam-285)
eṉṟum,
miṉṉēr paccai miñiṟṟukkuraṟ cīṟiyāḻ 	(puṟam-308)
eṉṟum,
kaival cīṟiyāḻ kaṭaṉaṟin tiyakka	(puṟam-398)
eṉṟum caṅkakkālap pulavarkaḷ palarum cīṟiyāḻaip paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  itupōl piṟa yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭavillai.  itaṉāl cīṟiyāḻ mutaṟ caṅkak kālam mutal aḻiyātu ellōrālum pōṟṟappeṟṟu varuvatum kavaṉikkattakkatu.  itu aḻiyāmalum ēṟṟam peṟṟum iruppataṟku itaṉ ciṟiya uruvamē mukkiyak kāraṇamākum.
inta yāḻāṉatu muṟkālattil pāṇarkaḷ palarālum pōṟṟappeṟṟu vantatu.  inta yāḻē mātavi taṉ pañciṉum melliya pāl pōṉṟa aḻakiya karaṅkaḷil ēnti vācikkappeṟṟa aḻakiya yāḻ.  ituvē civaṉārkku ukantatu eṉṟum irāvaṇaṉ icaittatu eṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṟatu.  āṉāl itaṟku nammāl pōtiya cāṉṟu kāṇamuṭiyavillai.
aruḷtiru. vipulānanta aṭikaḷ cīṟiyāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum orē karuvi eṉpatai ēṟṟukkoḷḷavillai.  mikat tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāy ivviraṇṭum iru vēṟu karuvikaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl tiru. ā. a. varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ mikat teḷivākac cīṟi yāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum orē karuvi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
atōṭu, tiru. vipulānantar cīṟiyāḻukkum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukkum iruvēṟu uruvappaṭaṅkaḷ kāraikkuṭi kumaraṉ āṇṭu malaril tantuḷḷār.  āṉāl inta iraṇṭu uruvap paṭaṅ kaḷaiyum avar etiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭār eṉṟu eṅkum kuṟippiṭa villai.  avar kuṟippiṭum cīṟi yāḻ uruvattai ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ enta ātārattaik kaṇṭu atai ēṟṟukkoṇṭiruk kiṟār?  eṉṟum emmāl aṟiyamuṭiyavillai.  ilakkiyap paṭippai vaittukkoṇṭu tam kaṟpaṉaiyil tiru. vipulānantar kaṇṭa uruvattaiyē tiru. varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉum ēṟṟukkoṇ ṭirukkiṟār eṉṟē karutukiṉṟēṉ.
aruḷā yātalō koṭitē yiruḷvarac
cīṟiyāḻ cevvaḻi paṇṇi yāḻaniṉ
kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka	(puṟam-144)
eṉṟa pā āyviṟkuriyatu.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ pattar, kōṭu, tivavu, oṟṟu, tantirikaram, narampu eṉṉum aṟuvakai uṟuppukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu.  cīṟi yāḻil uḷḷa pōrvait tōl ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil illai.  eṉavē ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil amainta pattariṉ parappu vaṟuvāy ākkap peṟṟu pōrvait tōlāl mūṭappeṟātu vīṇaiyiṉ kuṭam pōṉṟu iruttalvēṇṭum.  cīṟi yāḻil illāta oṟṟu tantirikaram ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil uḷa.  cīṟi yāḻ vaḷainta kōṭuḷḷatu.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ vaḷaiviṉṟi nimirntirukkum.  itaṉāl itu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉum peyaraip peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.  itaṉ oṟṟuṟuppu icai narampukaḷiṉ kīḻ, nel kaṉam iṭaiveḷiyuḷḷatu.  itaṉ mēl narampiṉai aḻuttip piṭittu nīḷattaip pātiyākki melivu narampilē uccameṉappaṭum valivu tōṉṟavum ceyvar.  itaṉai viḷakki, tiru. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉ avarkaḷ kalaikkaḷañciyattil teḷivākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.1  ellām vipulānantar yāḻ nūlaip paṭitta aḷavil kūṟiyavaiyē eṉpatil eṭṭuṇaiyum aiyamiṉṟu.
makara yāḻ
caṅkak kāla yāḻkaḷ nāṉkiṉuḷ makara yāḻum oṉṟu.  inta yāḻ, mutaṟ caṅkak kālantoṭṭu eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai tamiḻakattil iruntu vantatu.  inta nūl vakai yāḻkaḷukkum ellā uṟuppukaḷum kiṭṭattaṭṭa oruppōla iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇap paṭukiṟatu.  āṉāl inta nālvakai yāḻum vevvēṟu eṇkaḷ koṇṭa narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa.
kōṭṭiṉa tamaitiyuṅ koḻuviya vāṇiyum
māṭṭiya pattariṉ vakaiyu māṭakamun
tantiri yamaitiyuñ cāṟṟiya piṟavum
muntiya nūliṉ muṭinta vakaiyē
eṉṟa cilappatikāra uraiyāciriyar kūṟṟāl ēṉaiya yāḻkaḷiṉ uṟuppiṉaip pōlavē makara yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷum atāvatu pōrvai tiṇ kōl, tivavu, āṇi, pattar, maruppu, kōṭu, māṭakam, narampu mutaliya uṟuppukaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟu aṟiñar kaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  makarayāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi - (maṇi 4: paḷik. vari 3-6) eṉṟu maṇimēkalai kūṟukiṟatu.  itaṉāl makarayāḻ maṇimēkalai āciriyar kālam varai tamiḻakattil iruntu vantatu eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.
makara yāḻ 19 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu eṉṟum 17 narampukaḷai yuṭaiyatu eṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu.  inta yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷ aṉaittum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷai ottaṉa eṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.
makara yāḻkaḷaik kuyiluvōr iṭattōṭpakkam aṇaittuk koṇṭu kuyilumpoḻutu itaṉ tivavukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟa kōṭum pattarum varuvāyum makara mīṉiṉ uṟuppukaḷai ottaṉavāy iruppatāl itaṉai makara yāḻ eṉṟaṉar eṉa varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ makara yāḻukkut taṉ kaṟpaṉaiyil oru putiya uruvaṅ kaṟpittuḷḷār.
atōṭu niṟkātu, cilar makara maccam eṉpatu mutalaiyiṉ talaiyaiyum nāyiṉ uṭalaiyum nāṉku kālkaḷaiyum peṟṟa ōr purāṇa kāla vilaṅkeṉṟum, avvilaṅkiṉ tōṟṟattaip pōl ivyāḻum iruntateṉṟuṅ kūṟi, ikkaruttiṟkiṇaṅka ōr cittiramum varaintuḷḷaṉar.  ataiyum iṅku kaṇṭu aṟiñar cīrtūkki aṟintu koḷka eṉṟu pāṇar kaivaḻiyil kuṟippiṭṭu aṟiñar vipulāṉantar kaṇṭa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷukku maṟuppuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
aṟiñar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭṭōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpāl, avarkaḷāl ceyyappeṟṟa makara yāḻ eṉṟu kaṟpaṉai ceytukoṇṭu uruvamaikkum ōviyak kalaiyi lum icaikkalaiyilum vallunarāyirunta yavaṉar aḻakupeṟac ceyta makara yāḻāṉatu avar nāṭṭu makaramīṉ uruvattatāka iruntateṉak koḷḷalām eṉṟu tam yāḻ nūlil kūṟiyuḷḷār.
avar etu kūṟiṉālum kūṟaṭṭum; makara yāḻukku avar kaṇṭa uruvam tamiḻakattilō allatu piṟanāṭṭilō eṅkum kāṇap paṭṭatākak teriyavillai.  yavaṉa nāṭṭilum attakaiya uruvam kāṇappaṭavillai.  airōppāvil uḷḷa enta nāṭṭiṉarō kaṇṭa makara uruvattai maṉattiṟkoṇṭu kaṟpaṉaiyil iru makara yāḻ uruvattai vipulānantar kaṇṭu tam yāḻ nūlil tantuḷḷār.  ivviru uruvaṅkaḷum paṇṭaiya tamiḻar kaṇṭa makara yāḻ uruvam eṉṟu eṉṉāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiyavillai.
tiru. vipulānantar kaṇṭa makara yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷum aṟiñar ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ varainta makara uruvaṅkaḷum avar kaḷiṉ contak kaṟpaṉai uruvaṅkaḷēyākum.  ataṟku evvita ciṟpac cāṉṟukaḷō ōviyac cāṉṟukaḷō illai.
intiyā muḻuvatilum, ēṉ? parmā, cayām, cāvakam, kampōcakam pōṉṟa pala nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa palayāḻ ciṟpap paṭaṅ kaḷaik kaṇṭuḷḷēṉ.  avai etilum ivarkaḷ iruvarkaḷiṉ makara yāḻukkum cāṉṟu illai.
nāratap pēriyāḻ
mutaṟcaṅkak kālattilē vil yāḻum pēriyāḻum cīṟiyāḻum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.  kaṭaic caṅkak kālam varai vēṟu yāḻp peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai.  cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai, cīvakacintā maṇi, peruṅkatai mutaliya nūlkaḷil cakōṭa yāḻum makara yāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cumār 800āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻunta nūlākak karutappaṭum kallāṭattilē pēri yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ maruttuva yāḻ pōṉṟa cila yāḻkaḷiṉ peyar vakaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  300āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ eḻunta pala nāṭaka nūlkaḷil varāḷi yāḻ, vallaki yāḻ, muḷari yāḻ, makati yāḻ, pirakati yāḻ, tīkkucci kaiyāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, kaḷāvati yāḻ, kaccapi yāḻ, kiṉṉari yāḻ, irāvaṇācura yāḻ pōṉṟa pala yāḻkaḷ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ippoḻutu pala yāḻp peyarkaḷum, peyar teriya muṭiyāta pala ciṟpa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
mutal caṅkak kālattil iruntatu peruṅkalam eṉṉum paravaiyāḻ, atu 1000-narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu.  itu mutal ūḻiyil aḻintu paṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  appāl 1000-narampukaḷai yuṭaiya nāratappēri yāḻ eṉṟoru yāḻ talaitūkkiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  itu 32 viral aḷavu akalamum 84 viral aḷavu nīḷamum uṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  kallāṭar kallāṭattil,
vaṭamoḻi vititta vicainūl vaḻakkuṭa
ṉaṭuttaṉa veṇṇāṉ kaṅkuli yakattiṉu
nāṟpatiṟ ṟiraṭṭi nālaṅ kuliyiṉuṅ
kuṟumaiyu neṭumaiyuṅ kōṭal peṟṟatai
yāyiran tantiri niṟaipotu vicittuk
kōṭi muṉṟiṟ kuṟittumaṇi kuyiṟṟi
yirunilaṅ kiṭatti maṉaṅkaraṅ katuva
vāyirat teṭṭi lamaintaṉa piṟappup
piṟavi pētat tuṟaiyatu pōla
vāriyap pataṅko ṇāratap pēriyāḻ
naṉṉarko ḷaṉpā ṉaṉimukam pulampa
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
itaṉ poruḷ vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷiṉ vitippaṭi 32 viral aḷavu akalamum 84 viral aḷavu  nīḷamum uṭaiyatāy kuṟukalum nīṭṭalumāṉa narampukaḷ 1000-kaṭṭappeṟṟatāy mūṉṟu mūlaikaḷi lum irattiṉamaṇikaḷ poṟikkappeṟṟatāy nilamītu kiṭattik kaiviralkaḷāṟṟaṭavi 1008-vita icai olikaḷaip piṟappikkak kūṭiyatāy uḷḷa icaikkaruviyē nāratap pēriyāḻākum. 
ikkaruvi paṇṭaiya peruṅkalam pōl 1000 narampukaḷai yuṭaiyatāy iruntālum uruvattilum vēṟupaṭṭatāyk karutap paṭukiṟatu.  itaṉait tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ, itu mukkōṇa vaṭivāy mūṉṟu mūlaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy taraimītu kiṭattappeṟṟu kuyilappaṭuvatāy uḷḷa karuviyeṉa nām aṟiyumpōtu tāḻipōl puṭaittup parutta kalattaiyum vaḷainta vaṇaraiyum tivavukaḷākac cuṟṟappeṟṟa nīṇṭa kōṭṭiṉaiyum kavaittu uyarntu vaḷainta untiyiṉaiyum uṭaiyatāṉa toṉ ṟamiḻp pēriyāḻiṉ tōṟṟattiṉai ottirātu eṉpataṉai aiyamaṟak kāṇkiṟōm.  toṉṟamiḻp pēriyāḻ nāratap pēriyāḻaip pōl nilamītu kiṭattappaṭṭuk kuyilap peṟātu kuyiluvōriṉ vayiṟṟōṭu cērkkappaṭṭum iṭat tōṭpakkam aṇaikkap peṟṟum kuyilappaṭum karuvi eṉap paṇṭaiya nūlkaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
atōṭu avar kallāṭac ceyyuḷai ātāramāka vaittut tam kaṟpaṉaiyāl oru mukkōṇa nāratap pēriyāḻaiyum varain tuḷḷār.  ataiyum iṅkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm.
inta nāratap pēriyāḻiṉ cariyāṉa uruvam etuvum iṉṟu kiṭaikkavillai.  nāratap pēriyāḻum namatu paṇṭaiya peruṅ kalattai yottup peritāka ellā makkaḷum payaṉpaṭutta muṭiyā tiruntatāl aḻintu pōyiṟṟu.
nāratap pēriyāḻ mukkōṇa vaṭivāy mūṉṟu mūlaikaḷai yuṭaiyatu.  itu mantara, mattiya, tāram eṉṉum mūṉṟu nilaikaḷil curam paṭippaṭiyāy mēlē cellaccellat tīviramākiṟateṉṟum narampukaḷ kīḻē varavarak kuṟukip pōkiṟateṉṟum aṟiyak kiṭak kiṉṟatu.  mantira nilaiyil uḷḷa narampukaḷ nīḷamākavum kaṉamākavum irukkum.  atōṭu oliyum mantamāka eḻum.  narampukaḷ nīḷam kuṟaintu kaṉamum kuṟaintu iruppatāl pōkap pōka oliyum tīviramākiṟatu eṉṟu icai aṟiñarkaḷ eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ.
maruttuva yāḻ
maruttuva yāḻ tiruttam peṟṟatu; cevviya uruvam peṟṟatu; iṉiya olikaḷai eḻuppuvatu.  itu nāratap pēriyāḻaiyum kīcaka yāḻaiyum viṭac ciṟiyatu.  itaṉai aṟiñarkaḷ cīriya yāḻ eṉṟum teyva yāḻ eṉṟum aḻaippar.  itu nalla vaṭṭamāṉa vaṭivuṭai yatāy irukkum.  pattar muyal tōliṉāl mūṭap peṟṟirukkum.  naṭuvē oṟṟai narampu iṭakkaiyāṟ kaṭṭi iṭap pakkam mārc caṉaiyai 26 viral aḷavuḷḷatāy pattariṉ mēl vaittuk koṇṭu valakkaiyiṉ mūṉṟu puṟa viralkaḷālum, nuṉi viralālum varuṭi iyakkappaṭum.  itaṉkaṇ tūṅkal ōcai, tuḷḷal ōcai eṉṉum icai vikaṟpaṅkaḷait taḻuvi 62 vakai icai tōṉṟum.  itu 80 viral nīḷam uḷḷatu.  itaṉaik kallāṭaṉār tam ariya nūlākiya kallāṭattil,
niṟaimati vaṭṭattu muyaluri vicittu
nāppa ṇoṟṟai narampu kaṭippamait
tannaram pirupat tāṟaṅ kulipeṟa
viṭakkaran tuvakki yiṭakkī ḻamaittup
puṟaviraṉ mūṉṟi ṉuṉivira lakattu
maṟupat tiraṇṭicai yaṉaittuyir vaṇaṅku
maruttuvap peyarpeṟum vāṉak karuvi
tūṅkalun tuḷḷalun tulakki niṉṟicaippa
nāṉmukaṉ mutalā mūvarum pōṟṟa
muṉivarañ caliyuṭaṉ mukama ṉiyampat
tēvarka ḷaṉaivarun ticaiticai yiṟaiñca
eṉa, mika aḻakāka maruttuva yāḻiṉ māṇpai aḻuttam tiruttamāka eḻutiyuḷḷār.
1008 narampukaḷ uḷḷa nāratap pēri yāḻum 100 narampukaḷuḷḷa kīcaka yāḻum icaippataṟkum eṭuttuc celvataṟkum ēṟṟaṉavāka illātiruppatai eṇṇip piṟkālattē ciṟiya yāḻāka maruttuva yāḻ amaikkap peṟṟatu pōlum.
makkaḷiṭam puttuṇarcciyum putu vāḻvum eḻut toṭaṅkiya kālattil putiya yāḻum eḻuntatu.  iṟaivaṉ taṉmaiyai iṉṉoli yāl makkaḷukku uṇartta muṉvanta camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ, kālattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟum vacatikkukantavāṟum icaippataṟkut takka vaḻiyilum kuṟainta celavil niṟainta ciṟappuṭaiya ciṟu yāḻai uruvākkiṉar, eṉavum kūṟalām.
kīcaka yāḻ
kīcaka yāḻ, pēriyāḻukkup piṉ eḻunta cīriya yāḻ, āṉāl itu pēriyāḻiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilē amaikkappeṟṟa periya yāḻākum.  yāḻ vakaiyil itu peruṅkalam, nāratap pēriyāḻ ākiyavaikaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāy amaikkappeṟṟa periya yāḻākum.  itu 80 viral nīḷamum kaṉamāṉa uṭal amaippum uṭaiyatu.  nāratap pēriyāḻiṉ aṭiyoṟṟi 100 narampukaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatu itaṉuṭaiya periya uṭal amaippē itu kāla veḷḷattil nīnti vara muṭiyāmal kāṭēṟak kāraṇam eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉaik kallāṭar,
......J‹Å niṉṟicaippa
veḻuveṉa vuṭampupeṟ ṟeṇpataṅ kuliyiṉ
ṟantiri nūṟu taḻaṅkiya mukatta
kīcakap pēriyāḻ kiḷaiyuṭaṉ murala
eṉat tam cīriya nūlil teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tumpuru yāḻ
tumpuru yāḻ, nāratap pēriyāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ ākiyavaikaḷai viṭac ciṟiyatu; aḻakiyatu; icaippataṟku ēṟṟatu.  eṭuttuc celvataṟku eḷitāṉatu.  itu 12-viral cuṟṟaḷavum 94-viral nīḷamum uṭaiyatu.  9-narampukaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatu.  tōḷilum, kālilum tāṅka vaittu icaikkappaṭum.  itaṉai,
munnāṉ kaṅkuli muḻuvuṭaṟ cuṟṟu
maimpatiṟ ṟiraṭṭi yāṟuṭaṉ kaḻitta
vaṅkuli neṭumaiyu mamaittuṭ ṭūrntē
yoṉpatu tantiri yuṟuttinilai nīkki
yaṟuvāyk kāyiraṇ ṭaṇaitturai kaṭṭit
tōḷkāl vatintu toḻiṟpaṭat tōṉṟun
tumpuruk karuviyun tuṉṉiniṉ ṟicaippa
eṉṟu kallāṭam, tumpuru yāḻiṉ amaippai eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kiṟatu.  mēlum uraiyāciriyar itaṟku oṉpatu narampukaḷ kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum eṉṟum avaṟṟuḷ curuti narampu taṉittiruppa ēṉaiya eṭṭu narampukaḷum iraṇṭiraṇṭāka iṇaittuk kaṭṭap paṭṭirukkum eṉṟum itaṉai iyakkuṅkāl patumācāṉat tiliruntu toṭaiyilum tōḷilum porunta vaittu iyakkap peṟum eṉṟum viḷakkam tantuḷḷār.  ivviḷakkam mikavum ciṟappuṭaiyatākum.

9. yāḻiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcci
tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ icai aṟiviṟkēṟpa yāḻum vaḷarcci peṟṟu vantatu.  paṇṭaiya tamiḻarkaṇṭa paṇkaḷum yāḻkaḷum viṇ muṭṭa vaḷarntu uyarntaṉa.  paṇ kēḷvi eṉpavai icai, pālai (mūrccaṉai) eṉum iraṇṭiṉ cērkkaiyil pallāyiramākap perukiṉa.  ātiyil oru narampōṭu uruppeṟṟa vilyāḻ paṭippaṭi yāy 5 narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya vilyāḻāka arumpip piṉ 7 narampu kaḷaiyuṭaiya viṟkōṭṭiyāḻāka malarntatu.  nāḷaṭaivil yāḻ vakaikaḷ perukiṉa.  narampukaḷiṉ eṇkaḷ atikappaṭṭaṉa.  uruvum tiruvam māṟivantaṉa.  oru narampuṭaiya ātivil yāḻ iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, aintu, ēḻu, oṉpatu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉāṟu, irupattoṉṟu, nūṟu, āyiram eṉa eṇṇaṟṟa narampukaḷaip peṟṟatu.  palvēṟu peyarkaḷaiyum peṟṟu uyarntu peruki vaḷarntatu.
muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyār, peruṅkuṉṟūrk kiḻār, peruṅ kavucikaṉār, nallantuvaṉār, tiruvaḷḷuvar, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa pēraṟiñarkaḷ ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa varkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ kālattiṟku muṉpē yāḻ vevvēṟu peyarkaḷil narampukaḷiṉ eṇkaḷil māṟṟam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu.  āṉāl ataṉ potu amaippil atika māṟṟam etuvum eḻavillai.  atu vaḷaintu aḻakaṟṟatāy irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇappeṟṟālum atai evarum periya māṟṟam ceyyamuṉvaravillai.  kāraṇam karuviyai viṭa atil eḻuppappaṭum icai muṟaiyē avarkaḷukku mutaṉmaiyāka iruntatu eṉalām.
civaṉeṟi pēṇiya appar perumāṉ kālattilō allatu avaratu kālattiṟkuc caṟṟu muṉṉarō tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ mikat tuṇivāka muṉvantu yāḻ uruvattilum ataṉ amaippilum perum māṟṟam ceytuḷḷaṉar eṉavum kūṟalām.
patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ yāḻiṟku vēṟu peyarkaḷ etuvum taḻaittatākat teriyavillai.  appāl yāḻiṟkum vīṇai eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu.  yāḻai vīṇai eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum kālattil atāvatu ārampak kālattil yāḻ uruvattil māṟṟam peṟavillai; ātalāl akkaruviyil vaḷainta kōṭukaḷ māṟṟappeṟavillai.  āṉāl peyaraḷavil oru māṟṟam eḻuntatu pōlum.
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa cīvaka cintāmaṇiyaip paṭaitta pulavar tiruttakka tēvar; ivaratu kālam ki. pi. 8m nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  ivaratu cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil,
viruntāka yāḻ paṇṇi vīṇaitāṉ tōṟpāṉ	(cin. 730)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  itaṟku uraiyāciriyar mutalil iṉimai peṟa yāḻai icaippittup piṉpu taṉ miṭaṟṟāṉum yāḻālum pāṭiṉāḷ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
akkālattil vīṇai eṉṟa iṉṟaiya icaikkaruvi arumpa villai.  āṉāl yāḻum miṭaṟum uṭaṉnikaḻnta icai vīṇai eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉa eṇṇa iṭantarukiṟatu.  mēlum uraiyāciriyar nacciṉākkiṉiyār,
vīṇai eṉṟa yāḻaiyum pāṭṭaiyum	(cin. 730)
eṉṟu teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatāl yāḻum miṭaṟum uṭaṉ nikaḻnta icaikkē vīṇai eṉa akkālattil oru peyar uraikkap peṟṟatu eṉṟu aiyamaṟat terikiṟatu.  atōṭu avar vīṇai eṉṟa patattiṟkut tāḷattōṭu kaṇṭattilum karuviyiṉum piṟakkum pāṭṭu eṉṟu cintāmaṇiyiṉ 411ām ceyyuḷ uraiyuḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
veḷḷilai vēṟkaṇāṉaic cīvakaṉ vīṉaiveṉṟāṉ 					(cin. 741)
eṉṟa cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷukku uraiyāciriyar cīvakaṉ tattaiyai yāḻum, pāṭṭum veṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
cīvaka cintāmaṇi eṉum kāppiyam eḻunta kālattil vīṇai eṉpataṟku tāḷan tavaṟātu kaṇṭattālum yāḻālum piṟakkum pāṭṭum eṉṟē poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.  cīvaka cintāmaṇi āciriyar vīṇai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu iṉṟuḷḷa vīṇaiyāka iruntāl nacciṉārkkiṉiyār tam uraiyuḷ ataṉai uṟutiyākak kuṟippiṭṭiruppār.  avar kuṟippiṭātu viṭṭiruntālum in nūlai mutalmutalil nūl vaṭivil acciṭṭa tiru. u. cāmināta aiyar ataṉaik kuṟippiṭātu viṭṭirukkamāṭṭār.
cilappatikāram eṉṉum kāppiya nūlai eḻutiya iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ kālam ki. pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  akkālattil vaḻakkil iruntuvarum vīṇai karukkuḻiyil uruppeṟuvataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa kālam.  akkālattil vīṇai eṉṟa patam uruppeṟṟu viṭṭatu.  iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum yāḻiṟku vīṇai eṉṟa patattai upayōkittu vantuḷḷār cilappatikārattil,
nāṭakam uruppaci nalkā ḷāki
maṅkalam iḻappa vīṇai maṇmicait
taṅkuka ivaḷeṉac cāpam peṟṟa	(cin. kaṭalōṭu 6:21:23)
eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itaṟku uraiyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār vīṇai eṉpataṟku ippōtuḷḷa vīṇaiyaik kuṟip piṭātu uṭal eṉṟē poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷār.  virivuraiyil nārataṉ itaṉai aṟivittal karutit taṉatu yāḻiṟkup pakai narampu pāṭiṉaṉ.  ivaṟṟai ellām uṇarnta kuṟu muṉivaṉ nārataṉatu vīṇai maṅkalamiḻappa maṇṇilē maṇaiyāyk kiṭakkumāṟum uruppaci puviyilē piṟakkumāṟum cayantaṉ pūmiyil mūṅkilāyt tōṟṟumāṟum capittāṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
nārataṉ vīṇaiyaik kūṭa yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  makkaḷ kuralai (kaṇṭattai) cārīra vīṇai, carīra vīṇai, allatu kātra vīṇai eṉṟum piṟkālattil kuṟippiṭu vataik kaṇṭum cintikkalām.
yāḻum, kuḻalum cīṟum miṭarum	(cilap. araṅ 3: 26)
eṉṟac cilappatikārac ceyyuḷukku uraiyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār miṭaṟu, miṭaṟṟup pāṭal; itaṉaic cārīra vīṇai eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  miṭaṟākiya cārīrattiṟku vīṇai eṉṟu ak kālattil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatākavum terikiṟatu aṉṟō!
iḵtaṉṟic cīvakacintāmaṇiyil toṭṭiṉa muṭaiya vīṇaic ceviccuvai amirtam (cin. 204) eṉṟu varum ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷukku, kāmaṉ pāṭiṉa, oṟṟumaiyuṭaiya cārīra vīṇaiyākiya, cevikku iṉimai tarum amirtattai eṉṟu urai kūṟi miṭaṟṟiṉai vīṇai eṉṟu valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṟatu.
caṅkak kālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil peritum yāḻ eṉṟa patamē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appāl ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟ ṟāṇṭu mutal vīṇai eṉṟa peyar miṭaṟṟuppāṭalukkum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. piṉṉar yāḻukkum vīṇai eṉṟa peyar payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉat terikiṟatu.
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkum maṇi mēkalaiyil,
takarak kuḻalāl taṉṉōṭu mayaṅki
makarayāḻiṉ vāṉ kōṭu taḻīi	(maṇi. 4.55:56)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itil kuṟippiṭṭa makarayāḻ eṉṟa patattiṟku uraiyāciriyarkaḷ makara vīṇai eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟi cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil,
tīntoṭai makara vīṇait teḷviḷi
yeṭuppit tēṟṟi	(cin. 608)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷil varum makara vīṇaikku uraiyāciriyarkaḷ makara yāḻ eṉṟē poruḷ kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  maṇimēkalaiyil maṟṟōriṭattu,
makara vīṇaiyiṉ kiḷai narampu vaṭitta 	(maṇi 19:26)
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṟku uraiyāciriyar makara yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār .mēlum  makaravīṇai eṉṟu oru vīṇai eṅkum eppōtum iruntatillai.  makarayāḻē makaravīṇai eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu.  koṅku vēḷir iyaṟṟiya peruṅ kataiyil pala iṭaṅkaḷil yāḻ kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu.
yāṇar kūṭṭat tiyavaṉak kaiviṉai
māṇap puṇarntatōr makara vīṇai	(peruṅ 3: 15: 22-23)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷil kuṟippiṭum makara vīṇai eṉṟa patattiṟku uraiyāciriyar makarayāḻ eṉṟē urai eḻutiyuḷḷār.
`teyvap pēriyāḻ kaivayiṟ ṟirīi	(peruṅ 1: 48: 104)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷ aṭikku tiru. u. vē. cāminātaiyar teyvap pēriyāḻ - kōṭavati; iḵtu utayaṇaṇuṭaiya vīṇaiyiṉ peyar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār (peruṅ. pak 251).  maṟṟōriṭattil tīn toṭaip pēriyāḻ tivavoṭu koḻīi (peruṅ. 1-52: 85) eṉṟa aṭikkup pēriyāḻ eṉpatu iṉiya narampiṉaiyuṭaiya kōṭavati yeṉṉum periya yāḻ; eḻuvāy; tīn toṭai makara vīṇai `(civa. 608) eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār.
peruṅ katai vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu koṅku vēḷir eṉṉum kuṟunila maṉṉarāl tamiḻākkam ceyyappeṟṟatu.  innūl irāyāmaṇam pōl vaṭa moḻiyiṉiṉṟu moḻiyākkam ceyyap peṟṟatu eṉṟālum kampa irāmāyaṇam pōl ciṟappuṭaiyatu.  kālattāl mikap piṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉṟu karutappaṭṭālum cintā maṇi, cilaptikāram, maṇimēkalai pōṉṟa peruṅ kāppiyaṅ kaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāy vaittu aṟiñarkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭuvatu.
innūliṉ mutaṉūlāka viḷaṅkiya pirukatcamkitai eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi nūlil kāṇappaṭum vīṇaiyait tamiḻākkam ceyta koṅkuvēḷir eṉṉum tamiḻ aṟiñar, vīṇai eṉṟu  kuṟippiṭātu, yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itu, yāḻ iṭaikkālattiltāṉ vīṇai eṉṉum peyaraip peṟṟiruntatu; ātiyil yāḻiṟku vīṇai eṉṟa peyar iruntatu eṉpataiyum niṉaippūṭṭuvatāka irukkiṟatu.  innūl, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, aṭiyārkku nallār, mayilai nātar, nēmināta uraiyāciriyar, yāpperuṅkala virutti yuraiyāciriyar, vīracōḻiya uraiyāciriyar, takkayākap paraṇi uraiyāciriyar, ilakkaṇa viḷakka uraiyāciriyar mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ uraikaḷil innūl eṭuttāḷap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itaṉ perumaiyai yum itaṉ kālattaiyum aṟiyat tuṇai ceyyum.
teyvan taḻuviya cīriya nalyāḻ mutaṟ caṅkak kālattiṟku muṉpē makkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattil vēṭṭuva vāḻkkai naṭattiya tol paḻaṅkālattilē tōṉṟiyatu.  atu ātiyil vil yāḻāka arumpiyatu eṉpatē ataṉ toṉmaiyai uṇarttap pōtiya cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
vilyāḻ, viṟkōṭṭiyāḻākavum, pēriyāḻ ākavum, cakōṭa yāḻākavum, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻākavum makara yāḻākavum vaḷarcci peṟṟatu eṉṟālum atu, ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai ataṉ kōṭu māṟṟam peṟavillai.  atu oru narampuṭaṉ tōṉṟi āyiram narampukaḷ uṭaiyatāka vaḷarntatu.  atu vil vaṭivamāka ātiyil arumpip piṉ pattaraiyum vaḷainta kōṭṭaiyum peṟṟatu.  appāl atu makara mīṉiṉ uruvattaiyum peṟṟatu eṉṟālum atu tamiḻ icai peṟṟa vaḷarccikkoppa ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkavillai.  atu miḷiṟṟum paṇ tēṉākat tittikkum ceṅkarumpāy amutāy viḷaṅkiṉālum ataṉ vaṭivam aḻakuṭaiyatāy illai.  itaṉai maṉattiṟ koṇṭa vaḷḷuvar,
kaṇaikoṭitu yāḻkōṭu cevvitu āṅkuaṉṉa
viṉaippaṭu pālāl koḷal.	(tiruk. 279)
eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.  itaṉ poruḷ kaṇai kaviṉuṟak kāṇappaṭṭā lum ceyalāṟ koṭiyatu.  yāḻ kōṭāl vaḷaintu aḻakaṟṟa tāyk kāṇappaṭiṉum ceyalāl cevviyatu.  avvāṟē tavam cey vōraiyum koṭiyar cevviyar eṉpatu vaṭivāl koḷḷātu avar ceyalpaṭṭak kūṟṟāṉē aṟintu koḷka eṉpatākum.  itaṉai muṉ attiyāyattilum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm.
tamiḻ icai ātikālattil kuḻantaiyiṉ maḻalai moḻi pōl tōṉṟip piṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyac ceṟivu  mikkatāy kallaiyum urukkum āṟṟal vāyntatāy iṟaivaṉ kōpattaiyum taṇikkum taṉmai vāyntatāy ēṉ?  iṟaivaṉiṉ vaṭivāy iṉimaiyāy eḷitāy, amutāy, cuvaiyāy, cuvaiyiṉ payaṉāyp polintatu.  ataṟkēṟpa yāḻ vaḷarcci peṟavillai pōlum.
yāḻ vakai
ātiyil vil yāḻtāṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  appāl peruṅkalam eṉṉum ātiyāḻ tōṉṟiyatu.  itu mutaṟcaṅkak kālattil eḻuntu mutal ūḻiyilē maṟaintu pōyiṟṟu.
kaṭaic caṅka kālattilē pēriyāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ pōṉṟa yāḻkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  inta yāḻkaḷ cilappatikārattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  itaṉaic cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟuk kātaiyiṉ urai meyppikkiṟatu.  inta yāḻkaḷ nāṉkum nāṉku vitamāka ēṟṟattāḻvuḷḷa eṇkaḷuṭaiya narampu kaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa.
piṟkālattil pala yāḻ vakaikaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kallāṭaṉār, nārata yāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, maruttuva yāḻ, makara yāḻ, kaccapi yāḻ mutaliyaṉa iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.  19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil 
tiru. kaṅkaimuttup piḷḷai avarkaḷ tīkkuccikai yāḻ, varāḷi yāḻ, vallaki yāḻ, vilyāḻ mutaliya cila putiya yāḻ vakaikaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
yāḻiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭa mūtaṟiñarkaḷ yāḻiṉ toḻil kaḷai viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:-
(1) paṇṇal (2) parivaṭṭaṇai (3) ārāytal (4) kaivaral 
(5) celavu (6) viḷaiyāṭṭu (7) kaiyūḻ (8) kuṟum pōkku.
ivaikaḷai viḷakki aṟiñarkaḷ tam nūṟkaḷil naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
(1)	paṇṇal - pāṭa niṉaitta paṇṇukku iṇai, kiḷai, pakai, naṭpu narampukaḷ peyarum taṉmai, māttirai aṟintu vīkkutal.
(2)	parivaṭṭaṇai - avvāṟu vīkkiṉa narampai aka viralālum karaṇañ ceytu taṭavip pārttal.
(3)	ārāytal - arōkaṇa avarōkaṇa vakaiyāl icaiyait terital.
(4)	taivaral - curuti ēṟṟutal
(5)	celavu - āḷattiyilē nirampap pāṭutal
(6)	viḷaiyāṭṭu - pāṭa niṉaitta vaṇṇattil cantattai viṭuttal
(7)	kaiyūḻ - vaṇṇattil ceyta pāṭalkaḷ ellām iṉpa mākap pāṭal
(8)	kuṟumpōkku - kuṭakac celavum tuḷḷaṟ celavum pāṭutal
iḵtaṉṟi, vārttal, vaṭittal, untal, uṟaḻtal, uruṭṭal mutaliyaṉavum uḷa.
vārttal - cuṭṭu viralāl toḻil ceytal
vaṭittal - cuṭṭu viralum peru viralum kūṭṭi narampai akamum puṟamum ārāytal
untal - narampukaḷait teṟittu valiviṟ paṭṭatum meliviṟ paṭṭatum niral paṭṭatum aṟital
uṟaḻtal - oṉṟiṭaiyiṭṭum iraṇṭiṭaiyiṭṭum narampu kaḷait teṟittal.
uruṭṭal - iṭak kaic cuṭṭu viral uruṭṭalum valak kaic cuṭṭu viral uruṭṭalum cuṭṭōṭu peruviraṟ kūṭṭi uruṭṭalum iru peru viralum iyaintu uṭaṉ uruṭṭalum eṉap pala.
yāḻiṉ irukkai oṉpatu vakaippaṭum. atāvatu (1) patumam (2) uṟkaṭitam (3) oppaṭi irukkai (4) campuṭam (5) cuyamukam 
(6) cuvattikam (7) taṉippuṭam (8) maṇṭilam (9) rakapātam eṉpaṉa.  nāṭaka nūlōr yāḻiṉ irukkaiyai aimpatu eṉpar.
yāḻiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pala vakaippaṭum. avaiyāvatu 
(1) cempakai (2) ārppu (3) atirvu (4) kūṭam eṉappaṭum.
(1) cempakai - paṇṇōṭuḷarā
(2) ārppu - ōṅka icaittal
(3) atirvu - narampaic citaṟa untal
(4) kūṭam - taṉ pakaiyākiya āṟa narampiṉ icaiyiṟ kuṉṟit taṉṉōcai maḻuṅkal.
yāḻiṟkup pala vaḻikaḷiṟ kuṟṟam uṇṭākum.  yāḻiṉ maram nīrilē niṟṟal, aḻukutal, vētal, nila mayakkup pārilē niṟṟal, iṭi vīḻntiruttal, nōy, marappāl paṭal mutaliyavām.
muṟkālattil vāḻnta tamiḻ nāṭṭu icai vallunarkaḷ kuḻalaiyum yāḻaiyum tuṇaiyākak koṇṭē kural mutaliya ēḻicaikaḷaiyum kuṟṟamaṟa āyntu perum paṇkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻip piṟakkum tiṟaṅkaḷumākiya icai nuṭpaṅkaḷai iṉimaiyuṟa icaittuk kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ.  kuḻalum yāḻum paṇṭaiya tamiḻ makka ḷukku iṉpan tantaṉa; avarkaḷ cevikku uṇavāy iruntaṉa; avar kaḷukku amutāka viruntaḷittaṉa.  ivaikaḷai īṭilāta iṉpan tarum iṉṉicait tiṟaṅkaḷait tōṟṟuvikka valla ciṟappamaintaṉa eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar pōṉṟa aṟavōrkaḷum pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
yāḻiṉ maṟumalarcci
paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ uruvākkiya tīntamiḻ yāḻ patiṉāyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāy ataṉ uruvilum tiruvilum māṟṟamiṉṟi viḷaṅkiyatu.  makkaḷ nākarikam uyarntatum yāḻ uruvamum māṟṟam uṟṟatu.  icai vallunarkaḷ ēḻicaiyai yāḻil iṉimaiyuṟa icaikkavum kamakam mutaliyavaikaḷai mīṭṭavum, yāḻ eḻil peṟṟilaṅkavum yāḻil oru perum māṟṟam ceytaṉar.  māṟṟa muṟṟa yāḻiṟkuk kōṭu nimirntu cemmaiyuṟṟatāl ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṟaṉar.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻē piṉ vīṇaiyeṉap peyar ēṟṟu maṟumalarcciyuṟṟatu.  yāḻiṉ kōṭu oṉṟutāṉ nimirntatē yoḻiya ataṉ uṟuppu oṉṟum māṟṟam peṟavillai.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ yāḻiṉ maṟumalarcci eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, yāḻ vaḷarcciyil oru tiruppam eṉṟu icaikkaruvikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
yāḻ, ātiyil villil iruntu piṟantatu.  atu mutalil arai vaṭṭavaṭivil arumpiyatu.  ataṉ uṇmai arappā, mokañca tārō muttiraikaḷil naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  arappā muttirai kaḷil kāṇappaṭum yāḻ uruvam arai vaṭṭa vaṭivamākavē irukkiṟatu.  atuvē namakku 5000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ irunta yāḻ vaṭiviṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka miḷirkiṟatu.  ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka aruṭṭiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷār tam yāḻ nūlil naṉku tīṭṭikkāṭṭiya vil yāḻ vaṭivam atai iṉṉum meyp pippatāka irukkiṟatu.  ivaikaḷil 7 nāṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (paṭam 2, 3, 4 aip pārkka)
ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāṉa māṟṟam mokañcatārō nākarikattil kaṇṭa muttiraikaḷil kaṇṭa mukkōṇa vaṭivamāṉa yāḻ uruvamākum.  itu arai vaṭṭamāṉa yāḻ vaṭṭattil eḻunta māṟṟattaik kāṭṭuvatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  (paṭam 5-aip pārkka). itē mukkōṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivattil uḷḷa yāḻ ekiptiya ciṟpaṅkaḷilum cumēriya ciṟpaṅkaḷilum naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.  itil 10 nāṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nāṇkaḷai oru kutirai taṉ kālāl mīṭṭuvatu pōl ciṟpam tīṭṭappeṟṟirukkiṟatu 
(paṭam 6). ivvariya yāḻ ki. mu. 4000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa ciṟpa uruvil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi ki. mu. 3000ām āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa yāḻ uruvam oṉṟu kaltēyā (chaldea) nāṭṭil uḷḷa araca kuṭumpattārkaḷiṉ kallaṟai oṉṟiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itu aḻakākap palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  atiluḷḷa erutut talai poṟṟakaṭṭāl potiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  inta yāḻ vaṭivam iṉṟum īrāk nāṭṭil uḷḷa tolporuḷ kāṭcicālaiyil uḷḷatu.  itu arai vaṭṭavaṭiva yāḻiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu māṟutal peṟṟaḷḷatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  mēlum itu mukkōṇa vaṭivam vāyntatu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyā viṭṭālum nuṉi mukkōṇa vaṭivamāka amaikkap peṟṟu nērāka nimirttivaikkap peṟṟa icaikkaruvi eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. (paṭam 8, 9 aip pārkka)
mokañcatārōvilum kaltēyāvilum kāṇappaṭṭa yāḻkaḷ iru vēṟu aṭippaṭaikaḷaik koṇṭaṉavāy amain tirukkiṉṟaṉa.  inta iruvita aṭippaṭaikaḷum tamiḻaka yāḻkaḷiṉ mūla amaippait taḻuviyaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  oṉṟu arai vaṭṭamāṉa aṭippaṭaiyiliruntu vaḷarcci aṭaintatu.  maṟṟoṉṟu mukkōṇa vaṭiva aṭippaṭaiyiliruntu vaḷarcci aṭaintatu.  mukkōṇa amaippai viṭa arai vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa amaippē vaḷarcci peṟṟup paṭukkaik kōṭṭu vaṭivamāṉa ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻāka atāvatu iṉṟaiya vīṇaiyiṉ uruvamāka vaḷarcci peṟṟatu.  kōṭu vaḷaintatu yāḻ. kōṭu nimirntatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉpatu cemmaiyāṉa - atāvatu nērāka nimirkkappeṟṟa kōṭṭaip peṟṟatu eṉpatē poruḷākum.
arai vaṭṭamāṉa āti vilyāḻ nīṇṭakālattiṟkup piṉ caṟṟu nimirntu, iru nuṉikaḷum aṭiyai viṭa virintu mūṉṟām piṟaic cantiraṉ vaṭivamāy 10 mutal 17 eṇkaḷ uḷḷa narampu kaḷaip peṟṟum vevvēṟu peyarkaḷait tāṅkiyum nam nāṭṭil nilavivantatu.  inta māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷa vil yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  amarāvati, cāñci ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ekiptu nāṭṭi luḷḷa paḻam perum paṭimaṅkaḷilum ciṟpaṅkaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷi lum peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cilpaciriyiṉ ciṟpa uruviṉ kaikaḷil iṭampeṟṟa yāḻ itē vaṭivilē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  āṉāl ataṉ kōṭukaḷil iru ciṟu kuṭaṅkaḷ miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.  ivai ciṟitu aḻaku peṟa amaikkappeṟṟut tamiḻ aṉṉaiyiṉ tirukkaraṅkaḷil tikaḻvatu pōl camīpattil oru ōviyam tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. araivaṭṭam ciṟitu kuṟaintum vāy akaṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ tīṭṭiya pēriyāḻ vaṭivilum amarāvati kōlic ciṟpaṅkaḷilum inta māṟṟaṅkaḷai nām ōraḷavu kāṇa muṭiyum.  inta vaḷarccitāṉ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻunta ciṟpaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatu.  itaṉai araivaṭṭa vaṭivayāḻ peṟṟa mūṉṟāvatu vaḷarcciyiṉ nilai eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām.
mēṟkūṟiya araivaṭṭa amaippuḷḷa āti yāḻkaḷai viṭa atika māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatu vipulāṉantar kāṭṭum cakōṭa yāḻē yākum.  cakōṭa yāḻai viṭat teḷivāṉa māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷa putiya yāḻ uruvam oṉṟu camīpattil namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷatu namatu tavappayaṉēyākum.  intap putiya māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷa ariya yāḻ karuvi iṉṟu parmāvil uḷḷatu.  itu intiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu parmāviṟku pautta neṟiyaip parappuvataṟkuc ceṉṟa pautta tuṟavikaḷāl koṇṭu cellappeṟṟatākak kūṟappeṟukiṟatu.  parmā nāṭṭil uḷḷa inta yāḻ uruvam iṉṟum mīṭṭappeṟṟu varukiṟatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  iṉṟu ivvulakil kāṇappaṭum orē intiya yāḻ uruvam parmāvil kāṇappaṭum ippaḻamperum yāḻ uruvamēyākum.  itaṉaip parmīya makkaḷ cavuṇ eṉṟu aḻaik kiṟārkaḷ.  itu ki. pi. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuḷḷāka uruvākkappaṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉ aṭip pakuti tōṇi pōl tōṉṟukiṟatu.  itu marattāl ceyyappeṟṟu mēlē poṟṟakaṭṭāl mūṭappeṟṟu vaira maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  kompu ciṟitu vaḷaintu ataṉ nuṉiyil vairam, marakatam, māṇikkam pōṉṟa kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  inta yāḻiṟku 14 narampukaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  itu ōr ācaṉattiṉ mītu vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itaṟku oru nallapaṭi eṭuttu ceṉṉait tolporuṭkāṭciyil vaikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  iṉṟu pala yāḻ ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl marattiṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa paḻaiya yāḻ uruvam oṉṟum namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai.  inta nūṟṟāṇṭil mutaṉmutalāka nām kāṇum oru acal yāḻ uruviṉpaṭi ituvēyākum.  inta yāḻ kōṭu ceṅkuttāy nimirntuḷḷa tirumayam ciṟpattilum kaltēyā vilum kāṇappaṭṭa yāḻkaḷaip pōl uḷnōkki vaḷaintuḷḷa tāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  ēṉaiya iraṇṭu yāḻkaḷum nērākac ceṅkuttāy niṟkum kompukaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉa (paṭam 1, 18, 19-aip pārkka).
piṟa yāḻkaḷai viṭa tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ kāṭṭum nāratappēri yāḻ (1000-puri narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu) teḷivāṉa mukkōṇa amaippaiyuṭaiyatākum.  (paṭam 20).  inta amaippil kaltēyā nāṭṭiṉ kallaṟaiyil kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa maṟṟoru kāḷaikkaṉṟu mukantāṅkiya maṟṟoru yāḻ uruvam kaṇṭu eṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu (paṭam 21). inta yāḻ ki. mu. 27, 28ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil eḻuntatāka aṟiñarkaḷāl eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉ aṭippakutiyil uḷḷa peṭṭi pōṉṟa pakutiyiṉ nuṉiyil tamiḻaka yāḻai aṉucarittu oru kāḷaikkaṉṟu talai vaṭivam amaikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  itil 11 mutal 15 varai nāṇkaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟu aḻakiya ariya vēlaippāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  itaṉ kīḻē kāṇappaṭum caturamāṉa peṭṭi pōṉṟa vaṭiva amaippil iruntu kōṭu caṟṟu vaḷaintu piṉ nērāka ceṅkuttāy nimirntu niṟkiṟatu.  inta amaippiṉait taḻuvi-atāvatu ṭakara amaippil (ṭaṉ pōṉṟu) ekiptilum, cumēriyā nāṭṭilum ōviyattilum ciṟpattilum pala yāḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  kaltēyā nāṭṭiṉ yāḻ uruvil tamiḻakat taccarkaḷiṉ kaivaṇṇam oḷirkiṟatu.  inta yāḻiṉ kompu amaippu tirumeyyam eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa ciṟpa uruviṉ amaippait taḻuviyatākavē amaintuḷḷatu.  aṭippakuti ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatu (paṭam 22-24 varaip pārkka).
iṟutiyāka, cīṟiyāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum yāḻē nalla māṟṟattaip peṟṟatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka yāḻkaḷ peṟṟirunta uruvaṅkaḷil oru perum māṟṟam eḻuntatu.  cīṟiyāḻiṉ vaḷaivuṟṟirunta kōṭu peritum nimirkkappeṟṟatu.  cīriya yāḻē ciṟitāka, kaikku aṭakkamāka eḷitil eṅkum eṭuttuc cellattakkatāy amaintatu.  itaiyoṭṭip pala vil yāḻkaḷ kūṭa māṟṟam peṟṟeḻuntaṉa.  inta yāḻ mīṭṭuvataṟku eḷitāṉa tāyum pāṇarkaḷ inta yāḻaip paṭṭu uṟaiyuḷ aṭaittu ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu maṟṟōriṭattiṟku eḷitil eṭuttuc celvataṟku ēṟṟatāyum iruntatu (paṭam 25, 26).
cīṟiyāḻiṉ kōṭu nērāka nimirntatāl ataṉ aḻaku uyarntatu; icaikkum vacati mikuntatu.   kamakam pōṉṟa kavarccikaramāṉa iṉṉolikaḷai eḻuppa muṭintatu.  itaṉ nalattai aṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ itaṉ kōṭu muḻuvatum nimirttappeṟṟāl, yāḻ nēriyatāyum cīriyatāyum ciṟantatāyum viḷaṅkum eṉṟu eṇṇi kōṭu nimirttiya yāḻai uruvākkiṉar.  inta yāḻ cemmaiyāṉa kōṭṭaip peṟṟatāl ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṟum poruḷ tarum perum pēṟṟaip peṟṟatu.  itu ceṅkōlpōl nērāka nimirttappeṟṟatāl ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṟu kūṟiṉālum poruntum.  (paṭam 27) ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ iṉṟaiya tampurā allatu vīṇai vaṭivattai aṭaintatu; nām yāḻkaḷiṉ uruvaṅ kaḷai varicaiyāka kāla amaippil varicai oḻuṅkupaṭutti vaittup pārttāl yāḻiṉ uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa pariṇāma vaḷarcciyilē iṟutiyākac ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ataṉ vaṭivil vīṇaiyum iṇaintu niṟpatu eḷital pulaṉākum.  ceṅkōṭi yāḻ uruvāku muṉṉarē tuntiṉā pōṉṟa oru narampuḷḷa icaikkaruvikaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.  itu yāṉ kaṇṭu kūṟum putiya muṭipaṉṟu.  eṉakku muṉṉar pala aṟiñarkaḷ inta muṭipiṟku vantuḷḷārkaḷ.  āṉāl avarkaḷ yāḻkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷai oḻuṅkupaṭutti vaittu avaikaḷiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyai yellām teḷivāka viḷakki eṭuttukāṭṭi inta muṭipaip kūṟavillai eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.  ghisa§ nfh£il, jÄœ¥ bgU«òyt® Mãufh« mUs¥g‹ ã.V., avarkaḷ eṉ vīṭṭil uḷḷa cīṟiyāḻaip pārttu, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukku viḷakkam tarumpoḻutu, mēṟkaṇṭa karuttukkaḷai ātarittup pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ.
cakōṭa yāḻkūṭa, kōṭu nimirttappeṟṟāl iṉṟaiya vīṇaiyiṉ vaṭivattaip peṟum eṉṟu iṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷa pala ciṟpac cāṉṟukaḷ uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻtāṉ yāḻ vaḷarcciyiṉ iṟutikkaṭṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu eṉalām.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉpatu yāḻ amaippil oru ciṟiya māṟṟattaittāṉ-atāvatu kōṭu nimirttap peṟṟa māṟṟattaittāṉ peṟṟatu eṉalām.  iḵtaṉṟi, atu vēṟu oru ciṟu māṟṟattaiyum ēṟṟattaiyum peṟavillai.  āṉāl eḻil ciṟappaip peṟṟatu.  aḵtōṭu kōṭṭiṉ nuṉiyil oru putiya uruvam iṭam peṟṟatu eṉalām.  atāvatu putitākap purāṇa kāla vilaṅku eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum yāḻiyiṉ vaṭivam peṟṟatu.  tamiḻar kaṇṭa narampukkaruviyākiya yāḻil nīṇṭa kālamāka evvitamāṉa paṟavai allatu vilaṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷum iṭam peṟātu iruntu vantatu.  appāl ataṉ toṉmai marapu nilai eṉpatu māṟi, makara mīṉ vaṭivam peṟṟatu. tamiḻarkaḷōṭu utiracampantamāṉa toṭar puṭaiyavarkaḷākak karutappeṟum kaltēyarkaḷiṉ yāḻkaḷiṉ tamiḻnāṭṭuc civaneṟiyāḷarkaḷukku viruppamāṉa kāḷaimukamum kaṉṟumukamum iṭam peṟṟaṉa.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ kōṭṭiṉ iṟutiyil yāḻimukam iṭam peṟṟatu.  piṉṉar eḻunta vīṇai kaḷil mayil mukamum kiḷi mukaṅkūṭa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  camīpattil yāḻi mukavaṭivamum mīṉ vāl uruvamum amaintuḷḷa vaḷainta kōṭukaḷ intiya yāḻ vaṭivam oṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷatu. itaṉaip pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ ekiptilum papilōṉilum kiṭaippatākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl paḻaiya yāḻ vaḷarcci peṟṟu putturuvam peṟṟu nīṇṭa kōṭukaḷait tāṅkumpoḻutu kōṭṭiṉ nuṉiyil yāḻiyiṉ uruvattaip peṟuvatu poruttamāṉatum ciṟappāṉatumākum.  iḵtaṉṟi yāḻ nimirnta kōṭṭiṉaip peṟumpoḻutu atil oru ciṟitum māṟṟam ceyya evarum tuṇiyavillai eṉpatu iṅkuk kuṟippiṭat takkatu.  uṟuppukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷilkūṭa evarum māṟṟañ ceyya tuṇiyavillai.  yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷākiya 1. kavaikkaṭai, 2. oṟṟu, 
3. unti, 4. nuṇtuḷaiyāṇi, 5. pattal, 6. pōrvai, 7. paṇpoḻi narampu, 8. vaṟuvāy, 9. yāppu, 10. kōṭu, 11. tantirikaram, 12. kōl, 
13. tivavu, 14. takaippu āṇi, 15. māṭakam, 16. vaṇar, 17. puri narampuk kōl mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ aṉaittum kōṭu nimirkkap peṟṟa ceṅkōṭṭu yāḻil appaṭiyēyuḷḷaṉa.  cila uṟuppukaḷ peyar māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.1
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉiṉṟē iṉṟaiya vīṇaiyum ataṉ pirivukaḷāka iṉṟu malintu kiṭakkum pala tantik karuvikaḷum piṟantaṉa eṉpatu eṉatu uṟutiyāṉa muṭipu.  yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷ palavum vīṇaiyil iruppatē yāḻiṉiṉṟu vīṇai piṟantatu eṉpatai uṟutippaṭuttum.  yāḻiṉ nērāṉa pariṇāma vaḷarcci yilē - uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa niyatiyil tamiḻakattil, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāl kāṇappeṟṟa uyariya icaikkaruvi vīṇai eṉpatil immiyaḷavum aiyamiṉṟu.  mēlum iṉṟum yāḻiṉ 18 uṟuppukaḷaiyum ovvoṉṟākac ceytu poruttiṉāl vīṇai uruppeṟṟuviṭum.
itaṉai emmatippiṟkuriya aṟiñar tiru. āpirakām paṇṭita riṉ arumai makaṉār tiru. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉku āyntu pāṇar kaivaḻi eṉṉum paṇ ēṭṭil pāṅkuṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭi, em karuttai araṇ ceytuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
ēḻu narampukaḷaiyum oṉpatu icaikaḷukkuriya tavavukaḷaip peṟṟa kōṭṭiṉaiyum, narampukaḷai valittal melittal ceyyum māṭakattiṉaiyum vaṇaraiyum āṇikaḷaiyum, piṟavaṟṟaiyum uṭaiyatāyirunta nam paṇṭaic ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻē taṟkālam nam kaiyakattil vīṇai eṉap peyar māṟṟam peṟṟu ulavukiṟatu eṉṟa muṭivāṉa uṇmaiyai nām ēṟṟukkoḷvatait tavirttu vēṟu vaḻiyillai eṉa nām aiyamaṟak kāṇkiṉṟōm eṉṟu kuṟip piṭṭuḷḷār.1
yāḻiṉ patiṉeṭṭu uṟuppukaḷil, taṇṭu, cuḷḷāṇi, pōrvai, vaṟuvāy, māṭakam, takaippu, unti, vaṇar, pattar eṉṟa oṉpatu uṟuppukaḷum taṇṭu, muḷḷāṇi, mūṭi, palakai, kaṇ māṭam, mēṭu, meṭṭu, vaḷaivu, pāḷai allatu kuṭam pōṉṟa 9 uṟuppukaḷum vēṟu tamiḻ peyarkaḷaip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  yāppu, kavaikkaṭai, oṟṟu, muṟukkāṇi, puri narampu allatu paṇmoḻi narampu, tantiri karam, pōṉṟaṉa ṭakāri nākapācam, vaḷaivurēkku piraṭai, curameṭṭu, tanti, kāṭic cakkai pōṉṟa vēṟṟumoḻip peyarai iṉṟu tāṅki irukkiṉṟaṉa.  tivavu, purinarampu, (kōlkaḷ) pōrvai, narampu eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē meḻuku, veṇkalam, marap palakai, eḵku eṉap poruḷkaḷil māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  taṉit tamiḻp peyarkaḷai ayalavarkaḷ māṟṟit tam moḻip peyarai vaḻaṅkiyatāl itu ayalavarkaḷiṉ icaikkaruviyāki viṭa muṭiyumā?  murukaṉai, cuppiramaṇiyaṉ eṉṟu vaṭamoḻip peya rāl aḻaittāl murukaṉ vaṭavarkaḷiṉ teyvam ākiviṭa muṭiyumā?  atu orupoḻutum ākamuṭiyātu.
paṇṭait tamiḻt teyvaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai vaṭa moḻi yil ākkiyavarkaḷ paṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum uruvum, piṟappiṭa mum kāṇamuṭiyātavāṟu piṟamoḻip peyarai vaḻaṅkiyatai āyntāl inta yāḻ uruvil uḷḷa uṟuppukaḷukkup peyar māṟṟam ceytatil viyappu oṉṟum illai.  āṉāl, yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷil oṉṟu iraṇṭait tavira maṟṟavaikaḷai māṟṟātu appaṭiyē vaittiruppatu kaṇṭu tamiḻaka icai vallōrkaḷ kaḻipēruvakai aṭaikiṉṟārkaḷ.  pattar, vaṟuvāy eṉum iru uṟuppukaḷ ciṟitu māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatāl yāḻkkaruvi maṟaintuviṭātu, aḻintu viṭātu, vīṇai yāḻukkut toṭar pillāta ayallavarkaḷiṉ icaikkaruviyāki viṭamuṭiyātu.
yāḻ muṟkālattil vīṇai eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟiruntatu. yāḻkkulam, vīṇaiyaip peṟṟapiṉ maṟaintu viṭṭatu.  ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku piṉṉarum ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku muṉṉarum eḻunta iṭaikkālattil, yāḻiṉ kōṭu nimirntu vēṟṟuruvam peṟṟa piṉṉarum cila kālam ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṟum pañcaki yāḻ eṉṟum taṉ paḻampeyarai vaittuk koṇṭiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  atu māṟṟam peṟṟu nīṇṭa kālam ceṉṟa piṉṉarē vīṇai eṉṟa taṉippeyarait tāṅkiyatu.  atu yāḻ eṉṟa peyarait tuṟantu vīṇai eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟu oṉṟiraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil yāḻ eṉṟa tāykkaruviyai amiḻtuṟaḻ teyvat tīñcuvai nalkiya tirukkaruviyai aḻittuviṭṭatu.  atu, naṇṭu  ippi vēykatali nācamuṟuṅkālam, koṇṭa karuvaḻikkum eṉṟa āṉṟōr mutumoḻikkēṟpa vīṇai taṉ tāykkulattai (yāḻai) aḻitta tōṭu ataṉ peyaraiyum tuṭaittuviṭṭatu.
appar kālattil yāḻ eṉṟa icaikkaruvikaḷ iruntu vantaṉa.  avar kālattiṟku muṉṉarē yāḻukku vīṇai eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkappeṟṟu vantatu.  avar kālattilē yāḻiṉiṉṟu vīṇai eṉṟa icaikkaruvi pirintatu.  muṟkālattil kōyilkaḷil yāḻtāṉ iṭam peṟṟiruntatu.  appāl yāḻum vīṇaiyum iṭam peṟ ṟiruntaṉa.  iṟutiyāka yāḻ akaṟṟappaṭṭu vīṇaiyē anta iṭattaic contappaṭuttikkoṇṭatu.  yāḻmurinātar kaiyil irunta yāḻum, vīṇātara taṭcaṇāmūrtti kaiyil irunta yāḻum akaṟṟappeṟṟu nīṇṭa kompuḷḷa yāḻ eṉṟa vīṇai (taṟkāla vīṇai) iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.  vīṇai eṉpatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ vaḻivanta oru narampukkaruvi eṉṉum namatu karuttaip pala aṟiñarkaḷ ēṟṟukkoṇṭu irukkiṟārkaḷ.  aṇṇāmalaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻ āyvuttuṟai virivuraiyāḷar vittuvāṉ tiru. ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉ avarkaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  atu aṭiyil vaṟumāṟu:1
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉai aṭiyoṟṟi amaintatu vīṇai eṉṉum icaikkaruviyākum.  yāḻil narampu kaṭṭappeṟuvataṟku iṭamāyuḷḷa kōṭu eṉṉum uṟuppiṉ vaḷaiviṉai māṟṟi ataṉkaṇ oru narampilēyē ēḻicaikaḷaiyum toṭuttu vācittaṟ kēṟṟa vakaiyil narampukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyaik kuṟaittu amaikkap peṟṟa narampuk karuviyē vīṇai eṉpatākum.  ovvoru curattiṟkum taṉittaṉi narampu kaṭṭappeṟṟatu paṇṭaiya tamiḻar payiṉṟa yāḻākum.  oru narampilēyē pala curaṅkaḷaiyum vācittaṟkēṟṟa tāṉa nilaikaḷaik  koṇṭatu iṉṟaiya vīṇaik karuviyākum, iṉṟaiya vīṇai narampiṟkup patilāka melliya eḵkuk kampi kaḷaip peṟṟuḷḷatu.  paṇṭaiyat tamiḻar vācitta yāḻākiya icaik karuviyum ataṉ aṭiyoṟṟi amaikkappeṟṟa, vaṭa nāṭṭiṟ peruka vaḻaṅkiya vīṇaiyeṉum icaikkaruviyum tamiḻmakkaḷāl tiṟampeṟa vācikkappeṟṟaṉa.
yāḻ uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa iyaṟkai niyatiyiṉ paṭi, vīṇai eṉum icaikkaruviyāy malarntatu eṉpatait tamiḻ icaikkup perumpaṇiyāṟṟiya takaicāl  periyār tiru. mu. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉpatu civanta marattāṟ ceyyappeṟṟu ēḻu tantikaḷuṭaṉ atāvatu - icai narampukaḷ nālum pakka  narampukaḷ mūṉṟum uṭaiyatāy taṟkālattil nām vaḻaṅki varum vīṇai eṉpatākat terikiṟatu.  yāḻi eṉṟa oru pūrvakāla mirukattiṉ talaiyaip pōl mēruviṉ pakkam ceyyappaṭṭiruntatiṉāl yāḻ eṉṟa peyar vantirukkalām eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.  vaṭa nāṭṭil yāḻ vakaikaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṟatai atikamākak kāṇamāṭṭōm. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷilum maicūr irācciyattilum āyiram āyiramāka vaḻaṅkivarum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṟa ciṟanta vāttiyam taṟkālattil vīṇai eṉṟa peyarāl aḻaikkappaṭu kiṟatu.  āṉāl avaikaḷai vaṭanāṭṭil kāṇpatu aritu.2
9ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē vīṇai tōṉṟi tirukkōyilkaḷi lellām ciṟappāṉa iṭattaip peṟṟukkoṇṭatu.  kōyil kaḷil yāḻum vīṇaiyum mīṭṭappeṟṟu varalāyiṉa.  eṉṟālum vīṇai eḻuntatum yāḻkkulam maṟaintu viṭavillai: yāḻ, palvēṟu peyar peṟṟum, vaṭivam peṟṟum eḻuntu vantaṉa.  ki. pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntavarākak karutappeṟum kallāṭar kālattil nāratap pēriyāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, maruttuva yāḻ pōṉṟavai oṉṟu mutal āyiram varai nāṇkaḷaip peṟṟu nāṭu muḻuvatum paravip pērum perumaiyum peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷākiya civaperumāṉum tirumālum yāḻai virumpiṉar.  civaperumāṉ yāḻai mīṭṭiṉār.  appar,
naṅkaiyaip pākam vaittār ñāṉattai navila vaittār
aṅkaiyil aṉalum vaittār āṉaiyiṉ urivai 					vaittār
taṅkaiyil yāḻum vaittār tāmarai malarum vaittār
kaṅkaiyaic caṭaiyil vaittār kaḻippālaic cērppaṉārē
eṉap pāṭi iruppatai eṇṇiyum, iṟaivaṉ yāḻmuri nātar eṉṟum, iṟaivi yāḻaip paḻitta moḻi, yāḻiṉum meṉmoḻi eṉṟa peyarkaḷait tāṅki iruppatai eṇṇiyum, yāḻtāṉ tamiḻar kaṇṭa icaikkaruvi civaperumāṉukkukantatu eṉṟum nampalām.  vīṇai putiya icaikkaruvi tamiḻar kaṇṭa karuviyaṉṟu; vaṭanāṭṭu icaikkaruvi eṉṟu cilar kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.  atu tavaṟu.
vēyuṟu tōḷipaṅkaṉ viṭamuṇṭa kaṇṭaṉ
mika nalla vīṇaitaṭavi
eṉṟa campantapperumāṉ pāṭalukku, vīṇaitaṭavi eṉṟa patat tiṟku yāḻai icaittu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu civaperumāṉuk kukantatu yāḻtāṉ vīṇai aṉṟu eṉac cilar vitaṇṭāvātam ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar.
emmiṟai vīṇai vācikkumē
eṉṟa pāṭalukkum yāḻukku vīṇai eṉṟoru peyar iruntatu eṉṟu mīṇṭum poruḷ tarukiṉṟaṉar.  appar, campantar kālattiṟku muṉ yāḻ vīṇai eṉṟa peyarāl aḻaikkappaṭṭirukkalām.  āṉāl appar kālattilē yāḻum, vīṇaiyum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.  yāḻ eṉṟoru icaikkaruviyum vīṇai eṉṟoru icaikkaruvi yum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.   iṟaivaṉ vīṇātarataṭcaṇāmūrtti eṉum peyaruṭaṉ tam kaikaḷil kōṭu nīṇṭa iṉṟaiya vīṇaiyait tāṅki iruppataiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉālum cilar ēṟṟuk koḷvatillai.  itu avarkaḷiṉ muraṭṭup piṭivātamē yoḻiya vēṟillai aṟivaiyum uṇmaiyaiyum ēṟṟukkoḷḷātavar kaḷukku eṉṉatāṉ coṉṉālum payaṉ taruvatillai.
vīṇai, appar aṭikaḷ, aruḷ peṟuvataṟku muṉpē atāvatu ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē uruppeṟṟu viṭṭatu.  atōṭu yāḻaip pōṉṟu, teyvīka icaikkaruviyāy tēṉ piliṟṟum naṟkaruviyāy iṟaivaṉ kaikaḷil ēṟiviṭṭatu.  tamiḻakak kōyilkaḷilum pukuntu tēvarkaḷ cuvaittumakiḻum amutuṟaḻ tamiḻicaik karuviyāy ēṟṟamuṟṟuḷḷatu.  appar aṭikaḷ,
vētaṅkaḷ ōtiyōr vīṇaiyēnti viṭaiyoṉṟu
tāmēṟi vēta kītā
eṉṟum, maṇivācakap perumāṉ,
paṇṇārnta vīṇai payiṉṟa viṟalavaṉē
ēḻicai yāḻ, vīṇai muralak kaṇṭēṉ
paṇṇōṭi yāḻ vīṇai payiṉṟāy pōṟṟi
aṟaikalaṅ kuḻala montai vīṇaiyākum
eṉṟum tamiḻmaṟai eṉappaṭum tiruvācakattil tiruppaḷḷi eḻucci yil, tirukkōyilil yāḻ, vīṇai ākiya iru icaik karuvikaḷ iṭam peṟṟiruntataik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  poruḷait tirittuk kūṟumuṭiyā vakaiyil,
iṉṉicai vīṇaiyar, yāḻiṉar orupāl
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṉāl yāḻ vīṇaiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta oru icaikkaruvi eṉpatum yāḻum vīṇaiyum taṉittaṉi icaik karuvi eṉpatum naṉkaṟiyalām.  yāḻum vīṇaiyum muṟkālattil namatu tirukkōyilkaḷil icaikkap peṟṟaṉa. namatu teyvaṅkaḷ ivviru icaik karuvikaḷil eḻum icai yolikaḷaiyum virumpic cuvaittaṉa.  tam kaikaḷil ivai iraṇṭaiyum taṉit taṉiyākat tāṅki icaittaṉa eṉṟu namatu paḻamperum camaya ēṭukaḷ kuṟip piṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷ vīṇaiyai yāḻaip pōl uvantu kaikaḷil ēntik kaṉivuṭaṉ mīṭṭi vantaṉa eṉṟu nāyaṉ mārkaḷ navilkiṉṟaṉar.  vīṇai, teyva nalyāḻiṉ tiṟam mikka tiruk kuḻantaiyākat tōṉṟiyatāl teyvaṅkaḷ atai virumpi icaittu vantaṉa. vēṟu nāṭṭiliruntu vanta icaik karuviyāy iruntāl eḷitil namatu teyvaṅkaḷ atai ēṟṟirukka muṭiyātu.  nam nāṭṭu camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ piṟa nāṭṭu icaik karuviyai yāḻōṭu oppiṭṭu matittup pōṟṟi irukka māṭṭārkaḷ.
yāḻ, toṭaricaiyuṭaṉ palarum kūṭip pāṭum nilaiyil amainta otticaip paṇkaḷai vācippataṟkēṟṟa amaippiṉai yuṭaiyatu; narampukaḷ atikamākap pūṭṭap peṟṟatu.  āṉāl oru narampilēyē pala icaikaḷaiyum mīṭṭuvataṟku ēṟṟatāṉa nilai kaḷai yāḻ peṟṟirukkavillai.  vīṇai ataip peṟṟiruntatu.  atōṭu vīṇai narampukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyaik kuṟaivākap peṟṟiruntatu.
nāḷaṭaivil vīṇai cemmaippaṭutta peṟṟatu; atu nallamaippum eḻilum ēṟṟamum peṟṟatu; yāḻai viṭa eḷitāka icaikka vallatu.  vīṇaiyil narampukaḷukkup patilāka eḵkuk kampikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟaṉa.  nāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veppanilai yāḻiṉ narampukaḷaip pātittatupōla vīṇait tantikaḷaip pātippa tillai.  eḵkuk kampikaḷ avaciyattiṟkēṟpa muṟukkāṇikaḷ mūlam melittal valittal mutaliyavaikaḷaic ceyyakkūṭiyavai kaḷāyt tikaḻntaṉa.  itil eḷitil paṇkaḷaiyum kamakaṅkaḷaiyum vācikkamuṭiyum.
itaṉāl, vīṇai, yāḻaiviṭa makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattaik kavarntatu.  vīṇaiyai vaṭa nāṭṭārum piṟa nāṭṭārum mīṭṭak kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.  tamiḻakattil caivamum vaiṇavamum vīḻcci peṟṟaṉa.  tamiḻar āṭci māṟiyatu.  tamiḻ makkaḷ aṭimai vāḻvu naṭattiṉar.  vaṟumaiyum ciṟumaiyum avarkaḷai vāṭṭi vataittaṉa.  nōy pēy pōl piccup piṭuṅkit tiṉṟatu.  innilaiyil yāḻkaḷ tīṇṭuvāraṟṟuk kiṭantu maṟaintaṉa.  vīṇaiyai nāṭuvōr kuṟain taṉar.  ataṉāl vaṭa intiyāvil vīṇai celvākkup peṟṟatu.  makkaḷ ataip pōṟṟi mīṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
pauttarkaḷ vīṇaiyai virumpavillai.  yāḻaip peritum virumpiṉar.  pauttam vaḷarnta īḻakam, kāḻakam, kampōcakam, cāvakam, campā, cappāṉ, cīṉam, parmā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil ellām yāḻ iṭam peṟṟatu.  atu āṅkāṅkē camaya icaik karuviyākac ciṟappuṟṟatu.
innilaiyil 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭilum tamiḻakattil āriyar celvākku ōṅkiyatu.  vīṇai, āriyar icaikkaruvi eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟappaṭṭatu.  ataṉait tamiḻar allātār pōṟṟavum payilavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻaṟiñar kūṭa itai nampavum eḻutavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  caravati kaiyilum vīṇai ēṟṟappaṭṭatu.
yāḻ nūlai yātta aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ pōṉṟōrkaḷaik kūṭa, intak kūṭṭam mayakki viṭṭatu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka aṭikaḷār tam yāḻ nūlil pala viṭaṅkaḷil vīṇai āriyar icaikkaruvi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṟkuc cilariṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷaiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  intiyak kalai mutu nitiyam eṉṉum āṅkila nūliṉiṉṟu oru mēṟkōḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. atu, varumāṟu;
vētattiṟ kūṟap peṟṟatum, pañcacikaṉ eṉṉum icai vallōṉ ēḻu curaṅkaḷaiyum irupattoru mūrccaṉaikaḷaiyum oru narampilē icaittuk kāṭṭuvataṟku icaintatum, paratamuṉi kaiyāṇṭatum vīṇaiyē
putta camayamāṉatu evviṭattum paravat toṭaṅkiya kālattilē accamayat talaivarkaḷ vēta oḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum avai tamakkuriya kuru vakuppiṉaraiyum paḻaiya āriya moḻiyaiyum otukki viṭṭup potu makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyait taḻuvip putiya kuru vakuppaiyum oḻukka muṟaikaḷaiyum vakuttuk koṇṭārkaḷ.  atu pōlavē āriya vīṇaiyaiyum otukkiviṭṭup potu makkaḷ kaik karuviyākiya yāḻaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum tiruviḻāk koṇ ṭāṭṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkumpaṭi ceytārkaḷ.  āṉāl āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilē putta camayam nāṭṭilē oḷi kuṉṟiyatōṭu paḻaiya yāḻum maṟaintu pōyiṟṟu.1
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kavaṉak kuṟaivālum kāla veḷḷattālum tamiḻakattai viṭṭu yāḻ maṟaintu viṭṭatu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka oṉṟiraṇṭukūṭa eñci irukkavillai.  iṉṟu tamiḻakattil yāḻ uruvaik kaṇṭavarkaḷ illai eṉṟu kūṟum aḷaviṟku yāḻ maṭṭumaṉṟi atu irunta iṭam kūṭat teriyātu maṟaintuviṭṭatu.  vīṇai vaṭa nāṭṭu icaikkaruvi eṉṟa mayakkattilō allatu piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉālō tamiḻnāṭṭut tirukkōyilkaḷil mīṭṭap paṭṭu vanta vīṇaiyum niṟuttappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.  iṉṟu kōyil kaḷil vīṇai icaikkappaṭuvatu aritākiviṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
āyiṉum ikkālattilum vīṇaik karuvikaḷil tañcāvūr vīṇai eṉpatu ciṟappāṉatākum.  tamiḻakattiṉ ciṟappamainta tañcai mānakaril vīṇaikaḷ aḻakākac ceyyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  akkaruvikaḷaic ceyyum kai vaṇṇam vāynta toḻilāḷarkaḷ tañcai māvaṭṭattil iruntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  vaṭanāṭṭiṟkum piṟa nāṭṭiṟkum tañcaiyiliruntu vīṇaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu aṉuppappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
yāḻiṉ iṉimaiyum āṟṟalum
yāḻ, iṉimaiyāṉa icaiyai eḻuppum icaik karuvi maṭṭumaṉṟu koṭiya uḷḷam vāynta arakkarkaḷaiyum aṉpum aruḷum uṭaiyavarkaḷāy māṟṟum āṟṟalum peṟṟuḷḷatu.  aṉpē, aruḷē amaitiyē uruvāy amainta āṇṭavaṉ kōpaṅ koṇṭāl akkōpattai evvāṟṟāṉum taṇikkamuṭiyātu.  āṉāl aḷappariya āṟṟal vāynta araṉāriṉ kōpattaiyum taṇikka vallatu yāḻ eṉpataṟku namatu purāṇak kataikaḷil eṇṇaṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ uṇṭu.  avaikaḷil oṉṟu irāvaṇaṉ katai.
irāvaṇaṉ ilaṅkai vēntaṉ.  avaṉ arakkaṉ eṉṟum, koṭiyavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉatu pakaivarkaḷum vaṭa nāṭṭārum eḻutiya nūṟkaḷ kūṟum.  āṉāl tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ irāvaṇaṉ civapaktaṉ; icai vallunaṉ.  avaṉ cellum iṭaṅkaḷuk kellām oru pūcaip peṭṭiyum ataṉuḷ oru poṉṉāṟ ceyta civaliṅkattaiyum vaittuc ceṉṟāṉ.  nāṭōṟum civa pūcai ceytu vantāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  ivaṉ nīṟaṇintavaṉ; piṟa uyir kaḷukku ūṟu ceyyātavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
irāvaṇaṉ mēlatu niṟu, vaṇṇan taruvatu nīṟu
eṉṟu campantar tam tirunīṟṟuppatittil irāvaṇaṉaip pōṟṟu kiṉṟār.  irāvaṇaṉ taṉ celvap perukkai eṇṇātu tiru nīṟṟai avamatikkātu aṇintu civaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu, avaṉ aruḷai aṭaintatu eṇṇat takkatākum.
irāvaṇaṉ nākar kulattōṉum ciṟpavallunaṉumākiya mayaṉ makaḷ maṇṭōtari eṉṉum eḻilmikka civapaktaiyai maṇantu koṇṭavaṉ.  avaṉai maṇivācakar,
ērtarum ēḻulakētta evvuruvun taṉṉuruvāy
ārkalicūḻ teṉṉilaṅkai aḻakamar vaṇṭōtarikkum
pēraruḷ iṉpam aḷitta peruntuṟai mēyapirāṉ					(tiruvācakam: kuyil 18. 5-7)
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.  irāvaṇaṉ maṭṭumallāmal avaṉ maṉaivi kaṟpukkaraci maṇṭōtariyum civaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu avaṉatu aṉpaiyum aruḷaiyum peṟṟavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar.
irāvaṇaṉ kayilai malaiyaik kaṭantu ataṟkappāluḷḷa nāṭukaḷai yellām vella eṇṇit taṉ vimāṉattil ēṟic ceṉṟāṉ.  viṇmuṭṭa niṟkum kayilai malai vimāṉattait taṭai ceytatu.  eṉavē avaṉ vimāṉattiṉiṉṟu iṟaṅki, malaiyait takarttu vaḻiceytu atai kaṭakka muyaṉṟāṉ; muṭiyavillai; taṉatu talaivaṉākiya civaṉai niṉaintāṉ; taṉ yāḻai eṭuttu mīṭṭi ṉāṉ; iṉṉicaiyai icaittāṉ.  avaṉai niṉaintu aḻutu aruḷtara vēṇṭiṉāṉ; araṉār avaṉukkuk kāṭci aḷittu varampala aruḷiṉār.  avaṉ akamakiḻntu ilaṅkai mīṇṭāṉ.  itaṉāl, pukaḻēntip pulavar
neṟṟit taṉikkaṇ neruppaik kuḷirvikkum
koṟṟat taṉiyāḻ
eṉṟu pāṭiyatu mikap poruttamāṉatākum.  caivacamaya kuravar kaḷil oruvarāṉa appar aṭikaḷ,
pattilaṅku vāyālum pāṭalkēṭṭup parintavaṉukku
irāvaṇaṉeṉ ṟīnta nāmatattuvaṉai
eṉṟu pāṭi iruppatai ārāyntāl irāvaṇaṉukkum civa perumāṉukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa aṉpum toṭarpum naṉku teriyum.
nāṉmukam, tirumāl mutaliya tēvarkaḷ, paṉṉūṟāṇṭu tavam purintum civaperumāṉ tiruuruvattaiyō allatu tirumuṭiyaiyō allatu tiruvaṭiyaiyō kāṇātu tāḻvuṟṟu niṉṟaṉar.  arum perum muṉivar pallāṇṭu pala ariya tavam iyaṟṟiyum iṟaivaṉ tiruuruvaik kāṇamuṭiyātu pōyiṉar.  civapaktiyil ulakilē oppārum mikkārumaṟṟu viḷaṅkiya peṇṇiṉallāḷ peruṅ kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta uttami tavaniṟai tamiḻc celvi kāraikkāl ammaiyār iṟaivaṉaik kāṇa nāṭi nōṉpu nōṟṟu aṟam pala purintu tavam pala iyaṟṟik kāṭumalai aṉaittum tirintu, uṭalellām naintu nontu melintu uyiraṉaittum vāṭi, kayilamalaikkup pōy ataṉ uyarnta koṭumuṭiyil civaṉ iruppār eṉṟu nampi ataṉmītu ēṟa muṉaintār.  iṟaivaṉ vāḻum intap paṉimalaimītu kāl vaikkakkūṭātu eṉṟu eṇṇik kālāl naṭavātu talaiyāl naṭantu ceṉṟu tam uṭampil uḷḷa ūṉ ellām karaintu elumpellām tēyntu malaimītu ēṟic ceṉṟum iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷaik kāṇa muṭiyavillai.  ataṉāl avar uḷamnontu,
aṉṟut tiruuruvam kāṇātē āṭpaṭṭēṉ
iṉṟun tiruuruvaṅ kāṇkilēṉ - eṉṟuntāṉ
evvuruvō empirāṉ eṉpōrkaṭ keṉṉuraikkēṉ
evvuruvō niṉṉuruva mētu?
eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.  irāvaṇaṉ imayamalaiyiṉ kīḻ akappaṭṭu varunti iṟaivaṉai utavikku aḻaittu, yāḻ mīṭṭiṉāṉ.  avviṉṉoli kēṭṭu iṟaivaṉ maṉam iḷaki ōṭōṭiyum kayilai malaimītiṉiṉṟu kīḻē iṟaṅki vantu irāvaṇaṉ icaitta paṇṇoliyaic cevimaṭuttu iṉpuṟṟu makiḻntu avaṉukkut tiruuruvaik kāṭṭi avaṉ kēṭṭa varaṅkaḷum vāḷum aḷittār.
irāvaṇaṉ orumuṟai taḻiḻakattiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttuvara muṟpaṭṭāṉ.  valimai vāynta irāvaṇaṉait tamiḻnāṭṭut tārvēntarkaḷ pōriṭṭu veṟṟi peṟuvatu eḷitāṉatā?  itai aṟinta tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷ kalaṅkiṉar.  āṉāl irāvaṇiṉ uyariya paṇpaiyum avaṉatu aṟaneṟiyaiyum aṟinta akattiyar irāvaṇaṉai aḻaittu irāvaṇēcuvarā!  nī tamiḻakattai āḷavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟāyā?  ām eṉṟāl tamiḻakattōṭu pōriṭṭup pala makkaḷaik koṉṟu nāṭṭaiyum kāṭṭaiyum aḻittu pāḻ paṭṭuk kiṭakkum maṇṇai āḷavirumpukiṟāyā?  uṉatu valimai vāynta ampukaḷiṉ oṉṟaiyum ataṉ uṟaiyiṉiṉṟu eṭukkāmal oru uyiraiyum vataikkātu oru poruḷaiyum aḻikkātu uṉ kaiyiluḷḷa yāḻaikkoṇṭē tamiḻakattai veṉṟu atai 
āḷa virumpukiṟāyā?  eṉṟār.  irāvaṇaṉ makiḻntu yāḻāl tamiḻakattai veṉṟu āḷavirumpukiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ.  appaṭi yāṉāl, nī yāḻāl tamiḻaṉai veṉṟu viṭṭāl tamiḻakattai uṭaṉaṭiyākap peṟuvataṟkut taṭai illai eṉṟār.  ēḻulakilum iṇaiyaṟṟa icaivallōṉāy viḷaṅkiya irāvaṇap periyār (Ravana the Great) iṟumpūteyti inta icaippōṭṭikku iṇaṅki yāḻaik kaiyil eṭuttār.  uṭaṉē akattiyar akamakiḻntu nī icaip pōṭṭiyil eṉṉai veṉṟuviṭṭālē pōtum eṉṟār.  irāvaṇaṉ nallatu, ākaṭṭum eṉṟār.  paintamiḻ piṟanta pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa potikai malaimītu icaip pōṭṭi toṭaṅkiṟṟu.  kuṟumuṉivar tīntamiḻ payiṉṟa teyva nalyāḻait tam tirukkarattil ēnti atai mīṭṭiṉār.  appēriyāḻiṉiṉṟu paṇṇoli eḻuntatu.  arukē irunta kaṭumpāṟai kaṉivuṟṟu nīrp poruḷāki iḷaki ōṭiyatu.  irāvaṇaṉ kaṇṇāl kaṇṭu kaḷittāṉ.  appoḻutu akattiyar yāḻ oliyāl iḷakiyōṭum kaṟkuḻampil tam kaiyilirunta yāḻai vaittār.  pākāy ōṭiya pāṟai uṭaṉē kallāy, yāḻaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu iṟukiyatu.  akattiyar puṉmuṟuval koṇṭu irāvaṇēcuvarā, inta yāḻai eṭuttu mīṭṭu eṉṟār.  pāṟaiyai nekiḻac ceytu patintu kiṭakkum yāḻai eṭuttu mīṭṭa eṇṇiya irāvaṇaṉ muyaṟci oṉṟum palikkavillai.  nīṇṭanēram taṉatu āṟṟal aṉaittaiyum cela viṭṭup pārttum taṉ icaittiṟaṉaiyellām kāṭṭip pārttum, muṭiyavillai.  vēṟu yāḻai eṭuttu mīṭṭiyum pāṭiyum kal nekiḻavillai; yāḻaik kalliṉiṉṟu acaikkavē muṭiya villai.  irāvaṇaṉ taṉ tōlviyai eṇṇit talaikuṉintāṉ.  akattiyar vēṟu oru yāḻai eṭuttu mīṭṭiṉār.  anta pāṟai mīṇṭum nekiḻntu pākāy iḷaki nekiḻntu ōṭiyatu.  akattiyar atiṟkiṭanta tam yāḻait tam kaiyāl eṭuttār.  irāvaṇaṉ kaṇṭu iṟumpūteytiṉāṉ.  tamiḻakattiṉ icaittiṟaṉai uḷamāra, vāyārap pukaḻntāṉ.  atu mutal tamiḻakattaik kavaravēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttai maṟantāṉ.  taṉ vākkai iṟutivaraik kāppāṟṟi ṉāṉ.  taṉ kulateyvamākiya civaṉuṟaiyum tamiḻttirunāṭṭiṉ - teṉṉakattiṉ icaic ciṟappaik kaṇṭu kaḷittu akattiyariṭam viṭai peṟṟu, avaratu vāḻttaiyum tamiḻakattiṉ nēcattaiyum civaperumāṉiṉ aruḷaiyum aṉpaiyum peṟṟu īḻam mīṇṭāṉ.
itu oru paḻaiya purāṇakkatai.  itil kūṟappeṟṟa irāvaṇaṉ arakkarkulat talaivaṉ.  ilaṅkāpuriyiṉ iṇaiyilā vēntaṉ.  teṉṉavaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟṟavaṉ.  ivaṉ nākarkula naṅkai maṇṭōtari eṉṉum maṅkai nallāḷai maṇantavaṉ.  iruvarum civaperumāṉukku āṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ.  tirāviṭa kulattōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭavaṉ.  ivaṉ civavaḻipāṭṭaic ceytuvarupa vaṉ eṉpatai vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷkūṭa valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
ivaṉ iṟaivaṉ icaivaṭiviṉaṉ eṉṟu eṇṇiyavaṉ.  icaiyil vallōṉ; icaiyāl iṟaivaṉ aruḷaippeṟṟavaṉ.  icaiyiṉāl iṟaivaṉ tiruuruvaik kaṇṭāṉ; avaṉ tiru varuḷaip peṟṟavaṉ.  taṉatu koṭiyil yāḻaip poṟittavaṉ.  ivaṉukku aruḷkūrntu iṟaivaṉ irāvaṇaṉ eṉṟu peyar aḷittāṉ.  ataṉāl iṟaivaṉ irāvaṇa aṉukkiraka mūrtti eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarum peṟṟāṉ.  civaperumāṉuṭaiya 64 mūrttaṅkaḷil irāvaṇa aṉukkiraka mūrttamum oṉṟu.
icai, makkaḷ uḷḷattaip paṇpaṭuttukiṟatu.  atu arakkar uḷḷattaiyum iḷakac ceykiṟatu.  kallaiyum iḷakkum; uruk kattaiyum urukkum.  teyvaṅkaḷiṉ kōpattaiyum karaivikkum.  irāvaṇaṉ arakkaṉtāṉ; āṉāl icai, avaṉ uḷḷam aṉpum aruḷum niṟaiyumāṟu ceytatu.  irāvaṇaṉ iyaṟṟiya icai nūl irāvaṇīyam.  avaṉatu yāḻ irāvaṇātam eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kūṟuvar.1  avaṉ maruttuva nipuṇaṉ.  avaṉ iyaṟṟiya nūl iṉṟum ciṅkaḷa moḻiyil irukkiṟatu.

10.  yāḻum vīṇaiyum
tamiḻar kaṇṭa narampuk karuvikaḷ palappala.  avaikaḷil cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟavai yāḻum vīṇaiyumākum.  yāḻ ātiyil eḻunta teyvat taṉmai vāynta icaik karuviyākum.  vīṇai ataṟkup piṉṉar.  aṭuttapaṭiyākac ciṟappuṟṟu eḻunta icaik karuviyākum.  vīṇai yāḻ vakaikaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkiya ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ aṭiyoṟṟi amaikkappaṭṭa cīriya narampuk karuviyākum.
yāḻil narampu kaṭṭappeṟṟirukkum kōṭu, eṉṉum uṟuppai nimirttic cemmai peṟac ceytu, atiluḷḷa oru narampilēyē ēḻu icaikaḷaiyum toṭuttu mīṭṭum vakaiyil narampukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyaik kuṟaittu ēḻākac ceyyappeṟṟa narampuk karuviyē vīṇai.
vīṇai, cevikku iṉimaiyum uḷḷattiṟku uṇarcciyum uyirukku uvakaiyum ūṭṭavallatu.  itu yāḻ pōṉṟa icaik karuviyātaliṉ yāḻiṉ perumaiyaiyum pēṟṟaiyum peṟṟatu.  yāḻaip pōlavē teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tirukkaraṅkaḷilum iṭam peṟṟatu.
miṭaṟṟukkum vīṇai eṉṟa peyar uṇṭu.  paṇṇiṟ polinta pākkaḷai makkaḷ, teḷivāy aṟintu koḷḷum vaṇṇam kuyiluvōriṉ miṭaṟṟolikkuc caṟṟu iṇaiyākac ciṟu karuviyāy miḷirvatāl vīṇai viraivil ciṟappeytiyatu.  vīṇaiyilē ēḻu kēḷvikaḷukkum (captacuraṅkaḷukkum) uriya paṉṉiru pālaikaḷaip (12 icaittāṉaṅkaḷaip) piṟappikkaveṉa ovvoru pālaikkum ovvoru iṭamāka (tāṉamāka) paṉṉi raṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷ nilaipeṟa amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
oppaṟṟa mutuperum camayakuravarum toṇṭarumāṉa appar aṭikaḷ tam tēvārat tiruppatikattil iṟaivaṉuṭaiya tiruvaṭi niḻalai, vīṇaiyiṉ iṉṉolikku oppiṭṭuḷḷār.  atu varumāṟu:
mācil vīṇaiyum mālai matiyamum
vīcu teṉṟalum vīṅkiḷa vēṉilum
mūcu vaṇṭaṟaip poykaiyum pōṉṟatē
īca ṉentai iṇaiyaṭi nīḻalē
inta oru pāṭalē vīṇaiyiṉ ēṟṟattai eṭuttukkāṭṭu vataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ aṭiyoṟṟi eḻunta, vīṇai eṉum ariya icaikkaruvi caṅka kālattil illai.  caṅkat tokai nūṟkaḷil yāḻait tavira, vīṇai eṉṟa narampuk karuvi kūṟappaṭavē illai.  ki. pi. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉṉarē vīṇai tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ.
vīṇai, ki. pi. 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tañcai irakunāta nāyakka maṉṉariṉ kālattiltāṉ, iṉṟu peṟṟirukkum eḻil vaṭivaip peṟṟatu.  itaṉāl vīṇai tañcāvūr vīṇai eṉṉum tuñcāp perumaiyaip peṟṟatu.  mutaṉmutalākat tamiḻakattil yāḻ ārāycciyait tuvakki vaitta aṟiñar vipulāṉantar 9, tantikaḷai yuṭaiya tumpuru yāḻum orē tantiyaiyuṭaiya maruttuva yāḻum, vīṇaiyaip pōṉṟa karuvikaḷē eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
vīṇai piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya narampuk karuviyāy irun tālum tamiḻc cāṉṟōr vīṇaikku ēṟṟam aḷittu vantaṉar.  cēkkiḻār aṭikaḷ,
kōtaiyar kuḻalcūḻ vaṇṭiṉ kuḻāttoli yoru pāṟkōla
vētiyar vētavāymai mikumoli yorupāṉ mikka
vētamil vipañci vīṇai yāḻoli yorupā lēttu
nātamil kalaṅkal kīta nayappoli yorupālāka.					(periya purāṇam 1200)
eṉṟu periya purāṇattil tiruñāṉa campantap perumāṉ tiru maṇattil vipañci vīṇai mīṭṭappeṟṟa ciṟappai eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
vīṇai piṟanta katai
yāḻ piṟanta kataiyai ituvarai, nām naṉkaṟiyavillai.  āṉāl vīṇai piṟanta katai namakkut teriyum.
kayilāca malaiyil malaimakaḷākiya umātēvi ayarntu tūṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟāḷ.  avaḷ vaḷaikaḷ oḷirum tam kai kaḷait tam mārpiṉmītu vaittapaṭiyē tuyilkiṟāḷ.  avaḷ tūkkat til mūccuviṭum poḻutu oru iṉṉoli arumpiyatu.  avaḷatu parutta iru koṅkaikaḷiṉmītu vaittirunta avaḷatu kaikaḷil aṇintirunta vaḷaikaḷ mūccu viṭumpoḻutu vimmit tāmum iyakkattāl mella oli ceytaṉa.  ampikaiyiṉ aḻakoḷirum intak kōlamum avaḷatu mūkkiliruntu eḻum melliya oliyum civaperumāṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷiṟpaṭṭaṉa; kātukaḷil olittaṉa.  attirukkōlam oru nittiya icaic ciṉṉampōl iruntatu.  atiṉiṉṟu civaperumāṉ tēviyiṉ tirucciṉṉattai niṉaip pūṭṭum oṉṟai uruvākka niṉaittār.  avar ampikaiyiṉ ayarntu tuyilum ariya kāṭciyaik kaṇṭu kaḷikūrntu mōṉat tiyāṉak kōlam pūṇṭār.  appoḻutu avarkoṇṭa nilaiyai vaittu avarai, yōka taṭcaṇā mūrtti eṉṟaṉar.  intat tiyāṉa nilaiyilum iṟaiviyiṉ eḻiṟkōlam avaruṭaiya akattiṉiṉṟu akalātu kāṭci aḷittu vantatu.  ataṉ viḷaivāka ampikaiyiṉ aḻakiya peruntaṉaṅkaḷ  curaikkāykaḷākavum ataṉ mītu vait tirunta avaratu kai vīṇait taṇṭākavum eḻuntaṉa.  iṟaiva ṉuṭaiya intak kaṟpaṉaiyiṉ viḷaivāka uruttira vīṇai uruvā yiṟṟu.  ivveḻiṟ kōlattil eḻunta iṟaivaṉuṭaiya tōṟṟam vīṇātara taṭcaṇāmūrttam eṉṟu aḻaikkap peṟṟatu.
vīṇaiyiṉ nīṇṭa vaṭivam melliyalāḷākiya ampikai yiṉ tiruuruvattaik kuṟikkiṟatu.  avaḷatu tirukkaraṅkaḷaiyum atu niṉaippūṭṭi varukiṟatu.  vīṇaiyiṉ iru kuṭaṅkaḷum iru taṉaṅ kaḷaiyum, vīṇaiyiṉ iṉṉoli umātēviyiṉ melliya mūccai yum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivvāṟu iṟaiviyiṉ eḻil uruvattaik kāṭṭum tōṟṟamum oliyum uḷḷa vīṇai ilayam uṭaiyatu.  ilayam umaipaṅkaṉukku uriyatu.  ataṉāl vīṇai civam, cakti eṉṟa iruvarukkum uriya ciṉṉamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  nāṉku tanti kaḷaiyuṭaiyatum vīṇaiyil mukkiyamāṉatum ākiya vācikkum pakuti curutikkut teyvamākiya umā tēviyiṉ amcam.  arukiluḷḷa ēṉaiya mīṭṭalukkuriya mūṉṟu tantikaḷum tāḷam allatu ilayat tiṟku amaintavai.  antap pakuti ilayattiṉ āti teyvamākiya civapirāṉuṭaiya amcam.  inta icaik karuviyai iṟaivaṉ taṉṉuṭaiya tiyāṉattiṉāl uruvākkiṉāṉ.  piṟaku nāṉmukaṉukku ikkaruviyai aḷittāṉ.  piramatēvaṉ piṉ taṉ maṉaiviyākiya kalaimakaḷukku aḷittāṉ eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ1.
vīṇaikku ēṉaiya icaik karuvikaḷukkillāta ēṟṟamum tōṟṟamum cīrum ciṟappum uṇṭu.  atu eḻil mika vāyntu iṉitāyk kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.  ataṉ iṉṉoli uḷḷattiṟku uṇarcciyūṭṭi uyirukku uvakai aḷikkiṟatu.  perum muḻakka miṉṟi (valivu nilaiyilum, tāratāyiyilum, mantāra tāyiyilum, melivu nilaiyilum) iṉitāka icaikkum ēṟṟam vāyntuḷḷatu.  vīṇaiyai mīṭṭi atai niṟuttiviṭṭāl ataṉ iṉiya nātam uṭaṉē niṉṟu viṭuvatillai.  ataṉ iṉṉoli alaikaḷ nīṇṭa nēram olittu melliya alaikaḷaippōl aṭuttaṭuttup paravi niṟkum.  valatu kaiyāl mīṭṭiya oli iṭatu kaiyāl icaikkum pōtum iṭaiyaṟātu iṉpamūṭṭum.  ēṉaiya icaik karuvikaḷ mīṭṭi, tantiyiṉiṉṟu kaiyai allatu villai eṭuttatum oli ōyntu viṭum.  vayaliṉiṉiṉṟu villai eṭuttuviṭṭāl uṭaṉē oli niṉṟu viṭum.  vīṇai avvāṟaṉṟu.
vīṇaikku oli eḻuppum tantikaḷ ēḻu.  avaikaḷil nāṉku vīṇaiyiṉ mārpil uḷḷa meṭṭukaḷiṉ mītuḷḷaṉa.  vīṇait taṇṭiṉmītu porutti irukkum ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷē meṭṭu kaḷākum.  immeṭṭukaḷ vīṇaiyil 24 uḷḷaṉa.  mēlum 4 kampikaḷukkum 96 iṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  meṭṭukaḷ mītu cellum kampikaḷai iṭatu kaiviralkaḷāl aḻuttit tēyttu curaṅkaḷai mīṭṭuvar, vīṇaiyai icaippōr.  itil taṉiyāka uḷḷa 3 kampikaḷ tāḷak kampikaḷ eṉappaṭum.  ivaṟṟai valatukaic cuṇṭu viralāl, mēl nōkki mīṭṭuvar.  itiṉiṉṟu eḻum oliyaik kuṭam taṇṭu ākiya pākaṅkaḷ perukki aṉuppukiṉṟaṉa.1
vīṇai, iṉṟuḷḷa uyarnta, tantik karuvikaḷil oṉṟu.  itu mika nuṭpamāṉa kēḷviyai (curutiyai) aḷakkum oru ciṟanta karuviyāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu.  inta icaik karuviyiṉ cīraiyum ciṟappaiyuṅ kaṇṭu vaṭa intiyarkaḷ itai atikamākap payaṉ paṭutta muṉvantuḷḷaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷ vīṇaiyai maṭṭumaṉṟi yāḻ, kuḻal, muracu mutaliya icaik karuvikaḷaiyum tamiḻp paṇkaḷaiyum nīṇṭa kālamākak kaiviṭṭu viṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṭci maṟainta tōṭu avarkaḷiṉ icaip payiṟciyum, piṟa, kalaip payiṟcikaḷum maṟaintuviṭṭaṉa.  tamiḻar taṅkaḷiṉ pala icaikkaruvikaḷaiyum pala tamiḻp paṇkaḷaiyum nīṇṭa kālamāka maṟantuviṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻar kaḷiṉ icaikkaruvikaḷ palavaṟṟai, piṟa iṉattavarkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭaiya tāka eṇṇi vaḷarkka muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ icaik karuvikaḷākiya palavaṟṟai, piṟa iṉattavarkaḷākiya yāḻ, vīṇai, mattaḷam, timilai, kaṇappaṟai, kuḻal, kaimaṇi pōṉṟavaikaḷaip piṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ icaik karuvikaḷākakkūṭa eṇṇat talaip paṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.  cātāraṇap pāmarat tamiḻarkaḷil cilar intap pāpattaic ceytāl tamiḻaṉṉai maṉṉikkalām; āṉāl muṉṉēṟṟam aṭaintavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum makkaḷ ip pāvattaic ceytu vantatāl, tamiḻaṉṉai eppaṭi maṉṉippāḷ?  ipperum pāvattiṟku kaḻuvāy tēṭat tamiḻ icaik kalaiñarkaḷ iṉṟē muṉvaravēṇṭum.  ēṉ?  tamiḻaka aracē muṉ varavēṇṭum, eṉpatu ematu uḷamārnta ācai.
namatu yāḻiṉaip paṟṟi vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa icai vallunarkaḷ oru ciṟitum aṟiyārkaḷ.  āṉāl vīṇaiyaip paṟṟi naṉku ārāyntirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.  vīṇai, yāḻiṉiṉṟu, yāḻiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyāl eḻunta oru icaikkaruvi eṉpatu avarkaḷ cevikaḷil viḻuntatē illai.  āṉāl namatu icai nipuṇarkaḷ vīṇaiyaip paṟṟi aṟintiruppatai viṭa avarkaḷ atikam aṟintirukkiṟārkaḷ.  vīṇai mikat toṉmaiyāṉa icaikkaruvi.  narampuk karuvikaḷiṉ tāy vīṇai.  āriyarkaḷ kaṇṭa cīriya icaik karuvi.  ātikālattil iṟaivaṉ vētakāla muṉivarkaḷukku aḷit tāṉ.  vētaṅkaḷil itaṟku ātāram uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟi varukiṉ ṟaṉar.  ippoḻutu etaṟkum cāṉṟukaḷ kēṭkum kālamāka iruppa tāl irukku, yacur, cāmam, atarvaṇam mutaliya āriyarkaḷiṉ vētaṅkaḷil ātāraṅkaḷ tēṭiṉar; kiṭaikkavillai.  ōviyaṅkaḷ, ciṟpaṅkaḷ, cācaṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa ātāraṅkaḷum kiṭaikkavillai.  viññāṉa rītiyāka vīṇaiyiṉ tōṟṟattaiyum ataṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyaiyum avarkaḷāl kāṇavē muṭiyavillai.  āriyarkaḷ pārata pūmikku varumpoḻutu vīṇaiyaik koṇṭuvantārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟku immi aḷavum ātāram illai.  vīṇai tirāviṭarkaḷiṭamiruntu, allatu avarkaḷ vaḻivanta tamiḻar kaḷiṭamiruntu āriyarkaḷ peṟṟa icaikkaruvi eṉṟō allatu aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya arappā nākarikatti ṉiṉṟu pariṇamittatu eṉṟō kūṟa avarkaḷ nāṇukiṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl cila vaṭanāṭṭu icaivallunarkaḷ vīṇai, aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar eḻunta intiyap paṇpāṭṭiṉiṉṟu eḻuntatu eṉṟu kūṟa maṉamiṉṟi vīṇai eṉum cīriya icaikkaruvi piṉlāntu, cārcciyā kākacup pakutikaḷil tōṉṟiya icaikkaruvi eṉṟu tayakkamiṉṟi kūṟiyum eḻutiyum varukiṉṟaṉar.
nām, vīṇai, tirāviṭap perumakkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya ariya icaikkaruvi eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm.  itaṉai mēṉāṭṭu viññāṉa viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar eḻunta arappāp paṇpāṭṭiṉ ariya
